```rust
pub fn format_tst(at_ms: i64) -> String {
    chrono::DateTime::from_timestamp_millis(at_ms)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .format("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%.6f%z")
        .to_string()
}

pub fn parse_tst(tst: &str) -> Result<i64, RecordError> {
    chrono::DateTime::parse_from_str(tst, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%.f%z")
        .map(|t| t.timestamp_millis())
        .map_err(|_| RecordError::Timestamp(tst.to_string()))
}

impl RecordLine {
    pub fn new(at_ms: i64, topic: &str, payload: &[u8]) -> Self {
        Self {
            tst: format_tst(at_ms),
            topic: topic.to_string(),
            qos: 0,
            retain: 0,
            payloadlen: payload.len(),
            payload: String::from_utf8_lossy(payload).into_owned(),
        }
    }

    pub fn at_ms(&self) -> Result<i64, RecordError> {
        parse_tst(&self.tst)
    }
}

pub fn read_lines(reader: impl BufRead) -> impl Iterator<Item = Result<RecordLine, RecordError>> {
    reader.lines().enumerate().filter_map(|(i, line)| match line {
        Err(e) => Some(Err(RecordError::Io(e.to_string()))),
        Ok(text) if text.trim().is_empty() => None,
        Ok(text) => Some(
            serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(|e| RecordError::Json { line: i + 1, reason: e.to_string() }),
        ),
    })
}
```

```rust
//! DJI-Nachrichten (Doku v1.11.3, Referenz `docs/reference/dji-cloud-api-m350.md`).
//! osd/state werden NICHT als feste Structs gelesen: sie kommen als Teil-Updates und mit
//! wechselnden Zahltypen. Der Übersetzer führt sie als JSON zusammen und liest Felder tolerant
//! über [`number`]/[`int`]. Feste Structs nur für Nachrichten mit stabiler Form.

use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::{Map, Value};

/// Fluggerät M350 RTK (`type`), RC Plus (`type`); `sub_type` jeweils 0.
pub const AIRCRAFT_TYPE: u32 = 89;
pub const GATEWAY_TYPE: u32 = 119;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize)]
pub struct Envelope {
    pub tid: Option<String>,
    pub bid: Option<String>,
    pub timestamp: Option<i64>,
    pub method: Option<String>,
    pub gateway: Option<String>,
    pub need_reply: Option<u8>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub data: Value,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct UpdateTopo {
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub kind: Option<u32>,
    pub sub_type: Option<u32>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub sub_devices: Vec<SubDevice>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct SubDevice {
    pub sn: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub kind: Option<u32>,
    pub sub_type: Option<u32>,
    pub index: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq, Default)]
pub struct LiveCapacity {
    #[serde(default)]
    pub device_list: Vec<LiveDevice>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct LiveDevice {
    pub sn: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub camera_list: Vec<LiveCamera>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct LiveCamera {
    pub camera_index: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub video_list: Vec<LiveVideo>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct LiveVideo {
    pub video_index: String,
    pub video_type: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub switchable_video_types: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct LiveStatusEntry {
    pub video_id: String,
    pub video_type: Option<String>,
    pub video_quality: Option<u8>,
    pub status: Option<u8>,
    pub error_status: Option<i64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub struct ServiceReply {

    pub output: Option<Value>,
}

/// Dokumentierte Felder der Fluggeräte-osd/state (Referenz Kap. 3). Alles andere zählt als unbekannt.
pub const AIRCRAFT_DOCUMENTED: &[&str] = &[
    "latitude", "longitude", "height", "elevation", "home_distance", "horizontal_speed", "vertical_speed",
    "attitude_head", "attitude_pitch", "attitude_roll", "wind_speed", "wind_direction", "battery",
    "low_battery_warning_threshold", "serious_low_battery_warning_threshold", "mode_code", "mode_code_reason",
    "home_latitude", "home_longitude", "position_state", "gear", "control_source", "track_id", "height_limit",
    "is_near_height_limit", "is_near_area_limit", "obstacle_avoidance", "night_lights_state", "total_flight_time",
    "total_flight_distance", "total_flight_sorties", "storage", "firmware_version", "country", "cameras",
];

/// Dokumentierte Felder der RC-osd/state (Referenz Kap. 3.11).
pub const GATEWAY_DOCUMENTED: &[&str] = &[
    "latitude", "longitude", "height", "capacity_percent", "wireless_link", "live_capacity", "live_status",
    "dongle_infos", "firmware_version",
];

/// Nutzlast-Structs heißen `{type}-{subtype}-{gimbalindex}`, z. B. `61-0-0`.
pub fn is_payload_key(key: &str) -> bool {
    let parts: Vec<_> = key.split('-').collect();
    parts.len() == 3 && parts.iter().all(|p| !p.is_empty() && p.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()))
}

/// Zahlenfeld tolerant lesen. `Ok(None)`: fehlt oder `null`. `Err(())`: vorhanden, aber keine Zahl.

/// Ganzzahl im Bereich von `T`; Kommazahlen mit Nachkommaanteil 0 werden angenommen.

}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn obj(v: Value) -> Map<String, Value> {
        v.as_object().unwrap().clone()
    }

    #[test]
    fn numbers_accept_int_and_float_and_reject_text() {
        let o = obj(json!({"a": 1, "b": 2.5, "c": "x", "d": null}));
        assert_eq!(number(&o, "a"), Ok(Some(1.0)));
        assert_eq!(number(&o, "b"), Ok(Some(2.5)));
        assert_eq!(number(&o, "c"), Err(()));
        assert_eq!(number(&o, "d"), Ok(None));
        assert_eq!(number(&o, "fehlt"), Ok(None));
    }

    #[test]
    fn ints_reject_fractions_and_out_of_range() {
        let o = obj(json!({"p": 63.0, "q": 63.5, "r": 300}));
        assert_eq!(int::<u8>(&o, "p"), Ok(Some(63)));
        assert_eq!(int::<u8>(&o, "q"), Err(()));
        assert_eq!(int::<u8>(&o, "r"), Err(()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn decodes_update_topo_from_the_rc_plus_example() {
        let env: Envelope = serde_json::from_value(json!({
            "tid": "t", "bid": "b", "method": "update_topo", "timestamp": 1,
            "data": {"domain": "3", "type": 119, "sub_type": 0, "thing_version": "1.1.2",
                     "sub_devices": [{"sn": "AC1", "domain": "0", "type": 89, "sub_type": 0, "index": "A"}]}
        })).unwrap();
        let topo: UpdateTopo = serde_json::from_value(env.data).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(topo.kind, Some(GATEWAY_TYPE));
        assert_eq!(topo.sub_devices[0].sn, "AC1");
        assert_eq!(topo.sub_devices[0].kind, Some(AIRCRAFT_TYPE));
    }

    #[test]
    fn payload_keys_are_recognised() {
        assert!(is_payload_key("61-0-0"));
        assert!(is_payload_key("39-0-7"));
        assert!(!is_payload_key("battery"));
        assert!(!is_payload_key("61-0"));
    }
}
```

```rust
pub fn parse(topic: &str) -> DjiTopic<'_> {
    let parts: Vec<&str> = topic.split('/').collect();
    let [root, "product", sn, leaf] = parts.as_slice() else { return DjiTopic::Other };
    if !is_valid_sn(sn) {
        return DjiTopic::Other;
    }
    match (*root, *leaf) {
        ("thing", "osd") => DjiTopic::Osd { sn },
        ("thing", "state") => DjiTopic::State { sn },
        ("sys", "status") => DjiTopic::Status { gateway: sn },
        ("thing", "services_reply") => DjiTopic::ServicesReply { gateway: sn },
        ("thing", "events") => DjiTopic::Events { gateway: sn },
        ("thing", "requests") => DjiTopic::Requests { gateway: sn },
        _ => DjiTopic::Other,
    }
}
```

```rust
pub fn number(obj: &Map<String, Value>, key: &str) -> Result<Option<f64>, ()> {
    match obj.get(key) {
        None | Some(Value::Null) => Ok(None),
        Some(Value::Number(n)) => n.as_f64().filter(|f| f.is_finite()).map(Some).ok_or(()),
        Some(_) => Err(()),
    }
}

pub fn int<T: TryFrom<i64>>(obj: &Map<String, Value>, key: &str) -> Result<Option<T>, ()> {
    let Some(f) = number(obj, key)? else { return Ok(None) };
    if f.fract() != 0.0 {
        return Err(());
    }
    T::try_from(f as i64).map(Some).map_err(|_| ())
}
```

### Task B2: Synthetischer Flug als Fixture

**Strang:** backend

Es gibt noch keinen echten M0-Mitschnitt; die M0-Tasks 5 und 6 sind offen. Diese Fixture bildet die dokumentierte Form ab, mit erfundenen Seriennummern und Koordinaten. Ein echter Mitschnitt kommt nach dem Feldtest bereinigt dazu (siehe „Offene Punkte“).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sim/mod.rs`, `src/sim/synth.rs`, `tests/fixtures/dji/synthetic-flight.jsonl` (erzeugt, committet)
- Modify: `src/lib.rs` (`pub mod sim;`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `dji::record::RecordLine`, `contract::examples::{GATEWAY_SN, AIRCRAFT_SN, HOME}`
- Produces:
  - `sim::synth::Scenario { gateway_sn: String, aircraft_sn: String, home: GeoPoint, osd_interval_ms: i64, gap: Option<(i64, i64)> }` mit `Scenario::fixture()` (2-s-Takt, Lücke 15 s) und `Scenario::live(gateway_sn, aircraft_sn)` (1-s-Takt, ohne Lücke)
  - `sim::synth::generate(&Scenario, start_ms: i64) -> Vec<RecordLine>` (zeitlich sortiert)
  - `sim::synth::FIXTURE_PATH = "tests/fixtures/dji/synthetic-flight.jsonl"`, `FIXTURE_START_MS = 1_792_150_000_000`

**Ablauf des Szenarios** (Zeit ab Start):

| Zeit | Ereignis |
|---|---|
| 0 s | `update_topo` mit `sub_devices: []` (RC an, Fluggerät aus); RC-osd, danach alle 2 s |
| 4 s | `update_topo` mit M350; RC-state mit `live_capacity` (H20N `61-0-0`, FPV `39-0-7`) |
| 6–20 s | Fluggerät am Boden: `mode_code` 0, dann 1, dann 2; erste osd vollständig, danach Teil-Updates, Akku nur jede zweite Nachricht |
| 22 s | `mode_code` 4 (automatischer Start), Steigflug |
| 30–150 s | `mode_code` 3, Suchmuster (Rechteck mit 4 Schenkeln) in 60 m |
| 80–95 s | keine osd vom Fluggerät (Lücke, nur bei `fixture()`) |
| 150–170 s | `mode_code` 9 (RTH), Rückflug |
| 170–180 s | `mode_code` 10, Sinkflug |
| 182–190 s | `mode_code` 0 am Boden |
| 192 s | `update_topo` mit `sub_devices: []` |

Jede Fluggeräte-osd enthält außerdem das undokumentierte Feld `extra_probe` (Zahl), damit die Zählung „unbekannte Felder“ etwas sieht. Die state-Nachricht des Fluggeräts bei 6 s trägt `home_latitude` und `home_longitude`.

```rust
//! Synthetischer M350-Flug im DJI-Format (Doku v1.11.3). Grundlage für Replay-Tests und den
//! Simulator. Koordinaten und Seriennummern sind erfunden.

use serde_json::{Value, json};

use crate::contract::GeoPoint;
use crate::contract::examples::{AIRCRAFT_SN, GATEWAY_SN, HOME};
use crate::dji::record::RecordLine;

pub const FIXTURE_PATH: &str = "tests/fixtures/dji/synthetic-flight.jsonl";
pub const FIXTURE_START_MS: i64 = 1_792_150_000_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Scenario {
    pub gateway_sn: String,
    pub aircraft_sn: String,
    pub home: GeoPoint,
    pub osd_interval_ms: i64,
    /// Zeitfenster ohne Fluggeräte-osd, relativ zum Start.
    pub gap: Option<(i64, i64)>,
}

impl Scenario {
    pub fn fixture() -> Self {
        Self { gateway_sn: GATEWAY_SN.into(), aircraft_sn: AIRCRAFT_SN.into(), home: HOME, osd_interval_ms: 2_000, gap: Some((80_000, 95_000)) }
    }

    pub fn live(gateway_sn: &str, aircraft_sn: &str) -> Self {
        Self { gateway_sn: gateway_sn.into(), aircraft_sn: aircraft_sn.into(), home: HOME, osd_interval_ms: 1_000, gap: None }
    }
}

mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::dji::record::read_lines;

    fn render(lines: &[RecordLine]) -> String {
        lines.iter().map(|l| serde_json::to_string(l).unwrap() + "\n").collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn committed_fixture_matches_the_generator() {
        let fresh = render(&generate(&Scenario::fixture(), FIXTURE_START_MS));
        let path = std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(FIXTURE_PATH);
        if std::env::var_os("UPDATE_FIXTURES").is_some() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
            std::fs::write(&path, &fresh).unwrap();
        }
        let committed = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).expect("Fixture fehlt: UPDATE_FIXTURES=1 cargo test sim::synth");
        assert_eq!(committed, fresh, "Fixture veraltet: UPDATE_FIXTURES=1 cargo test sim::synth");
    }

    #[test]
    fn scenario_covers_topology_partial_updates_and_gap() {
        let lines = generate(&Scenario::fixture(), FIXTURE_START_MS);
        let topos: Vec<_> = lines.iter().filter(|l| l.topic.ends_with("/status")).collect();
        assert_eq!(topos.len(), 3);
        assert!(topos[0].payload.contains("\"sub_devices\":[]"));
        assert!(topos[1].payload.contains(AIRCRAFT_SN));

        let osd: Vec<_> = lines.iter().filter(|l| l.topic == format!("thing/product/{AIRCRAFT_SN}/osd")).collect();
        let with_battery = osd.iter().filter(|l| l.payload.contains("capacity_percent")).count();
        assert!(with_battery > 0 && with_battery < osd.len(), "Akku nur in einem Teil der Nachrichten");

        let times: Vec<i64> = osd.iter().map(|l| l.at_ms().unwrap() - FIXTURE_START_MS).collect();
        assert!(times.windows(2).any(|w| w[1] - w[0] >= 15_000), "Lücke von mindestens 15 s");
        assert!(lines.windows(2).all(|w| w[0].at_ms().unwrap() <= w[1].at_ms().unwrap()), "zeitlich sortiert");
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_line_is_valid_json_in_the_m0_format() {
        let text = render(&generate(&Scenario::fixture(), FIXTURE_START_MS));
        for line in read_lines(text.as_bytes()) {
            let line = line.unwrap();
            let _: Value = serde_json::from_str(&line.payload).unwrap();
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
struct Builder<'a> {
    s: &'a Scenario,
    start: i64,
    out: Vec<(i64, RecordLine)>,
    seq: u64,
}

impl Builder<'_> {
    fn push(&mut self, t: i64, topic: String, data: Value, method: Option<&str>) {
        self.seq += 1;
        let mut env = json!({
            "tid": format!("sim-t-{}", self.seq),
            "bid": format!("sim-b-{}", self.seq),
            "timestamp": self.start + t,
            "gateway": self.s.gateway_sn,
            "data": data,
        });
        if let Some(m) = method {
            env["method"] = json!(m);
            env.as_object_mut().unwrap().remove("gateway"); // update_topo trägt kein gateway (Referenz Kap. 1)
        }
        let line = RecordLine::new(self.start + t, &topic, env.to_string().as_bytes());
        self.out.push((t, line));
    }

    fn topo(&mut self, t: i64, with_aircraft: bool) {
        let subs = if with_aircraft {
            json!([{ "sn": self.s.aircraft_sn, "domain": "0", "type": 89, "sub_type": 0, "index": "A", "thing_version": "1.1.2" }])
        } else {
            json!([])
        };
        let topic = format!("sys/product/{}/status", self.s.gateway_sn);
        self.push(t, topic, json!({ "domain": "3", "type": 119, "sub_type": 0, "thing_version": "1.1.2", "sub_devices": subs }), Some("update_topo"));
    }
}

/// Wegpunkte des Flugs: (Zeit relativ, Nord-Offset m, Ost-Offset m, Höhe über Start m, mode_code).
fn flight_plan() -> Vec<(i64, f64, f64, f64, u8)> {
    vec![
        (6_000, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0),
        (12_000, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1),
        (18_000, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 2),
        (22_000, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 4),
        (30_000, 0.0, 0.0, 60.0, 3),
        (60_000, 300.0, 0.0, 60.0, 3),
        (90_000, 300.0, 250.0, 60.0, 3),
        (120_000, 0.0, 250.0, 60.0, 3),
        (150_000, 0.0, 120.0, 60.0, 9),
        (170_000, 0.0, 0.0, 30.0, 10),
        (180_000, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 10),
        (182_000, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0),
        (190_000, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0),
    ]
}

fn interpolate(plan: &[(i64, f64, f64, f64, u8)], t: i64) -> (f64, f64, f64, u8, f64) {
    let i = plan.iter().rposition(|p| p.0 <= t).unwrap_or(0);
    let a = plan[i];
    let b = plan.get(i + 1).copied().unwrap_or(a);
    let f = if b.0 > a.0 { (t - a.0) as f64 / (b.0 - a.0) as f64 } else { 0.0 };
    let (n, e, h) = (a.1 + (b.1 - a.1) * f, a.2 + (b.2 - a.2) * f, a.3 + (b.3 - a.3) * f);
    let heading = (b.2 - a.2).atan2(b.1 - a.1).to_degrees();
    (n, e, h, a.4, heading)
}

pub fn generate(scenario: &Scenario, start_ms: i64) -> Vec<RecordLine> {
    let mut b = Builder { s: scenario, start: start_ms, out: Vec::new(), seq: 0 };
    let home = scenario.home;
    let to_geo = |n: f64, e: f64| {
        let lat = home.lat + n / 111_320.0;
        let lon = home.lon + e / (111_320.0 * home.lat.to_radians().cos());
        (lat, lon)
    };

    b.topo(0, false);
    b.topo(4_000, true);
    b.topo(192_000, false);

    let rc_topic = format!("thing/product/{}/osd", scenario.gateway_sn);
    for t in (0..=192_000).step_by(2_000) {
        let (lat, lon) = to_geo(-45.0, -20.0);
        b.push(t, rc_topic.clone(), json!({
            "latitude": lat, "longitude": lon, "height": 110.2, "capacity_percent": 81,
            "wireless_link": { "sdr_quality": 4, "sdr_link_state": 1, "4g_link_state": 1, "4g_quality": 3, "4g_uav_quality": 3, "4g_gnd_quality": 3 }
        }), None);
    }
    b.push(4_000, format!("thing/product/{}/state", scenario.gateway_sn), json!({
        "live_capacity": { "available_video_number": 2, "coexist_video_number_max": 1, "device_list": [{
            "sn": scenario.aircraft_sn, "available_video_number": 2, "coexist_video_number_max": 1,
            "camera_list": [
                { "camera_index": "61-0-0", "available_video_number": 1, "coexist_video_number_max": 1,
                  "video_list": [{ "video_index": "normal-0", "video_type": "wide", "switchable_video_types": ["wide", "zoom", "thermal"] }] },
                { "camera_index": "39-0-7", "available_video_number": 1, "coexist_video_number_max": 1,
                  "video_list": [{ "video_index": "normal-0", "video_type": "normal", "switchable_video_types": [] }] }
            ]
        }]}
    }), None);
    b.push(6_000, format!("thing/product/{}/state", scenario.aircraft_sn), json!({
        "home_latitude": home.lat, "home_longitude": home.lon, "mode_code_reason": 0, "firmware_version": "10.01.00.09"
    }), None);

    let plan = flight_plan();
    let osd_topic = format!("thing/product/{}/osd", scenario.aircraft_sn);
    let mut n_msg = 0;
    let mut t = 6_000;
    while t <= 190_000 {
        let in_gap = scenario.gap.is_some_and(|(a, z)| t >= a && t < z);
        if !in_gap {
            let (n, e, h, mode, heading) = interpolate(&plan, t);
            let (lat, lon) = to_geo(n, e);
            let battery_pct = 98 - (t / 6_000) as i64;
            let mut data = json!({
                "latitude": lat, "longitude": lon, "elevation": h, "height": h + 109.4,
                "attitude_head": heading.round() as i64, "mode_code": mode,
                "horizontal_speed": if mode == 3 || mode == 9 { 10.0 } else { 0.0 },
                "vertical_speed": 0.0, "home_distance": (n * n + e * e).sqrt(),
                "extra_probe": n_msg,
            });
            if n_msg == 0 || n_msg % 2 == 0 {
                data["battery"] = json!({ "capacity_percent": battery_pct, "remain_flight_time": 1500 - t / 1000, "return_home_power": 20, "landing_power": 10,
                    "batteries": [{ "index": 0, "capacity_percent": battery_pct, "voltage": 46_500, "temperature": 31.5 },
                                  { "index": 1, "capacity_percent": battery_pct, "voltage": 46_480, "temperature": 31.2 }] });
                data["position_state"] = json!({ "is_fixed": 2, "quality": 5, "gps_number": 21, "rtk_number": 18 });
            }
            b.push(t, osd_topic.clone(), data, None);
            n_msg += 1;
        }
        t += scenario.osd_interval_ms;
    }

    b.out.sort_by_key(|(t, _)| *t);
    b.out.into_iter().map(|(_, l)| l).collect()
}
```

```rust
//! Was der Kern über die Welt erfährt – in eigenen Begriffen, ohne DJI-Typen.

use crate::contract::{BrokerStatus, GeoPoint, Lens, LinkQuality, Telemetry, VideoSource};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Observed {
    /// Empfangszeit in ms seit Epoch.
    pub at_ms: i64,
    pub observation: Observation,
}

/// Flugzustand nach der Einteilung des Adapters (Referenz Kap. 4): Boden, Luft, unklar.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ModeClass {
    Ground,
    Air,
    Unknown,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct LiveStreamInfo {
    pub video_id: String,
    pub lens: Option<Lens>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum StreamCommand {
    Start { video_id: String },
    Stop,
    ChangeLens { lens: Lens },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Observation {
    /// Topologie der Fernsteuerung; `aircraft_sn: None` heißt Fluggerät aus oder Funk weg.
    Topology { gateway_sn: String, aircraft_sn: Option<String> },
    GatewayTelemetry { gateway_sn: String, position: Option<GeoPoint>, battery_percent: Option<u8>, link: LinkQuality },
    /// `periodic`: aus dem periodischen Strom (osd) – nur solche Proben zählen für Phase und Spur.
    AircraftTelemetry {
        aircraft_sn: String,
        gateway_sn: String,
        telemetry: Telemetry,
        mode_class: ModeClass,
        periodic: bool,
    },
    VideoSources { gateway_sn: String, sources: Vec<VideoSource> },
    /// Aktueller Stream laut Gerät; `live: None`: kein Stream läuft. `error`: deutscher Satz, wenn
    /// das Gerät in `live_status.error_status` einen Fehler meldet.
    StreamStatus { gateway_sn: String, live: Option<LiveStreamInfo>, error: Option<String> },
    CommandIssued { gateway_sn: String, command: StreamCommand },
    CommandFinished { gateway_sn: String, command: StreamCommand, result: Result<(), String> },
    Broker(BrokerStatus),
    Diagnostics { unknown_fields: Vec<String>, invalid_messages: u32 },
    RawRecording(Option<String>),
    /// Namen der Fernsteuerungen aus der Verwaltung, vollständige Liste (SN, Name).
    Labels(Vec<(String, String)>),
}
```

```rust
//! Start- und Landeerkennung mit Entprellung (Referenz Kap. 4):
//! - Ein Wechsel gilt erst nach 2 aufeinanderfolgenden Proben im neuen Zustand.
//! - Start braucht zusätzlich Plausibilität (Höhe über Start > 1 m oder |Steigrate| > 0,5 m/s),
//!   sonst würden laufende Motoren am Boden schon als Flug zählen.
//! - Landung braucht keine Plausibilität: niedriges Schweben darf keinen Flug beenden, deshalb
//!   entscheidet in der Luft allein der Modus.
//! - `Unknown`-Proben (Update, nicht verbunden) ändern nichts und setzen nichts zurück.

use crate::contract::FlightPhase;

use super::observation::ModeClass;

const CONFIRM_SAMPLES: u8 = 2;
const TAKEOFF_MIN_ELEVATION_M: f64 = 1.0;
const TAKEOFF_MIN_VERTICAL_SPEED_MPS: f64 = 0.5;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct Sample {
    pub at_ms: i64,
    pub class: ModeClass,
    pub elevation_m: Option<f64>,
    pub vertical_speed_mps: Option<f64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Transition {
    TookOff { at_ms: i64 },
    Landed { at_ms: i64 },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PhaseTracker {
    stable: FlightPhase,
    candidate: Option<(FlightPhase, u8, i64)>,
}

impl PhaseTracker {
    pub fn new(initial: FlightPhase) -> Self {
        Self { stable: initial, candidate: None }
    }

    pub fn phase(&self) -> FlightPhase {
        self.stable
    }

    pub fn observe(&mut self, s: &Sample) -> Option<Transition> {
        todo!()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn s(at_ms: i64, class: ModeClass, elevation: f64) -> Sample {
        Sample { at_ms, class, elevation_m: Some(elevation), vertical_speed_mps: Some(0.0) }
    }

    #[test]
    fn takeoff_needs_two_plausible_air_samples_and_dates_back_to_the_first() {
        let mut t = PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Ground);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(0, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(2_000, ModeClass::Air, 3.0)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(4_000, ModeClass::Air, 8.0)), Some(Transition::TookOff { at_ms: 2_000 }));
        assert_eq!(t.phase(), FlightPhase::Airborne);
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_air_outlier_is_ignored() {
        let mut t = PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Ground);
        t.observe(&s(0, ModeClass::Air, 3.0));
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(2_000, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(4_000, ModeClass::Air, 3.0)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.phase(), FlightPhase::Ground);
    }

    #[test]
    fn spinning_motors_on_the_ground_are_no_takeoff() {
        let mut t = PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Ground);
        for i in 0..5 {
            assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(i * 2_000, ModeClass::Air, 0.1)), None);
        }
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(10_000, ModeClass::Air, 1.5)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(12_000, ModeClass::Air, 2.5)), Some(Transition::TookOff { at_ms: 10_000 }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn low_hover_does_not_land_but_two_ground_samples_do() {
        let mut t = PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Airborne);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(0, ModeClass::Air, 0.4)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(2_000, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(4_000, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0)), Some(Transition::Landed { at_ms: 2_000 }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_samples_neither_change_nor_reset() {
        let mut t = PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Airborne);
        t.observe(&s(0, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0));
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(2_000, ModeClass::Unknown, 0.0)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&s(4_000, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0)), Some(Transition::Landed { at_ms: 0 }));
    }

    #[test]
    fn vertical_speed_alone_makes_a_takeoff_plausible() {
        let mut t = PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Ground);
        let climb = |at| Sample { at_ms: at, class: ModeClass::Air, elevation_m: None, vertical_speed_mps: Some(2.0) };
        t.observe(&climb(0));
        assert_eq!(t.observe(&climb(2_000)), Some(Transition::TookOff { at_ms: 0 }));
    }
}
```

```rust
    pub fn observe(&mut self, s: &Sample) -> Option<Transition> {
        let target = match (self.stable, s.class) {
            (_, ModeClass::Unknown) => return None,
            (FlightPhase::Airborne, ModeClass::Ground) => FlightPhase::Ground,
            (FlightPhase::Airborne, ModeClass::Air) => {
                self.candidate = None;
                return None;
            }
            (_, ModeClass::Air) if plausible_takeoff(s) => FlightPhase::Airborne,
            _ => {
                self.candidate = None;
                return None;
            }
        };
        let (count, first_at) = match self.candidate {
            Some((phase, count, first_at)) if phase == target => (count + 1, first_at),
            _ => (1, s.at_ms),
        };
        if count < CONFIRM_SAMPLES {
            self.candidate = Some((target, count, first_at));
            return None;
        }
        self.candidate = None;
        self.stable = target;
        Some(match target {
            FlightPhase::Airborne => Transition::TookOff { at_ms: first_at },
            _ => Transition::Landed { at_ms: first_at },
        })
    }
}

fn plausible_takeoff(s: &Sample) -> bool {
    s.elevation_m.is_some_and(|e| e > TAKEOFF_MIN_ELEVATION_M)
        || s.vertical_speed_mps.is_some_and(|v| v.abs() > TAKEOFF_MIN_VERTICAL_SPEED_MPS)
}
```

```rust
//! Flugspur in Segmenten. Eine Lücke über `SEGMENT_GAP_MS` beginnt ein neues Segment (Spec §5);
//! die Strecke über eine Lücke hinweg zählt nicht zur Flugstrecke.

use crate::contract::{SEGMENT_GAP_MS, TrackPoint};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Appended {
    pub segment: u32,
    pub point: TrackPoint,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct TrackBuilder {
    segments: Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>>,
    distance_m: f64,
    max_elevation_m: Option<f64>,
}

pub fn haversine_m(a: &TrackPoint, b: &TrackPoint) -> f64 {
    const R: f64 = 6_371_008.8;
    let (la1, la2) = (a.lat.to_radians(), b.lat.to_radians());
    let dla = la2 - la1;
    let dlo = (b.lon - a.lon).to_radians();
    let h = (dla / 2.0).sin().powi(2) + la1.cos() * la2.cos() * (dlo / 2.0).sin().powi(2);
    2.0 * R * h.sqrt().asin()
}

impl TrackBuilder {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn from_segments(segments: Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>>) -> Self {
        todo!()
    }

    /// `None`, wenn der Punkt nicht neuer ist als der letzte (Duplikat oder Rückläufer).
    pub fn push(&mut self, point: TrackPoint) -> Option<Appended> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub fn segments(&self) -> &[Vec<TrackPoint>] {
        &self.segments
    }

    pub fn distance_m(&self) -> f64 {
        self.distance_m
    }

    pub fn max_elevation_m(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        self.max_elevation_m
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn p(t_ms: i64, lat: f64, elevation: f64) -> TrackPoint {
        TrackPoint { t_ms, lat, lon: 10.0, elevation_m: Some(elevation) }
    }

    #[test]
    fn gap_starts_new_segment_and_is_not_counted_as_distance() {
        let mut t = TrackBuilder::new();
        assert_eq!(t.push(p(0, 52.0, 5.0)).unwrap().segment, 0);
        assert_eq!(t.push(p(2_000, 52.001, 30.0)).unwrap().segment, 0);
        let after_gap = t.push(p(2_000 + SEGMENT_GAP_MS + 1, 52.010, 60.0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(after_gap.segment, 1);
        assert_eq!(t.segments().len(), 2);
        assert!((t.distance_m() - 111.2).abs() < 1.0, "nur die 0,001° im ersten Segment: {}", t.distance_m());
        assert_eq!(t.max_elevation_m(), Some(60.0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn exactly_the_gap_threshold_stays_in_the_segment() {
        let mut t = TrackBuilder::new();
        t.push(p(0, 52.0, 0.0));
        assert_eq!(t.push(p(SEGMENT_GAP_MS, 52.0, 0.0)).unwrap().segment, 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn duplicates_and_out_of_order_points_are_dropped() {
        let mut t = TrackBuilder::new();
        t.push(p(2_000, 52.0, 0.0));
        assert_eq!(t.push(p(2_000, 52.0, 0.0)), None);
        assert_eq!(t.push(p(1_000, 52.0, 0.0)), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn resumed_builder_recomputes_stats_and_continues_last_segment() {
        let mut t = TrackBuilder::from_segments(vec![vec![p(0, 52.0, 10.0), p(2_000, 52.001, 20.0)]]);
        assert!((t.distance_m() - 111.2).abs() < 1.0);
        assert_eq!(t.push(p(4_000, 52.002, 25.0)).unwrap().segment, 0);
    }
}
```

```rust
    pub fn from_segments(segments: Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>>) -> Self {
        let mut t = Self::new();
        for (i, seg) in segments.into_iter().enumerate() {
            for point in seg {
                if i >= t.segments.len() {
                    t.segments.push(Vec::new());
                } else {
                    t.add_distance(&point);
                }
                t.note_elevation(&point);
                t.segments[i].push(point);
            }
        }
        t
    }

    pub fn push(&mut self, point: TrackPoint) -> Option<Appended> {
        let last_t = self.segments.last().and_then(|s| s.last()).map(|p| p.t_ms);
        match last_t {
            Some(last) if point.t_ms <= last => return None,
            Some(last) if point.t_ms - last > SEGMENT_GAP_MS => self.segments.push(Vec::new()),
            Some(_) => self.add_distance(&point),
            None => self.segments.push(Vec::new()),
        }
        self.note_elevation(&point);
        let segment = (self.segments.len() - 1) as u32;
        self.segments.last_mut().unwrap().push(point.clone());
        Some(Appended { segment, point })
    }

    fn add_distance(&mut self, point: &TrackPoint) {
        if let Some(prev) = self.segments.last().and_then(|s| s.last()) {
            self.distance_m += haversine_m(prev, point);
        }
    }

    fn note_elevation(&mut self, point: &TrackPoint) {
        if let Some(e) = point.elevation_m {
            self.max_elevation_m = Some(self.max_elevation_m.map_or(e, |m| m.max(e)));
        }
    }
```

In `from_segments` wird `add_distance` vor dem Einfügen in dasselbe Segment aufgerufen. Damit zählt nur der Abstand innerhalb eines Segments, über Segmentgrenzen hinweg nicht.

### Task B4: Übersetzer DJI → Observation

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/dji/translate.rs`
- Modify: `src/dji/mod.rs` (`pub mod translate;`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `dji::{topic, wire, record}` (B1), Fixture (B2), `situation::observation::*` (B3)
- Produces:
  - `dji::translate::Translator::new()`
  - `Translator::handle(&mut self, at_ms: i64, topic: &str, payload: &[u8]) -> Translated`
  - `Translated { observations: Vec<Observed>, reply: Option<Outgoing>, command_reply: Option<CommandReply> }`
  - `Outgoing { topic: String, payload: Vec<u8> }`
  - `CommandReply { gateway_sn: String, tid: String, method: String, result: i64 }`
  - `Translator::persisted(&self, sn) -> Option<(String, Map<String, Value>)>`: Gateway-SN und gemergter Stand eines Geräts, zum Speichern nach einer state-Nachricht (B12)
  - `Translator::restore(&mut self, at_ms, sn, gateway_sn, data: Map<String, Value>) -> Translated`: spielt einen gespeicherten Stand ein, als käme er als state-Nachricht; ohne `Diagnostics`
  - `dji::translate::{mode_label, mode_class, camera_label, live_error_text}`; `live_error_text(code)` ist der deutsche Satz zu einem 513xxx-Code (auch für `dji::control`, B13)

**Regeln:**
- osd und state jedes Geräts werden tief in den letzten Stand gemergt. Objekte werden zusammengeführt, Arrays und Werte ersetzt.
- Ein Gerät ist die Fernsteuerung, wenn die Topic-SN gleich `gateway` der Hülle ist oder in einer Topologie als Gateway auftauchte. Sonst ist es ein Fluggerät; sein Gateway steht in der Hülle (`gateway`), ersatzweise in der Topologie.
- Fluggeräte-osd ergibt `AircraftTelemetry { periodic: true }`, Fluggeräte-state ergibt `periodic: false`.
- Fernsteuerungs-osd und -state ergeben `GatewayTelemetry`. Enthält die eingehende Nachricht `live_capacity`, kommt zusätzlich `VideoSources`, enthält sie `live_status` (osd **oder** state), kommt `StreamStatus`. Meldet ein Eintrag `error_status ≠ 0` und läuft kein Stream, trägt `StreamStatus.error` den Satz aus `live_error_text`.
- Je Nachricht kommt `Diagnostics` mit undokumentierten Top-Level-Feldern der *eingehenden* Nachricht (`aircraft.<feld>`/`gateway.<feld>`). Nutzlast-Schlüssel wie `61-0-0` zählen nicht. Kein JSON oder ein Feld mit falschem Typ ergibt `invalid_messages: 1`. Ohne Befund kommt nichts.
- Ein ungültiger Wert zählt genau einmal: Die Gültigkeit wird an der *eingehenden* Nachricht geprüft, die Werte kommen aus dem gemergten Stand. Ein falscher Wert im Stand liefert dort nur `None`, bis ein gültiger ihn ersetzt.
- `update_topo` ergibt `Topology` und die Antwort auf `status_reply` mit `tid`/`bid`, `method`, `data.result = 0`, `timestamp` = Empfangszeit.
- `services_reply` ergibt `command_reply`.

```rust
//! Übersetzt DJI-MQTT in `Observation`s. Zustandsbehaftet nur, weil DJI Teil-Updates schickt
//! (Referenz Kap. 1): je Gerät wird der letzte Stand gehalten und jede Nachricht hineingemergt.

use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};

use serde_json::{Map, Value, json};

use crate::contract::{Battery, FlightMode, GeoPoint, Gnss, Lens, LinkQuality, RtkFix, Telemetry, VideoSource};
use crate::situation::observation::{LiveStreamInfo, ModeClass, Observation, Observed};

use super::topic::{self, DjiTopic};
use super::wire::{self, Envelope, LiveCapacity, LiveStatusEntry, ServiceReply, UpdateTopo};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Outgoing {
    pub topic: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct CommandReply {
    pub gateway_sn: String,
    pub tid: String,
    pub method: String,
    pub result: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct Translated {
    pub observations: Vec<Observed>,
    pub reply: Option<Outgoing>,
    pub command_reply: Option<CommandReply>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Translator {
    merged: HashMap<String, Map<String, Value>>,
    gateways: HashSet<String>,
    aircraft_gateway: HashMap<String, String>,
}

pub fn mode_class(code: u8) -> ModeClass {
    match code {
        0..=2 => ModeClass::Ground,
        3..=12 | 15..=18 => ModeClass::Air,
        _ => ModeClass::Unknown,
    }
}

pub fn mode_label(code: u8) -> &'static str {
    match code {
        0 => "Bereitschaft",
        1 => "Startvorbereitung",
        2 => "startbereit",
        3 => "manueller Flug",
        4 => "automatischer Start",
        5 => "Wegpunktflug",
        6 => "Panoramaaufnahme",
        7 => "Verfolgung",
        8 => "ADS-B-Ausweichen",
        9 => "Rückkehr zum Startpunkt",
        10 => "automatische Landung",
        11 => "Zwangslandung",
        12 => "Landung (Three-blade)",
        13 => "Firmware-Update",
        14 => "nicht verbunden",
        15 => "Hinderniserkennung (APAS)",
        16 => "Virtual Stick",
        17 => "Live-Flugsteuerung",
        18 => "RTK-Fixing in der Luft",
        _ => "unbekannter Modus",
    }
}

/// Kameraname aus dem `type` im Kameraindex (Referenz Kap. 0).
pub fn camera_label(camera_index: &str) -> String {
    match camera_index.split('-').next().unwrap_or("") {
        "39" => "FPV".into(),
        "42" => "H20".into(),
        "43" => "H20T".into(),
        "61" => "H20N".into(),
        "82" => "H30".into(),
        "83" => "H30T".into(),
        other => format!("Kamera {other}"),
    }
}

/// Livestream-Fehlercodes (Referenz Kap. 5, `71.error-code.md`) als deutscher Satz.
pub fn live_error_text(code: i64) -> String {
    let reason = match code {
        513002 => "Kamera existiert nicht oder Kameratyp falsch",
        513003 => "Livebild läuft bereits",
        513005 => "Qualitätseinstellung falsch",
        513006 => "Start fehlgeschlagen",
        513008 => "Bildübertragungsdaten fehlerhaft",
        513010 => "Gerät ohne Netz",
        513011 => "Livestream ist in Pilot 2 nicht aktiviert",
        513012 => "Linsenwechsel während des Streams nicht möglich",
        513013 => "Protokoll nicht unterstützt",
        513014 => "Parameter falsch oder unvollständig",
        513015 => "Netz zu langsam",
        513016 => "Dekodierung fehlgeschlagen – ist die Flugansicht in Pilot 2 offen?",
        513017 => "pausiert während eines Datei-Downloads",
        _ => "unbekannter Fehler",
    };
    format!("Pilot 2 lehnt ab: {reason} ({code}).")
}

    #[test]
    fn services_reply_is_passed_on_for_correlation() {
        let mut t = Translator::new();
        let tr = run(&mut t, 0, "thing/product/RC1/services_reply", json!({"tid": "t9", "bid": "b9", "method": "live_start_push", "data": {"result": 513011}}));
        assert_eq!(tr.command_reply, Some(CommandReply { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), tid: "t9".into(), method: "live_start_push".into(), result: 513011 }));

    #[test]
    fn whole_fixture_translates_without_invalid_messages() {
        let path = std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(FIXTURE_PATH);
        let file = std::io::BufReader::new(std::fs::File::open(path).unwrap());
        let mut t = Translator::new();
        let (mut periodic, mut invalid) = (0, 0);
        for line in read_lines(file) {
            let line = line.unwrap();
            for o in t.handle(line.at_ms().unwrap(), &line.topic, line.payload.as_bytes()).observations {
                match o.observation {
                    Observation::AircraftTelemetry { periodic: true, ref aircraft_sn, ref gateway_sn, .. } => {
                        assert_eq!((aircraft_sn.as_str(), gateway_sn.as_str()), (AIRCRAFT_SN, GATEWAY_SN));
                        periodic += 1;
                    }
                    Observation::Diagnostics { invalid_messages, .. } => invalid += invalid_messages,
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
        }
        assert!(periodic > 80, "{periodic} periodische Proben");
        assert_eq!(invalid, 0);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Übersetzer implementieren**

```rust
impl Translator {
    pub fn handle(&mut self, at_ms: i64, topic: &str, payload: &[u8]) -> Translated {
        let mut out = Translated::default();
        let parsed = topic::parse(topic);
        if parsed == DjiTopic::Other {
            return out;
        }
        let Ok(env) = serde_json::from_slice::<Envelope>(payload) else {
            out.observations.push(diag(at_ms, vec![], 1));
            return out;
        };
        match parsed {
            DjiTopic::Status { gateway } => self.on_status(at_ms, gateway, env, &mut out),
            DjiTopic::Osd { sn } => self.on_props(at_ms, sn, env, true, &mut out),
            DjiTopic::State { sn } => self.on_props(at_ms, sn, env, false, &mut out),
            DjiTopic::ServicesReply { gateway } => {
                if let (Some(tid), Some(method), Ok(reply)) =
                    (env.tid.clone(), env.method.clone(), serde_json::from_value::<ServiceReply>(env.data))
                {
                    out.command_reply = Some(CommandReply { gateway_sn: gateway.into(), tid, method, result: reply.result });
                } else {
                    out.observations.push(diag(at_ms, vec![], 1));
                }
            }
            // Für M350/RC Plus nicht dokumentiert (Referenz Kap. 0); bewusst ignoriert.
            DjiTopic::Events { .. } | DjiTopic::Requests { .. } | DjiTopic::Other => {}
        }
        out
    }

    fn on_status(&mut self, at_ms: i64, gateway: &str, env: Envelope, out: &mut Translated) {
        if env.method.as_deref() != Some("update_topo") {
            return;
        }
        let Ok(topo) = serde_json::from_value::<UpdateTopo>(env.data) else {
            out.observations.push(diag(at_ms, vec![], 1));
            return;
        };
        self.gateways.insert(gateway.to_string());
        let aircraft = topo
            .sub_devices
            .iter()
            .find(|d| d.kind == Some(wire::AIRCRAFT_TYPE) || d.kind.is_none())
            .map(|d| d.sn.clone())
            .filter(|sn| topic::is_valid_sn(sn));
        if let Some(sn) = &aircraft {
            self.aircraft_gateway.insert(sn.clone(), gateway.to_string());
        }
        out.reply = Some(Outgoing {
            topic: topic::status_reply(gateway),
            payload: json!({ "tid": env.tid, "bid": env.bid, "method": "update_topo", "timestamp": at_ms, "data": { "result": 0 } })
                .to_string()
                .into_bytes(),
        });
        out.observations.push(Observed { at_ms, observation: Observation::Topology { gateway_sn: gateway.into(), aircraft_sn: aircraft } });
    }

    fn on_props(&mut self, at_ms: i64, sn: &str, env: Envelope, periodic: bool, out: &mut Translated) {
        let Value::Object(incoming) = env.data else {
            out.observations.push(diag(at_ms, vec![], 1));
            return;
        };
        let is_gateway = env.gateway.as_deref() == Some(sn) || self.gateways.contains(sn);
        let (prefix, documented) = if is_gateway { ("gateway", wire::GATEWAY_DOCUMENTED) } else { ("aircraft", wire::AIRCRAFT_DOCUMENTED) };
        let unknown: Vec<String> = incoming
            .keys()
            .filter(|k| !documented.contains(&k.as_str()) && !wire::is_payload_key(k))
            .map(|k| format!("{prefix}.{k}"))
            .collect();
        let merged = self.merged.entry(sn.to_string()).or_default();
        deep_merge(merged, &incoming);
        let merged = merged.clone();
        // Gültigkeit an der eingehenden Nachricht prüfen, damit ein falscher Wert genau einmal zählt;
        // die Werte selbst kommen aus dem gemergten Stand.
        let mut invalid = false;
        let mut ignored = false;

        if is_gateway {
            self.gateways.insert(sn.to_string());
            gateway_values(&incoming, &mut invalid);
            let (position, battery_percent, link) = gateway_values(&merged, &mut ignored);
            out.observations.push(Observed { at_ms, observation: Observation::GatewayTelemetry {
                gateway_sn: sn.into(),
                position,
                battery_percent,
                link,
            }});
            if let Some(cap) = incoming.get("live_capacity") {
                match serde_json::from_value::<LiveCapacity>(cap.clone()) {
                    Ok(cap) => out.observations.push(Observed { at_ms, observation: Observation::VideoSources { gateway_sn: sn.into(), sources: video_sources(&cap) } }),
                    Err(_) => invalid = true,
                }
            }
            if let Some(status) = incoming.get("live_status") {
                match serde_json::from_value::<Vec<LiveStatusEntry>>(status.clone()) {
                    Ok(entries) => {
                        let live = entries.iter().find(|e| e.status == Some(1)).map(|e| LiveStreamInfo {
                            video_id: e.video_id.clone(),
                            lens: e.video_type.as_deref().map(parse_lens),
                        });
                        let error = if live.is_some() {
                            None
                        } else {
                            entries.iter().find_map(|e| e.error_status.filter(|c| *c != 0)).map(live_error_text)
                        };
                        out.observations.push(Observed { at_ms, observation: Observation::StreamStatus { gateway_sn: sn.into(), live, error } });
                    }
                    Err(_) => invalid = true,
                }
            }
        } else {
            let gateway = env.gateway.clone().filter(|g| topic::is_valid_sn(g)).or_else(|| self.aircraft_gateway.get(sn).cloned());
            if let Some(g) = &gateway {
                self.aircraft_gateway.insert(sn.to_string(), g.clone());
            }
            match gateway {
                Some(gateway_sn) => {
                    telemetry(&incoming, &mut invalid);
                    let (telemetry, mode_code) = telemetry(&merged, &mut ignored);
                    out.observations.push(Observed { at_ms, observation: Observation::AircraftTelemetry {
                        aircraft_sn: sn.into(),
                        gateway_sn,
                        telemetry,
                        mode_class: mode_code.map_or(ModeClass::Unknown, mode_class),
                        periodic,
                    }});
                }
                None => invalid = true,
            }
        }
        if !unknown.is_empty() || invalid {
            out.observations.push(diag(at_ms, unknown, u32::from(invalid)));
        }
    }

    pub fn persisted(&self, sn: &str) -> Option<(String, Map<String, Value>)> {
        let gateway = if self.gateways.contains(sn) { sn.to_string() } else { self.aircraft_gateway.get(sn)?.clone() };
        Some((gateway, self.merged.get(sn)?.clone()))
    }

    pub fn restore(&mut self, at_ms: i64, sn: &str, gateway_sn: &str, data: Map<String, Value>) -> Translated {
        let payload = json!({ "gateway": gateway_sn, "data": data }).to_string();
        let mut out = self.handle(at_ms, &format!("thing/product/{sn}/state"), payload.as_bytes());
        // Was beim ersten Empfang schon gezählt wurde, zählt nach dem Neustart nicht noch einmal.
        out.observations.retain(|o| !matches!(o.observation, Observation::Diagnostics { .. }));
        out
    }
}

fn gateway_values(m: &Map<String, Value>, invalid: &mut bool) -> (Option<GeoPoint>, Option<u8>, LinkQuality) {
    let position = geo(m, "latitude", "longitude", invalid);
    let link = m.get("wireless_link").and_then(Value::as_object).map(|w| LinkQuality {
        sdr_quality: ok(wire::int(w, "sdr_quality"), invalid),
        lte_quality: ok(wire::int(w, "4g_quality"), invalid),
    });
    (position, ok(wire::int(m, "capacity_percent"), invalid), link.unwrap_or_default())
}

fn diag(at_ms: i64, unknown_fields: Vec<String>, invalid_messages: u32) -> Observed {
    Observed { at_ms, observation: Observation::Diagnostics { unknown_fields, invalid_messages } }
}

fn ok<T>(r: Result<Option<T>, ()>, invalid: &mut bool) -> Option<T> {
    r.unwrap_or_else(|()| {
        *invalid = true;
        None
    })
}

fn deep_merge(target: &mut Map<String, Value>, patch: &Map<String, Value>) {
    for (k, v) in patch {
        match (target.get_mut(k), v) {
            (Some(Value::Object(t)), Value::Object(p)) => deep_merge(t, p),
            _ => {
                target.insert(k.clone(), v.clone());
            }
        }
    }
}

fn geo(obj: &Map<String, Value>, lat: &str, lon: &str, invalid: &mut bool) -> Option<GeoPoint> {
    let la = ok(wire::number(obj, lat), invalid)?;
    let lo = ok(wire::number(obj, lon), invalid)?;
    // DJI meldet vor dem GNSS-Fix 0/0; das ist keine Position.
    let in_range = (-90.0..=90.0).contains(&la) && (-180.0..=180.0).contains(&lo) && !(la == 0.0 && lo == 0.0);
    in_range.then_some(GeoPoint { lat: la, lon: lo })
}

fn telemetry(m: &Map<String, Value>, invalid: &mut bool) -> (Telemetry, Option<u8>) {
    let mode_code: Option<u8> = ok(wire::int(m, "mode_code"), invalid);
    let battery = m.get("battery").and_then(Value::as_object).map(|b| Battery {
        percent: ok(wire::int(b, "capacity_percent"), invalid),
        remaining_flight_s: ok(wire::int(b, "remain_flight_time"), invalid),
        return_home_percent: ok(wire::int(b, "return_home_power"), invalid),
    });
    let gnss = m.get("position_state").and_then(Value::as_object).map(|p| Gnss {
        fix: ok(wire::int::<u8>(p, "is_fixed"), invalid).and_then(|v| match v {
            0 => Some(RtkFix::NotStarted),
            1 => Some(RtkFix::Fixing),
            2 => Some(RtkFix::Fixed),
            3 => Some(RtkFix::Failed),
            _ => None,
        }),
        quality: ok(wire::int(p, "quality"), invalid),
        gps_satellites: ok(wire::int(p, "gps_number"), invalid),
        rtk_satellites: ok(wire::int(p, "rtk_number"), invalid),
    });
    let t = Telemetry {
        position: geo(m, "latitude", "longitude", invalid),
        height_ellipsoid_m: ok(wire::number(m, "height"), invalid),
        elevation_takeoff_m: ok(wire::number(m, "elevation"), invalid),
        home_distance_m: ok(wire::number(m, "home_distance"), invalid),
        horizontal_speed_mps: ok(wire::number(m, "horizontal_speed"), invalid),
        vertical_speed_mps: ok(wire::number(m, "vertical_speed"), invalid),
        heading_deg: ok(wire::number(m, "attitude_head"), invalid).map(|h| (h + 360.0) % 360.0),
        battery,
        gnss,
        mode: mode_code.map(|code| FlightMode { code, label: mode_label(code).into() }),
        home: geo(m, "home_latitude", "home_longitude", invalid),
    };
    (t, mode_code)
}

fn parse_lens(video_type: &str) -> Lens {
    match video_type {
        "wide" => Lens::Wide,
        "zoom" => Lens::Zoom,
        // Kamera-Einstellungen nennen Wärmebild „ir“, live_lens_change „thermal“ (Referenz 5.4).
        "thermal" | "ir" => Lens::Thermal,
        _ => Lens::Normal,
    }
}

fn video_sources(cap: &LiveCapacity) -> Vec<VideoSource> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for device in &cap.device_list {
        for camera in &device.camera_list {
            for video in &camera.video_list {
                out.push(VideoSource {
                    // Alle drei Teile stammen aus live_capacity (Referenz 5.1).
                    video_id: format!("{}/{}/{}", device.sn, camera.camera_index, video.video_index),
                    camera_index: camera.camera_index.clone(),
                    camera_label: camera_label(&camera.camera_index),
                    lens: video.video_type.as_deref().map_or(Lens::Normal, parse_lens),
                    switchable_lenses: video.switchable_video_types.iter().map(|v| parse_lens(v)).collect(),
                });
            }
        }
    }
    out
}
```

### Task B5: Lage-Kern `Situation` und Replay

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/situation/state.rs`, `src/situation/stream.rs`, `tests/replay.rs`
- Modify: `src/situation/mod.rs` (`pub mod state; pub mod stream;`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `situation::{observation, phase, track}` (B3), `dji::translate::Translator` (B4, nur im Integrationstest), `contract::*`
- Produces:
  - `situation::state::{Situation, Config, Effect, ResumeFlight}`
  - `Situation::new(config: Config, next_flight_id: i64, resume: Vec<ResumeFlight>) -> Situation`
  - `Situation::set_config(&mut self, Config)`
  - `Situation::apply(&mut self, Observed) -> Vec<Effect>`
  - `Situation::tick(&mut self, now_ms: i64) -> Vec<Effect>`
  - `Situation::snapshot(&self, now_ms: i64) -> SituationSnapshot`
  - `Situation::stream(&self, gateway_sn: &str) -> Option<&StreamState>`
  - `Situation::gateway(&self, sn: &str) -> Option<&GatewayState>`
  - `Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: bool }`
  - `ResumeFlight { flight: FlightSummary, segments: Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>> }`
  - `Effect::{Publish(LiveEvent), InsertFlight(FlightSummary), AppendTrackPoint { flight_id, segment, point }, UpdateFlight(FlightSummary), RequestLiveStart { gateway_sn }}`
  - `situation::stream::{on_issued, on_finished, on_status}`, reine Übergänge auf `StreamState`

**Verhalten:**
- Start: neue Flug-ID `next_flight_id`, dann hochzählen. `started_at_ms` ist die erste Luftprobe. Puffer-Punkte ab diesem Zeitpunkt werden nachgetragen.
- Landung: Der Flug schließt mit `Landed` und `ended_at_ms` = erste Bodenprobe.
- `tick`: Ein offener Flug ohne Probe seit `FLIGHT_CLOSE_AFTER_MS` schließt mit `ConnectionLost` und `ended_at_ms` = letzte Probe. `System` geht höchstens alle 5 s raus, und nur, wenn sich etwas geändert hat.
- `Topology { aircraft_sn: None }` schließt **keinen** Flug. Bei RC-Signalverlust kommt das Fluggerät oft in der Luft zurück; derselbe Flug läuft dann weiter, mit neuem Segment.
- Livebild: Ist bei einem Start `auto_live_on_takeoff` gesetzt und der Stream der RC weder `starting` noch `live`, folgt `RequestLiveStart`.

```rust
        assert_eq!(s.lens, Some(Lens::Zoom));
    }
```

```rust
pub fn on_issued(s: &mut StreamState, command: &StreamCommand, at_ms: i64) -> bool {
    let before = s.clone();
    match command {
        StreamCommand::Start { video_id } => {
            s.phase = StreamPhase::Starting;
            s.video_id = Some(video_id.clone());
            s.error = None;
        }
        StreamCommand::Stop => s.phase = StreamPhase::Stopping,
        StreamCommand::ChangeLens { .. } => {}
    }
    changed(s, &before, at_ms)
}

pub fn on_finished(s: &mut StreamState, command: &StreamCommand, result: &Result<(), String>, at_ms: i64) -> bool {
    let before = s.clone();
    match (command, result) {
        (StreamCommand::Start { .. }, Ok(())) => s.phase = StreamPhase::Live,
        (StreamCommand::Start { .. }, Err(message)) => {
            s.phase = StreamPhase::Failed;
            s.error = Some(message.clone());
        }
        (StreamCommand::Stop, Ok(())) => {
            s.phase = StreamPhase::Idle;
            s.video_id = None;
        }
        (StreamCommand::Stop, Err(_)) => s.phase = StreamPhase::Live,
        (StreamCommand::ChangeLens { lens }, Ok(())) => s.lens = Some(*lens),
        (StreamCommand::ChangeLens { .. }, Err(_)) => {}
    }
    changed(s, &before, at_ms)
}

pub fn on_status(s: &mut StreamState, live: &Option<LiveStreamInfo>, error: Option<&str>, at_ms: i64) -> bool {
    let before = s.clone();
    match live {
        Some(info) => {
            s.phase = StreamPhase::Live;
            s.video_id = Some(info.video_id.clone());
            if info.lens.is_some() {
                s.lens = info.lens;
            }
            s.error = None;
        }
        // Das Gerät meldet selbst einen Fehler: der Stream ist gescheitert, mit Grund.
        None if error.is_some() && matches!(s.phase, StreamPhase::Starting | StreamPhase::Live) => {
            s.phase = StreamPhase::Failed;
            s.error = error.map(String::from);
        }
        None if matches!(s.phase, StreamPhase::Live | StreamPhase::Stopping) => {
            s.phase = StreamPhase::Idle;
            s.video_id = None;
        }
        None => {}
    }
    changed(s, &before, at_ms)
}

fn changed(s: &mut StreamState, before: &StreamState, at_ms: i64) -> bool {
    let differs = s.phase != before.phase || s.video_id != before.video_id || s.lens != before.lens || s.error != before.error;
    if differs {
        s.updated_at_ms = at_ms;
    }
    differs
}
```

```rust
//! Der Lage-Kern: hält den Live-Zustand aller Geräte, erkennt Flüge, baut Spuren und sagt der
//! Laufzeit per `Effect`, was zu speichern und zu verteilen ist. Rein und deterministisch:
//! Zeit kommt nur über `Observed::at_ms` und `tick(now_ms)`.

use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap};

use crate::contract::*;

use super::observation::{Observation, Observed};
use super::phase::{PhaseTracker, Sample, Transition};
use super::stream;
use super::track::TrackBuilder;

const SYSTEM_PUBLISH_EVERY_MS: i64 = 5_000;
/// Punkte, die vor der bestätigten Startprobe liegen können (Entprellung über 2 Proben).
const PENDING_POINTS: usize = 4;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Config {
    pub auto_live_on_takeoff: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]

    pub flight: FlightSummary,
    pub segments: Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Effect {
    Publish(LiveEvent),
    InsertFlight(FlightSummary),
    AppendTrackPoint { flight_id: i64, segment: u32, point: TrackPoint },
    UpdateFlight(FlightSummary),
    RequestLiveStart { gateway_sn: String },
}

}

    tracker: PhaseTracker,
    flight: Option<OpenFlight>,

}

pub struct Situation {
    config: Config,
    next_flight_id: i64,
    gateways: BTreeMap<String, GatewayState>,
    aircraft: BTreeMap<String, AircraftEntry>,
    streams: BTreeMap<String, StreamState>,
    labels: HashMap<String, String>,
    broker: BrokerStatus,
    unknown: BTreeMap<String, u32>,
    invalid_messages: u32,
    raw_recording: Option<String>,
    system_dirty: bool,
    system_published_ms: i64,
}

    pub fn new(config: Config, next_flight_id: i64, resume: Vec<ResumeFlight>) -> Self {
        todo!()

        self.config = config;

    pub fn apply(&mut self, obs: Observed) -> Vec<Effect> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub fn tick(&mut self, now_ms: i64) -> Vec<Effect> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub fn snapshot(&self, now_ms: i64) -> SituationSnapshot {
        todo!()
    }

    pub fn stream(&self, gateway_sn: &str) -> Option<&StreamState> {
        self.streams.get(gateway_sn)
    }

    pub fn gateway(&self, sn: &str) -> Option<&GatewayState> {
        self.gateways.get(sn)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::situation::observation::{LiveStreamInfo, ModeClass, StreamCommand};

    const ON: Config = Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: true };
    const OFF: Config = Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: false };

    fn tel(at: i64, class: ModeClass, elevation: f64, lat: f64) -> Observed {
        Observed {
            at_ms: at,
            observation: Observation::AircraftTelemetry {
                aircraft_sn: "AC1".into(),
                gateway_sn: "RC1".into(),
                telemetry: Telemetry {
                    position: Some(GeoPoint { lat, lon: 10.0 }),
                    elevation_takeoff_m: Some(elevation),
                    ..Telemetry::default()
                },
                mode_class: class,
                periodic: true,
            },
        }
    }

    fn feed(s: &mut Situation, obs: Vec<Observed>) -> Vec<Effect> {
        obs.into_iter().flat_map(|o| s.apply(o)).collect()
    }

    fn fly(s: &mut Situation) -> Vec<Effect> {
        feed(s, vec![
            tel(0, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0, 52.000),
            tel(2_000, ModeClass::Air, 3.0, 52.001),
            tel(4_000, ModeClass::Air, 10.0, 52.002),
            tel(6_000, ModeClass::Air, 20.0, 52.003),
            tel(8_000, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0, 52.003),
            tel(10_000, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0, 52.003),
        ])
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_flight_is_detected_stored_and_published() {
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        let effects = fly(&mut s);
        let inserted: Vec<_> = effects.iter().filter_map(|e| match e { Effect::InsertFlight(f) => Some(f), _ => None }).collect();
        assert_eq!(inserted.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!((inserted[0].id, inserted[0].started_at_ms), (1, 2_000));
        let points = effects.iter().filter(|e| matches!(e, Effect::AppendTrackPoint { .. })).count();
        assert_eq!(points, 4, "2 s, 4 s, 6 s und die Probe bei 8 s vor der Bestätigung der Landung");
        let ended = effects.iter().find_map(|e| match e { Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::FlightEnded { flight }) => Some(flight), _ => None }).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ended.ended_at_ms, Some(8_000));
        assert_eq!(ended.end_reason, Some(FlightEndReason::Landed));
        assert!(ended.distance_m > 200.0);
        assert!(effects.contains(&Effect::UpdateFlight(ended.clone())));
        assert!(s.snapshot(10_000).active_flights.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn takeoff_requests_live_video_only_when_enabled_and_idle() {
        let mut on = Situation::new(ON, 1, vec![]);
        assert!(fly(&mut on).contains(&Effect::RequestLiveStart { gateway_sn: "RC1".into() }));
        let mut off = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        assert!(!fly(&mut off).iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::RequestLiveStart { .. })));

        let mut live = Situation::new(ON, 1, vec![]);
        live.apply(Observed { at_ms: 0, observation: Observation::StreamStatus {
            gateway_sn: "RC1".into(),
            live: Some(LiveStreamInfo { video_id: "v".into(), lens: None }),
            error: None,
        }});
        assert!(!fly(&mut live).iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::RequestLiveStart { .. })), "läuft schon");
    }

    #[test]
    fn resumed_flight_continues_without_a_second_insert() {
        let open = ResumeFlight {
            flight: FlightSummary { id: 41, aircraft_sn: "AC1".into(), gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), started_at_ms: 0, ended_at_ms: None, end_reason: None, max_elevation_m: Some(20.0), distance_m: 0.0 },
            segments: vec![vec![TrackPoint { t_ms: 0, lat: 52.0, lon: 10.0, elevation_m: Some(20.0) }]],
        };
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 42, vec![open]);
        let effects = feed(&mut s, vec![tel(60_000, ModeClass::Air, 30.0, 52.01), tel(62_000, ModeClass::Air, 30.0, 52.011)]);
        assert!(!effects.iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::InsertFlight(_))));
        let segments: Vec<u32> = effects.iter().filter_map(|e| match e { Effect::AppendTrackPoint { flight_id: 41, segment, .. } => Some(*segment), _ => None }).collect();
        assert_eq!(segments, vec![1, 1], "nach der Neustartlücke ein neues Segment");
        assert_eq!(s.snapshot(62_000).aircraft[0].flight_id, Some(41));
    }

    #[test]
    fn silence_closes_the_flight_as_connection_lost() {
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        feed(&mut s, vec![tel(0, ModeClass::Air, 5.0, 52.0), tel(2_000, ModeClass::Air, 6.0, 52.001)]);
        assert!(!s.tick(2_000 + FLIGHT_CLOSE_AFTER_MS).iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::UpdateFlight(_))), "genau an der Grenze noch offen");
        let effects = s.tick(2_000 + FLIGHT_CLOSE_AFTER_MS + 1);
        let ended = effects.iter().find_map(|e| match e { Effect::UpdateFlight(f) => Some(f), _ => None }).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ended.end_reason, Some(FlightEndReason::ConnectionLost));
        assert_eq!(ended.ended_at_ms, Some(2_000));
    }

    #[test]
    fn aircraft_off_in_topology_keeps_the_flight_open() {
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        feed(&mut s, vec![tel(0, ModeClass::Air, 5.0, 52.0), tel(2_000, ModeClass::Air, 6.0, 52.001)]);
        let effects = s.apply(Observed { at_ms: 3_000, observation: Observation::Topology { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), aircraft_sn: None } });
        assert!(!effects.iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::UpdateFlight(_))));
        assert_eq!(s.snapshot(3_000).active_flights.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(s.snapshot(3_000).gateways[0].aircraft_sn, None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn diagnostics_are_aggregated_and_published_throttled() {
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        let diag = |at| Observed { at_ms: at, observation: Observation::Diagnostics { unknown_fields: vec!["aircraft.neu".into()], invalid_messages: 1 } };
        assert!(s.apply(diag(0)).is_empty());
        s.apply(diag(1));
        let first = s.tick(1_000);
        assert_eq!(first.len(), 1);
        assert!(s.tick(2_000).is_empty(), "nichts Neues");
        s.apply(diag(2_500));
        assert!(s.tick(3_000).is_empty(), "gedrosselt");
        let Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::System { system }) = &s.tick(6_000)[0] else { panic!() };
        assert_eq!(system.unknown_fields, vec![UnknownFieldCount { key: "aircraft.neu".into(), count: 3 }]);
        assert_eq!(system.invalid_messages, 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn stream_commands_flow_into_state_and_events() {
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        let cmd = StreamCommand::Start { video_id: "v".into() };
        let e = s.apply(Observed { at_ms: 1, observation: Observation::CommandIssued { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), command: cmd.clone() } });
        assert!(matches!(&e[..], [Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Stream { stream })] if stream.phase == StreamPhase::Starting));
        s.apply(Observed { at_ms: 2, observation: Observation::CommandFinished { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), command: cmd, result: Err("nein".into()) } });
        assert_eq!(s.stream("RC1").unwrap().phase, StreamPhase::Failed);
    }

    #[test]
    fn state_messages_do_not_refresh_aircraft_freshness() {
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        s.apply(tel(0, ModeClass::Ground, 0.0, 52.0));
        let mut state_only = tel(20_000, ModeClass::Unknown, 0.0, 52.0);
        if let Observation::AircraftTelemetry { periodic, .. } = &mut state_only.observation {
            *periodic = false;
        }
        s.apply(state_only);
        assert_eq!(s.snapshot(20_000).aircraft[0].last_seen_ms, 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn labels_and_topology_build_the_gateway() {
        let mut s = Situation::new(OFF, 1, vec![]);
        s.apply(Observed { at_ms: 0, observation: Observation::Labels(vec![("RC1".into(), "RC Plus 1".into())]) });
        s.apply(Observed { at_ms: 1, observation: Observation::Topology { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), aircraft_sn: Some("AC1".into()) } });
        let g = s.gateway("RC1").unwrap();
        assert_eq!((g.label.as_deref(), g.aircraft_sn.as_deref(), g.last_seen_ms), (Some("RC Plus 1"), Some("AC1"), 1));
    }
}
```

```rust
impl Situation {
    pub fn new(config: Config, next_flight_id: i64, resume: Vec<ResumeFlight>) -> Self {
        let mut s = Self {
            config,
            next_flight_id,
            gateways: BTreeMap::new(),
            aircraft: BTreeMap::new(),
            streams: BTreeMap::new(),
            labels: HashMap::new(),
            broker: BrokerStatus::NotConfigured,
            unknown: BTreeMap::new(),
            invalid_messages: 0,
            raw_recording: None,
            system_dirty: false,
            system_published_ms: i64::MIN / 2,
        };
        for r in resume {
            let last_t = r.segments.iter().flatten().map(|p| p.t_ms).max().unwrap_or(r.flight.started_at_ms);
            let state = AircraftState {
                sn: r.flight.aircraft_sn.clone(),
                gateway_sn: r.flight.gateway_sn.clone(),
                last_seen_ms: last_t,
                phase: FlightPhase::Airborne,
                flight_id: Some(r.flight.id),
                telemetry: Telemetry::default(),
            };
            s.next_flight_id = s.next_flight_id.max(r.flight.id + 1);
            s.aircraft.insert(r.flight.aircraft_sn.clone(), AircraftEntry {
                state,
                tracker: PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Airborne),
                flight: Some(OpenFlight { summary: r.flight, track: TrackBuilder::from_segments(r.segments), last_sample_ms: last_t }),
                pending: Vec::new(),
            });
        }
        s
    }

    pub fn apply(&mut self, obs: Observed) -> Vec<Effect> {
        let at = obs.at_ms;
        let mut fx = Vec::new();
        match obs.observation {
            Observation::Topology { gateway_sn, aircraft_sn } => {
                let g = self.gateway_entry(&gateway_sn, at);
                g.aircraft_sn = aircraft_sn;
                g.last_seen_ms = at;
                if g.aircraft_sn.is_none() {
                    g.video_sources.clear();
                }
                fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Gateway { gateway: g.clone() }));
            }
            Observation::GatewayTelemetry { gateway_sn, position, battery_percent, link } => {
                let g = self.gateway_entry(&gateway_sn, at);
                g.position = position.or(g.position);
                g.battery_percent = battery_percent.or(g.battery_percent);
                g.link = link;
                g.last_seen_ms = at;
                fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Gateway { gateway: g.clone() }));
            }
            Observation::VideoSources { gateway_sn, sources } => {
                let g = self.gateway_entry(&gateway_sn, at);
                g.video_sources = sources;
                fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Gateway { gateway: g.clone() }));
            }
            Observation::AircraftTelemetry { aircraft_sn, gateway_sn, telemetry, mode_class, periodic } => {
                self.on_aircraft(at, aircraft_sn, gateway_sn, telemetry, mode_class, periodic, &mut fx);
            }
            Observation::StreamStatus { gateway_sn, live, error } => {
                let s = self.streams.entry(gateway_sn.clone()).or_insert_with(|| stream::idle(&gateway_sn, at));
                if stream::on_status(s, &live, error.as_deref(), at) {
                    fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Stream { stream: s.clone() }));
                }
            }
            Observation::CommandIssued { gateway_sn, command } => {
                let s = self.streams.entry(gateway_sn.clone()).or_insert_with(|| stream::idle(&gateway_sn, at));
                if stream::on_issued(s, &command, at) {
                    fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Stream { stream: s.clone() }));
                }
            }
            Observation::CommandFinished { gateway_sn, command, result } => {
                let s = self.streams.entry(gateway_sn.clone()).or_insert_with(|| stream::idle(&gateway_sn, at));
                if stream::on_finished(s, &command, &result, at) {
                    fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Stream { stream: s.clone() }));
                }
            }
            Observation::Broker(status) => {
                self.broker = status;
                fx.push(self.publish_system(at));
            }
            Observation::Diagnostics { unknown_fields, invalid_messages } => {
                for key in unknown_fields {
                    *self.unknown.entry(key).or_default() += 1;
                }
                self.invalid_messages += invalid_messages;
                self.system_dirty = true;
            }
            Observation::RawRecording(name) => {
                self.raw_recording = name;
                fx.push(self.publish_system(at));
            }
            Observation::Labels(labels) => {
                self.labels = labels.into_iter().collect();
                for g in self.gateways.values_mut() {
                    let label = self.labels.get(&g.sn).cloned();
                    if g.label != label {
                        g.label = label;
                        fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Gateway { gateway: g.clone() }));
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        fx
    }

    fn gateway_entry(&mut self, sn: &str, at: i64) -> &mut GatewayState {
        let label = self.labels.get(sn).cloned();
        self.gateways.entry(sn.to_string()).or_insert_with(|| GatewayState {
            sn: sn.into(),
            label,
            last_seen_ms: at,
            aircraft_sn: None,
            position: None,
            battery_percent: None,
            link: LinkQuality::default(),
            video_sources: vec![],
        })
    }

    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
    fn on_aircraft(&mut self, at: i64, sn: String, gateway_sn: String, telemetry: Telemetry, class: super::observation::ModeClass, periodic: bool, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
        // Nach einem Serverneustart kommt kein update_topo – die Telemetrie verrät die Zuordnung.
        let g = self.gateway_entry(&gateway_sn, at);
        if g.aircraft_sn.as_deref() != Some(&sn) {
            g.aircraft_sn = Some(sn.clone());
            fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Gateway { gateway: g.clone() }));
        }
        let stream_busy = self.streams.get(&gateway_sn).is_some_and(|s| matches!(s.phase, StreamPhase::Starting | StreamPhase::Live));
        let entry = self.aircraft.entry(sn.clone()).or_insert_with(|| AircraftEntry {
            state: AircraftState { sn: sn.clone(), gateway_sn: gateway_sn.clone(), last_seen_ms: at, phase: FlightPhase::Ground, flight_id: None, telemetry: Telemetry::default() },
            tracker: PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Ground),
            flight: None,
            pending: Vec::new(),
        });
        entry.state.gateway_sn = gateway_sn.clone();
        // Nur der periodische Strom (osd) belegt Frische; eine seltene state-Nachricht darf einen
        // osd-Ausfall nicht überdecken.
        if periodic {
            entry.state.last_seen_ms = at;
        }
        let point = telemetry.position.map(|p| TrackPoint { t_ms: at, lat: p.lat, lon: p.lon, elevation_m: telemetry.elevation_takeoff_m });
        let sample = Sample { at_ms: at, class, elevation_m: telemetry.elevation_takeoff_m, vertical_speed_mps: telemetry.vertical_speed_mps };
        // Ein state-Update ersetzt nur, was es trägt; osd ersetzt den ganzen Stand (er ist bereits gemergt).
        entry.state.telemetry = telemetry;

        if periodic {
            if let Some(p) = &point {
                entry.pending.push(p.clone());
                if entry.pending.len() > PENDING_POINTS {
                    entry.pending.remove(0);
                }
            }
            match entry.tracker.observe(&sample) {
                Some(Transition::TookOff { at_ms }) => {
                    let summary = FlightSummary {
                        id: self.next_flight_id,
                        aircraft_sn: sn.clone(),
                        gateway_sn: gateway_sn.clone(),
                        started_at_ms: at_ms,
                        ended_at_ms: None,
                        end_reason: None,
                        max_elevation_m: None,
                        distance_m: 0.0,
                    };
                    self.next_flight_id += 1;
                    fx.push(Effect::InsertFlight(summary.clone()));
                    fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::FlightStarted { flight: summary.clone() }));
                    let mut flight = OpenFlight { summary, track: TrackBuilder::new(), last_sample_ms: at };
                    for p in entry.pending.drain(..).filter(|p| p.t_ms >= at_ms) {
                        push_point(&mut flight, p, fx);
                    }
                    entry.flight = Some(flight);
                    if self.config.auto_live_on_takeoff && !stream_busy {
                        fx.push(Effect::RequestLiveStart { gateway_sn: gateway_sn.clone() });
                    }
                }
                Some(Transition::Landed { at_ms }) => {
                    if let Some(flight) = entry.flight.take() {
                        let done = close(flight, at_ms, FlightEndReason::Landed);
                        fx.push(Effect::UpdateFlight(done.clone()));
                        fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::FlightEnded { flight: done }));
                    }
                }
                None => {
                    if let (Some(flight), Some(p)) = (entry.flight.as_mut(), point) {
                        flight.last_sample_ms = at;
                        push_point(flight, p, fx);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        entry.state.phase = entry.tracker.phase();
        entry.state.flight_id = entry.flight.as_ref().map(|f| f.summary.id);
        fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Aircraft { aircraft: entry.state.clone() }));
    }

    pub fn tick(&mut self, now_ms: i64) -> Vec<Effect> {
        let mut fx = Vec::new();
        for entry in self.aircraft.values_mut() {
            let stale = entry.flight.as_ref().is_some_and(|f| now_ms - f.last_sample_ms > FLIGHT_CLOSE_AFTER_MS);
            if stale {
                let flight = entry.flight.take().unwrap();
                let last = flight.last_sample_ms;
                let done = close(flight, last, FlightEndReason::ConnectionLost);
                entry.tracker = PhaseTracker::new(FlightPhase::Ground);
                entry.state.phase = FlightPhase::Ground;
                entry.state.flight_id = None;
                fx.push(Effect::UpdateFlight(done.clone()));
                fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::FlightEnded { flight: done }));
                fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::Aircraft { aircraft: entry.state.clone() }));
            }
        }
        if self.system_dirty && now_ms - self.system_published_ms >= SYSTEM_PUBLISH_EVERY_MS {
            fx.push(self.publish_system(now_ms));
        }
        fx
    }

    fn system(&self) -> SystemStatus {
        SystemStatus {
            broker: self.broker,
            unknown_fields: self.unknown.iter().map(|(key, count)| UnknownFieldCount { key: key.clone(), count: *count }).collect(),
            invalid_messages: self.invalid_messages,
            raw_recording: self.raw_recording.clone(),
        }
    }

    fn publish_system(&mut self, now_ms: i64) -> Effect {
        self.system_dirty = false;
        self.system_published_ms = now_ms;
        Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::System { system: self.system() })
    }

    pub fn snapshot(&self, now_ms: i64) -> SituationSnapshot {
        SituationSnapshot {
            server_time_ms: now_ms,
            freshness: FreshnessThresholds { delayed_after_ms: FRESHNESS_DELAYED_MS, lost_after_ms: FRESHNESS_LOST_MS },
            gateways: self.gateways.values().cloned().collect(),
            aircraft: self.aircraft.values().map(|a| a.state.clone()).collect(),
            active_flights: self
                .aircraft
                .values()
                .filter_map(|a| a.flight.as_ref())
                .map(|f| ActiveFlight { flight: summary_now(f), segments: f.track.segments().to_vec() })
                .collect(),
            streams: self.streams.values().cloned().collect(),
            system: self.system(),
        }
    }
}

fn push_point(flight: &mut OpenFlight, p: TrackPoint, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if let Some(appended) = flight.track.push(p) {
        let id = flight.summary.id;
        fx.push(Effect::AppendTrackPoint { flight_id: id, segment: appended.segment, point: appended.point.clone() });
        fx.push(Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::TrackPoint { flight_id: id, segment: appended.segment, point: appended.point }));
    }
}

fn summary_now(f: &OpenFlight) -> FlightSummary {
    FlightSummary { distance_m: f.track.distance_m(), max_elevation_m: f.track.max_elevation_m(), ..f.summary.clone() }
}

fn close(f: OpenFlight, ended_at_ms: i64, reason: FlightEndReason) -> FlightSummary {
    FlightSummary { ended_at_ms: Some(ended_at_ms), end_reason: Some(reason), ..summary_now(&f) }
}
```
