```rust
//! Replay des synthetischen Flugs durch Übersetzer und Lage-Kern. Echte, bereinigte Mitschnitte
//! kommen nach dem ersten Feldtest als weitere Fixture dazu (Plan M1, Offene Punkte).

use std::io::BufReader;

use suchlage::contract::{FlightEndReason, LiveEvent};
use suchlage::dji::record::read_lines;
use suchlage::dji::translate::Translator;
use suchlage::sim::synth::FIXTURE_PATH;
use suchlage::situation::state::{Config, Effect, Situation};

pub fn replay(path: &str) -> (Situation, Vec<Effect>) {
    let file = BufReader::new(std::fs::File::open(path).unwrap());
    let mut translator = Translator::new();
    let mut situation = Situation::new(Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: true }, 1, vec![]);
    let mut effects = Vec::new();
    for line in read_lines(file) {
        let line = line.unwrap();
        let at = line.at_ms().unwrap();
        for obs in translator.handle(at, &line.topic, line.payload.as_bytes()).observations {
            effects.extend(situation.apply(obs));
        }
        effects.extend(situation.tick(at));
    }
    (situation, effects)
}

#[test]
fn synthetic_flight_yields_one_landed_flight_with_two_segments() {
    let (situation, effects) = replay(FIXTURE_PATH);
    let ended: Vec<_> = effects
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|e| match e { Effect::Publish(LiveEvent::FlightEnded { flight }) => Some(flight), _ => None })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(ended.len(), 1, "genau ein Flug");
    let flight = ended[0];
    assert_eq!(flight.end_reason, Some(FlightEndReason::Landed));
    let duration_s = (flight.ended_at_ms.unwrap() - flight.started_at_ms) / 1000;
    assert!((150..=170).contains(&duration_s), "Flugdauer {duration_s} s");
    assert!(flight.distance_m > 800.0, "Strecke {} m (ohne die Lücke)", flight.distance_m);
    assert_eq!(flight.max_elevation_m, Some(60.0));

    let segments: std::collections::BTreeSet<u32> = effects
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|e| match e { Effect::AppendTrackPoint { segment, .. } => Some(*segment), _ => None })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(segments.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec![0, 1], "die 15-s-Lücke trennt die Spur");

    assert!(effects.contains(&Effect::RequestLiveStart { gateway_sn: "RCSIM0001".into() }));
    let snapshot = situation.snapshot(0);
    assert_eq!(snapshot.gateways[0].aircraft_sn, None, "Fluggerät am Ende ausgeschaltet");
    assert_eq!(snapshot.system.unknown_fields[0].key, "aircraft.extra_probe");
    assert_eq!(snapshot.system.invalid_messages, 0);
}
```

`migrations/0001_init.sql`:

```sql
-- Schema M1. Zeiten in ms seit Epoch (UTC).

CREATE TABLE users (
  id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  oidc_issuer   TEXT NOT NULL,
  oidc_subject  TEXT NOT NULL,
  display_name  TEXT NOT NULL,
  role          TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('admin', 'operator')),
  created_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,
  UNIQUE (oidc_issuer, oidc_subject)
);

CREATE TABLE kiosk_tokens (
  id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  label         TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(label) BETWEEN 1 AND 80),
  token_hash    TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  created_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE sessions (
  token_hash     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id        INTEGER REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  kiosk_token_id INTEGER REFERENCES kiosk_tokens(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  expires_at_ms  INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at_ms  INTEGER NOT NULL,
  CHECK ((user_id IS NULL) <> (kiosk_token_id IS NULL))
);

CREATE TABLE settings (
  key           TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  value         TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE remote_controllers (
  id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  label             TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (length(label) BETWEEN 1 AND 80),
  pairing_code_hash TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  sn                TEXT UNIQUE,
  mqtt_password     TEXT,
  stream_key        TEXT,
  created_at_ms     INTEGER NOT NULL,
  paired_at_ms      INTEGER,
  CHECK ((sn IS NULL) = (paired_at_ms IS NULL))
);

CREATE TABLE flights (
  id              INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  aircraft_sn     TEXT NOT NULL,
  gateway_sn      TEXT NOT NULL,
  started_at_ms   INTEGER NOT NULL,
  ended_at_ms     INTEGER,
  end_reason      TEXT CHECK (end_reason IN ('landed', 'connection_lost')),
  max_elevation_m REAL,
  distance_m      REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  CHECK ((ended_at_ms IS NULL) = (end_reason IS NULL))
);
-- Spec §5: höchstens ein laufender Flug je Fluggerät – auch über einen Neustart hinweg.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX flights_one_open_per_aircraft ON flights (aircraft_sn) WHERE ended_at_ms IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX flights_by_start ON flights (started_at_ms DESC);

CREATE TABLE track_points (
  flight_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES flights(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  t_ms        INTEGER NOT NULL,
  segment     INTEGER NOT NULL,
  lat         REAL NOT NULL,
  lon         REAL NOT NULL,
  elevation_m REAL,
  PRIMARY KEY (flight_id, t_ms)
) WITHOUT ROWID;

-- Letzter gemergter Gerätestand (osd+state) je Gerät. DJI schickt live_capacity, live_status und
-- den Startpunkt nur bei Änderung; nach einem Neustart kämen sie sonst nie wieder (Referenz 5.1).
CREATE TABLE device_state (
  sn            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  gateway_sn    TEXT NOT NULL,
  data          TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_at_ms INTEGER NOT NULL
);
```

```rust
//! SQLite-Anschluss nach Lifeline `db.rs`: WAL, busy_timeout 5 s, Fremdschlüssel, 5 Verbindungen.

use std::path::Path;
use std::time::Duration;

use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use sqlx::sqlite::{SqliteConnectOptions, SqliteJournalMode, SqlitePoolOptions};

pub async fn connect(path: &Path) -> Result<SqlitePool, sqlx::Error> {
    let options = SqliteConnectOptions::new()
        .filename(path)
        .create_if_missing(true)
        .journal_mode(SqliteJournalMode::Wal)
        .busy_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))
        .foreign_keys(true);
    SqlitePoolOptions::new().max_connections(5).acquire_timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).connect_with(options).await
}

pub async fn migrate(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<(), sqlx::migrate::MigrateError> {
    sqlx::migrate!("./migrations").run(pool).await
}

/// Frische In-Memory-DB je Test. Eine Verbindung, sonst sähe jede ihre eigene leere DB.
#[cfg(test)]
pub async fn test_pool() -> SqlitePool {
    let options = SqliteConnectOptions::new().in_memory(true).foreign_keys(true);
    let pool = SqlitePoolOptions::new().max_connections(1).connect_with(options).await.unwrap();
    migrate(&pool).await.unwrap();
    pool
}
```

```rust
//! Zufallstokens (256 Bit) und ihr Hash. In der DB steht nur der Hash (Lifeline-Muster).

use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

pub fn generate() -> String {
    let mut bytes = [0u8; 32];
    getrandom::fill(&mut bytes).expect("Betriebssystem liefert Zufall");
    hex::encode(bytes)
}

pub fn hash(token: &str) -> String {
    hex::encode(Sha256::digest(token.as_bytes()))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[test]
    fn tokens_are_long_unique_and_hash_stably() {
        let (a, b) = (super::generate(), super::generate());
        assert_eq!(a.len(), 64);
        assert_ne!(a, b);
        assert_eq!(super::hash("x"), super::hash("x"));
        assert_ne!(super::hash(&a), a);
    }
}
```

```rust
use sqlx::{Row, SqlitePool};

use crate::contract::{FlightEndReason, FlightSummary, TrackPoint};

fn reason_str(r: Option<FlightEndReason>) -> Option<&'static str> {
    r.map(|r| match r {
        FlightEndReason::Landed => "landed",
        FlightEndReason::ConnectionLost => "connection_lost",
    })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::db::test_pool;
    use crate::error::AppError;

    fn flight(id: i64, aircraft: &str) -> FlightSummary {
        FlightSummary { id, aircraft_sn: aircraft.into(), gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), started_at_ms: id * 1_000, ended_at_ms: None, end_reason: None, max_elevation_m: None, distance_m: 0.0 }
    }

    fn pt(t: i64) -> TrackPoint {
        TrackPoint { t_ms: t, lat: 52.0, lon: 10.0, elevation_m: Some(1.0) }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn flight_lifecycle_with_segments() {
        let pool = test_pool().await;
        assert_eq!(next_id(&pool).await.unwrap(), 1);
        insert(&pool, &flight(1, "AC1")).await.unwrap();
        append_points(&pool, 1, &[(0, pt(1)), (0, pt(2)), (1, pt(20))]).await.unwrap();
        append_points(&pool, 1, &[(1, pt(20))]).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(segments(&pool, 1).await.unwrap().iter().map(Vec::len).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec![2, 1]);
        assert_eq!(open(&pool).await.unwrap().len(), 1);

        let done = FlightSummary { ended_at_ms: Some(30), end_reason: Some(FlightEndReason::Landed), distance_m: 12.5, ..flight(1, "AC1") };
        update(&pool, &done).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(get(&pool, 1).await.unwrap(), Some(done));
        assert!(open(&pool).await.unwrap().is_empty());
        assert_eq!(next_id(&pool).await.unwrap(), 2);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn second_open_flight_for_the_same_aircraft_is_a_conflict() {
        let pool = test_pool().await;
        insert(&pool, &flight(1, "AC1")).await.unwrap();
        let err: AppError = insert(&pool, &flight(2, "AC1")).await.unwrap_err().into();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Conflict(_)), "{err:?}");
        insert(&pool, &flight(3, "AC2")).await.unwrap();
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn list_is_newest_first() {
        let pool = test_pool().await;
        for id in 1..=3 {
            let mut f = flight(id, &format!("AC{id}"));
            f.ended_at_ms = Some(id * 1_000 + 1);
            f.end_reason = Some(FlightEndReason::Landed);
            insert(&pool, &f).await.unwrap();
        }
        let ids: Vec<i64> = list(&pool, 2).await.unwrap().iter().map(|f| f.id).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, vec![3, 2]);
    }
}
```

`src/error.rs` ergänzen (Test im selben Modul):

```rust
impl From<sqlx::Error> for AppError {
    fn from(e: sqlx::Error) -> Self {
        if let sqlx::Error::Database(db) = &e {
            match db.kind() {
                sqlx::error::ErrorKind::UniqueViolation | sqlx::error::ErrorKind::ForeignKeyViolation => {
                    return AppError::Conflict("Eintrag steht im Konflikt mit dem bestehenden Stand".into());
                }
                sqlx::error::ErrorKind::CheckViolation => {
                    return AppError::Unprocessable("Eingabe verletzt eine Regel des Datenmodells".into());
                }
                _ => {}
            }
            // SQLITE_BUSY (5) und SQLITE_LOCKED (6): später erneut versuchen.
            if matches!(db.code().as_deref(), Some("5") | Some("6")) {
                return AppError::Unavailable("Datenbank ist gerade beschäftigt".into());
            }
        }
        if matches!(e, sqlx::Error::PoolTimedOut) {
            return AppError::Unavailable("Datenbank ist gerade beschäftigt".into());
        }
        AppError::Internal(e.to_string())
    }
}
```

```rust
pub async fn insert(pool: &SqlitePool, f: &FlightSummary) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
    sqlx::query("INSERT INTO flights (id, aircraft_sn, gateway_sn, started_at_ms, ended_at_ms, end_reason, max_elevation_m, distance_m) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)")
        .bind(f.id).bind(&f.aircraft_sn).bind(&f.gateway_sn).bind(f.started_at_ms).bind(f.ended_at_ms)
        .bind(reason_str(f.end_reason)).bind(f.max_elevation_m).bind(f.distance_m)
        .execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn update(pool: &SqlitePool, f: &FlightSummary) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
    sqlx::query("UPDATE flights SET ended_at_ms = ?, end_reason = ?, max_elevation_m = ?, distance_m = ? WHERE id = ?")
        .bind(f.ended_at_ms).bind(reason_str(f.end_reason)).bind(f.max_elevation_m).bind(f.distance_m).bind(f.id)
        .execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn append_points(pool: &SqlitePool, flight_id: i64, points: &[(u32, TrackPoint)]) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
    let mut tx = pool.begin().await?;
    for (segment, p) in points {
        sqlx::query("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO track_points (flight_id, t_ms, segment, lat, lon, elevation_m) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)")
            .bind(flight_id).bind(p.t_ms).bind(i64::from(*segment)).bind(p.lat).bind(p.lon).bind(p.elevation_m)
            .execute(&mut *tx).await?;
    }
    tx.commit().await
}

pub async fn list(pool: &SqlitePool, limit: i64) -> Result<Vec<FlightSummary>, sqlx::Error> {
    let rows = sqlx::query(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM flights ORDER BY started_at_ms DESC, id DESC LIMIT ?"))
        .bind(limit).fetch_all(pool).await?;
    Ok(rows.iter().map(summary).collect())
}

pub async fn get(pool: &SqlitePool, id: i64) -> Result<Option<FlightSummary>, sqlx::Error> {
    let row = sqlx::query(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM flights WHERE id = ?")).bind(id).fetch_optional(pool).await?;
    Ok(row.as_ref().map(summary))
}

pub async fn segments(pool: &SqlitePool, id: i64) -> Result<Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>>, sqlx::Error> {
    let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT segment, t_ms, lat, lon, elevation_m FROM track_points WHERE flight_id = ? ORDER BY t_ms")
        .bind(id).fetch_all(pool).await?;
    let mut out: Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>> = Vec::new();
    let mut current: Option<i64> = None;
    for row in rows {
        let segment: i64 = row.get("segment");
        if current != Some(segment) {
            out.push(Vec::new());
            current = Some(segment);
        }
        out.last_mut().unwrap().push(TrackPoint { t_ms: row.get("t_ms"), lat: row.get("lat"), lon: row.get("lon"), elevation_m: row.get("elevation_m") });
    }
    Ok(out)
}

pub async fn open(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<(FlightSummary, Vec<Vec<TrackPoint>>)>, sqlx::Error> {
    let rows = sqlx::query(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM flights WHERE ended_at_ms IS NULL")).fetch_all(pool).await?;
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for row in rows {
        let f = summary(&row);
        let segs = segments(pool, f.id).await?;
        out.push((f, segs));
    }
    Ok(out)
}

pub async fn next_id(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<i64, sqlx::Error> {
    sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(id), 0) + 1 FROM flights").fetch_one(pool).await
}
```

```rust
use sqlx::{Row, SqlitePool};

use crate::contract::Role;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct User {
    pub id: i64,
    pub display_name: String,
    pub role: Role,
}

fn role(s: &str) -> Role {
    if s == "admin" { Role::Admin } else { Role::Operator }
}

/// Findet das Konto über (issuer, subject) oder legt es an. Das erste Konto überhaupt wird Admin (ADR 0002).
pub async fn upsert_oidc(pool: &SqlitePool, issuer: &str, subject: &str, display_name: &str, now_ms: i64) -> Result<User, sqlx::Error> {
    let mut tx = pool.begin().await?;
    let existing = sqlx::query("SELECT id, role FROM users WHERE oidc_issuer = ? AND oidc_subject = ?")
        .bind(issuer).bind(subject).fetch_optional(&mut *tx).await?;
    let user = if let Some(row) = existing {
        let id: i64 = row.get("id");
        sqlx::query("UPDATE users SET display_name = ? WHERE id = ?").bind(display_name).bind(id).execute(&mut *tx).await?;
        User { id, display_name: display_name.into(), role: role(row.get("role")) }
    } else {
        let first: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users").fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;
        let r = if first == 0 { "admin" } else { "operator" };
        let id: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("INSERT INTO users (oidc_issuer, oidc_subject, display_name, role, created_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) RETURNING id")
            .bind(issuer).bind(subject).bind(display_name).bind(r).bind(now_ms).fetch_one(&mut *tx).await?;
        User { id, display_name: display_name.into(), role: role(r) }
    };
    tx.commit().await?;
    Ok(user)
}

pub async fn get(pool: &SqlitePool, id: i64) -> Result<Option<User>, sqlx::Error> {
    let row = sqlx::query("SELECT id, display_name, role FROM users WHERE id = ?").bind(id).fetch_optional(pool).await?;
    Ok(row.map(|r| User { id: r.get("id"), display_name: r.get("display_name"), role: role(r.get("role")) }))
}

pub async fn count(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<i64, sqlx::Error> {
    sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users").fetch_one(pool).await
}

#[cfg(test)]

    use crate::db::test_pool;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn first_account_becomes_admin_later_ones_operator_and_login_is_stable() {
        let pool = test_pool().await;
        let a = upsert_oidc(&pool, "https://id", "sub-a", "Ruben", 1).await.unwrap();
        let b = upsert_oidc(&pool, "https://id", "sub-b", "Gast", 2).await.unwrap();
        let a2 = upsert_oidc(&pool, "https://id", "sub-a", "Ruben Vitt", 3).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!((a.role, b.role), (Role::Admin, Role::Operator));
        assert_eq!((a2.id, a2.role, a2.display_name.as_str()), (a.id, Role::Admin, "Ruben Vitt"));
        assert_eq!(count(&pool).await.unwrap(), 2);
    }
}
```

```rust
use sqlx::{Row, SqlitePool};

use crate::contract::KioskTokenView;

pub async fn create(pool: &SqlitePool, label: &str, now_ms: i64) -> Result<(KioskTokenView, String), sqlx::Error> {

    let id: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("INSERT INTO kiosk_tokens (label, token_hash, created_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING id")
        .bind(label).bind(token::hash(&secret)).bind(now_ms).fetch_one(pool).await?;
    Ok((KioskTokenView { id, label: label.into(), created_at_ms: now_ms }, secret))
```

```rust
use sqlx::{Row, SqlitePool};

use super::users::{self, User};
use crate::token;

pub const USER_TTL_MS: i64 = 7 * 24 * 3_600_000;
/// Großbild läuft dauerhaft; Schluss macht der Widerruf des Tokens.
pub const KIOSK_TTL_MS: i64 = 365 * 24 * 3_600_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Principal {
    User(User),
    Kiosk { token_id: i64, label: String },
}

async fn create(pool: &SqlitePool, user_id: Option<i64>, kiosk_id: Option<i64>, ttl: i64, now_ms: i64) -> Result<String, sqlx::Error> {
    let secret = token::generate();
    sqlx::query("INSERT INTO sessions (token_hash, user_id, kiosk_token_id, expires_at_ms, created_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)")
        .bind(token::hash(&secret)).bind(user_id).bind(kiosk_id).bind(now_ms + ttl).bind(now_ms)
        .execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(secret)
}

pub async fn create_for_user(pool: &SqlitePool, user_id: i64, now_ms: i64) -> Result<String, sqlx::Error> {
    create(pool, Some(user_id), None, USER_TTL_MS, now_ms).await
}

pub async fn create_for_kiosk(pool: &SqlitePool, token_id: i64, now_ms: i64) -> Result<String, sqlx::Error> {
    create(pool, None, Some(token_id), KIOSK_TTL_MS, now_ms).await
}

pub async fn lookup(pool: &SqlitePool, secret: &str, now_ms: i64) -> Result<Option<Principal>, sqlx::Error> {
    let row = sqlx::query(
        "SELECT s.user_id, s.kiosk_token_id, k.label FROM sessions s LEFT JOIN kiosk_tokens k ON k.id = s.kiosk_token_id
         WHERE s.token_hash = ? AND s.expires_at_ms > ?",
    )
    .bind(token::hash(secret)).bind(now_ms).fetch_optional(pool).await?;
    let Some(row) = row else { return Ok(None) };
    if let Some(user_id) = row.get::<Option<i64>, _>("user_id") {
        return Ok(users::get(pool, user_id).await?.map(Principal::User));
    }
    Ok(Some(Principal::Kiosk { token_id: row.get("kiosk_token_id"), label: row.get("label") }))
}

pub async fn delete(pool: &SqlitePool, secret: &str) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
    sqlx::query("DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token_hash = ?").bind(token::hash(secret)).execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::db::test_pool;
    use crate::store::{kiosk, users};

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn sessions_expire_and_die_with_their_kiosk_token() {
        let pool = test_pool().await;
        let u = users::upsert_oidc(&pool, "i", "s", "Ruben", 0).await.unwrap();
        let s = create_for_user(&pool, u.id, 0).await.unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(lookup(&pool, &s, 1).await.unwrap(), Some(Principal::User(_))));
        assert_eq!(lookup(&pool, &s, USER_TTL_MS).await.unwrap(), None, "abgelaufen");
        assert_eq!(lookup(&pool, "falsch", 1).await.unwrap(), None);

        let (view, secret) = kiosk::create(&pool, "FüKw", 0).await.unwrap();
        let tid = kiosk::find_by_token(&pool, &secret).await.unwrap().unwrap();
        let ks = create_for_kiosk(&pool, tid, 0).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(lookup(&pool, &ks, 1).await.unwrap(), Some(Principal::Kiosk { token_id: view.id, label: "FüKw".into() }));
        assert!(kiosk::delete(&pool, view.id).await.unwrap());
        assert_eq!(lookup(&pool, &ks, 1).await.unwrap(), None, "Widerruf beendet die Sitzung");
    }
}
```

```rust
use serde::Serialize;
use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

pub async fn get<T: DeserializeOwned>(pool: &SqlitePool, key: &str) -> Result<Option<T>, sqlx::Error> {
    let raw: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key = ?").bind(key).fetch_optional(pool).await?;
    Ok(raw.and_then(|r| match serde_json::from_str(&r) {
        Ok(v) => Some(v),
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::warn!(key, error = %e, "gespeicherte Einstellung nicht lesbar – Vorgabe gilt");
            None
        }
    }))
}

pub async fn put<T: Serialize>(pool: &SqlitePool, key: &str, value: &T, now_ms: i64) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
    let json = serde_json::to_string(value).expect("Einstellungen sind serialisierbar");
    sqlx::query("INSERT INTO settings (key, value, updated_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value, updated_at_ms = excluded.updated_at_ms")
        .bind(key).bind(json).bind(now_ms).execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn put_overwrites_and_get_reads_back() {
        let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
        assert_eq!(super::get::<u32>(&pool, "x").await.unwrap(), None);
        super::put(&pool, "x", &1u32, 0).await.unwrap();
        super::put(&pool, "x", &2u32, 1).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(super::get::<u32>(&pool, "x").await.unwrap(), Some(2));
    }
}
```

```rust
use sqlx::{Row, SqlitePool};

use crate::contract::RemoteControllerView;
use crate::token;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct RemoteController {
    pub id: i64,
    pub label: String,
    pub sn: Option<String>,
    pub mqtt_password: Option<String>,
    pub stream_key: Option<String>,
    pub paired_at_ms: Option<i64>,
}

const COLUMNS: &str = "id, label, sn, mqtt_password, stream_key, paired_at_ms";

fn row(r: &sqlx::sqlite::SqliteRow) -> RemoteController {
    RemoteController { id: r.get("id"), label: r.get("label"), sn: r.get("sn"), mqtt_password: r.get("mqtt_password"), stream_key: r.get("stream_key"), paired_at_ms: r.get("paired_at_ms") }
}

pub fn view(rc: &RemoteController) -> RemoteControllerView {
    RemoteControllerView { id: rc.id, label: rc.label.clone(), sn: rc.sn.clone(), paired_at_ms: rc.paired_at_ms }
}

/// Legt eine Fernsteuerung an und gibt den Kopplungscode genau einmal zurück.
pub async fn create(pool: &SqlitePool, label: &str, now_ms: i64) -> Result<(RemoteController, String), sqlx::Error> {
    let code = token::generate();
    Ok((create_with_code(pool, label, &code, now_ms).await?, code))
}

/// Mit vorgegebenem Code. Außerhalb der Tests nur für die Simulator-Fernsteuerung im Feature `dev` (B14).
pub async fn create_with_code(pool: &SqlitePool, label: &str, code: &str, now_ms: i64) -> Result<RemoteController, sqlx::Error> {
    let id: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("INSERT INTO remote_controllers (label, pairing_code_hash, created_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?) RETURNING id")
        .bind(label).bind(token::hash(code)).bind(now_ms).fetch_one(pool).await?;
    Ok(RemoteController { id, label: label.into(), sn: None, mqtt_password: None, stream_key: None, paired_at_ms: None })
}

pub async fn list(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<RemoteController>, sqlx::Error> {
    Ok(sqlx::query(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM remote_controllers ORDER BY id")).fetch_all(pool).await?.iter().map(row).collect())
}

pub async fn delete(pool: &SqlitePool, id: i64) -> Result<Option<RemoteController>, sqlx::Error> {
    let r = sqlx::query(&format!("DELETE FROM remote_controllers WHERE id = ? RETURNING {COLUMNS}")).bind(id).fetch_optional(pool).await?;
    Ok(r.as_ref().map(row))
}

pub async fn find_by_code(pool: &SqlitePool, code: &str) -> Result<Option<RemoteController>, sqlx::Error> {
    let r = sqlx::query(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM remote_controllers WHERE pairing_code_hash = ?")).bind(token::hash(code)).fetch_optional(pool).await?;
    Ok(r.as_ref().map(row))
}

pub async fn find_by_sn(pool: &SqlitePool, sn: &str) -> Result<Option<RemoteController>, sqlx::Error> {
    let r = sqlx::query(&format!("SELECT {COLUMNS} FROM remote_controllers WHERE sn = ?")).bind(sn).fetch_optional(pool).await?;
    Ok(r.as_ref().map(row))
}

pub async fn bind(pool: &SqlitePool, id: i64, sn: &str, mqtt_password: &str, stream_key: &str, now_ms: i64) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
    sqlx::query("UPDATE remote_controllers SET sn = ?, mqtt_password = ?, stream_key = ?, paired_at_ms = ? WHERE id = ?")
        .bind(sn).bind(mqtt_password).bind(stream_key).bind(now_ms).bind(id).execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn labels(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<(String, String)>, sqlx::Error> {
    let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT sn, label FROM remote_controllers WHERE sn IS NOT NULL").fetch_all(pool).await?;
    Ok(rows.iter().map(|r| (r.get("sn"), r.get("label"))).collect())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::db::test_pool;
    use crate::error::AppError;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn pairing_binds_the_serial_number_once() {
        let pool = test_pool().await;
        let (rc, code) = create(&pool, "RC Plus 1", 0).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(find_by_code(&pool, &code).await.unwrap().unwrap().id, rc.id);
        assert_eq!(find_by_code(&pool, "falsch").await.unwrap(), None);
        bind(&pool, rc.id, "RC1", "pw", "key", 5).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(find_by_sn(&pool, "RC1").await.unwrap().unwrap().paired_at_ms, Some(5));
        assert_eq!(labels(&pool).await.unwrap(), vec![("RC1".to_string(), "RC Plus 1".to_string())]);

        let (other, _) = create(&pool, "RC Plus 2", 0).await.unwrap();
        let err: AppError = bind(&pool, other.id, "RC1", "pw", "key", 6).await.unwrap_err().into();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Conflict(_)), "SN gehört schon RC Plus 1");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn empty_label_is_rejected_by_the_schema() {
        let pool = test_pool().await;
        let err: AppError = create(&pool, "", 0).await.unwrap_err().into();
        assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Unprocessable(_)));
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Letzter Gerätestand als JSON (Spec §5, Neustart). Der Speicher kennt das Format nicht; das
//! liest und schreibt nur der DJI-Adapter (B12).

use serde_json::Value;
use sqlx::{Row, SqlitePool};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct StoredDevice {
    pub sn: String,
    pub gateway_sn: String,
    pub data: Value,
    pub updated_at_ms: i64,
}

pub async fn put(pool: &SqlitePool, sn: &str, gateway_sn: &str, data: &Value, now_ms: i64) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {
    sqlx::query("INSERT INTO device_state (sn, gateway_sn, data, updated_at_ms) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?) ON CONFLICT(sn) DO UPDATE SET gateway_sn = excluded.gateway_sn, data = excluded.data, updated_at_ms = excluded.updated_at_ms")
        .bind(sn).bind(gateway_sn).bind(data.to_string()).bind(now_ms).execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn all(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<StoredDevice>, sqlx::Error> {
    let rows = sqlx::query("SELECT sn, gateway_sn, data, updated_at_ms FROM device_state ORDER BY updated_at_ms").fetch_all(pool).await?;
    Ok(rows
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|r| {
            let data = serde_json::from_str(r.get::<&str, _>("data")).ok()?;
            Some(StoredDevice { sn: r.get("sn"), gateway_sn: r.get("gateway_sn"), data, updated_at_ms: r.get("updated_at_ms") })
        })
        .collect())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn last_state_per_device_wins() {
        let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
        super::put(&pool, "RC1", "RC1", &serde_json::json!({"a": 1}), 1).await.unwrap();
        super::put(&pool, "RC1", "RC1", &serde_json::json!({"a": 2}), 2).await.unwrap();
        let all = super::all(&pool).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(all.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!((all[0].data["a"].as_i64(), all[0].updated_at_ms), (Some(2), 2));
    }
}
```

### Task B7: Laufzeit-Aktor, Hub, Neustart-Resume

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/runtime/mod.rs`, `src/runtime/service.rs`
- Modify: `src/lib.rs` (`pub mod runtime;`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `situation::state::{Situation, Config, Effect, ResumeFlight}` (B5), `store::flights` (B6), `time::now_ms`
- Produces:
  - `runtime::service::start(pool: SqlitePool, config: Config, clock: Clock, live_requests: mpsc::UnboundedSender<String>, shutdown: watch::Receiver<bool>) -> Result<(SituationHandle, JoinHandle<()>), sqlx::Error>`
  - `runtime::Clock = Arc<dyn Fn() -> i64 + Send + Sync>`, `runtime::system_clock() -> Clock`
  - `SituationHandle` (Clone) mit:
    - `observe(Observed)` (async)
    - `observer() -> mpsc::Sender<Observed>` für den MQTT-Task: ein Weiterleiter in denselben Befehlskanal, damit `dji` nichts von `runtime` kennt und die Reihenfolge erhalten bleibt
    - `subscribe() -> Result<(SituationSnapshot, broadcast::Receiver<LiveEvent>), AppError>`
    - `snapshot()`, `gateway(sn) -> Option<GatewayState>`, `stream(sn) -> Option<StreamState>`
    - `set_config(Config)`
- Verhalten:
  - Der Aktor besitzt `Situation` und ist der einzige Schreiber von Flügen.
  - Spurpunkte werden gebündelt geschrieben: spätestens jede Sekunde, bei 20 Punkten und immer vor `UpdateFlight`.
  - `subscribe` liefert Snapshot und Empfänger atomar aus dem Aktor, ohne Lücke und ohne Doppel.
  - Beim Start werden offene Flüge aus der DB als `ResumeFlight` geladen.
  - Beim Shutdown werden die gepufferten Punkte geschrieben. Offene Flüge bleiben offen und werden beim nächsten Start fortgesetzt (Spec §5).

```rust
//! Laufzeit-Hülle um den reinen Lage-Kern: Aktor, Speicher, Verteilung.

pub mod service;

use std::sync::Arc;

pub type Clock = Arc<dyn Fn() -> i64 + Send + Sync>;

pub fn system_clock() -> Clock {
    Arc::new(crate::time::now_ms)
}
```

`src/runtime/service.rs`, Tests zuerst:

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;

use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, mpsc, oneshot, watch};
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;

use crate::contract::{GatewayState, LiveEvent, SituationSnapshot, StreamState, TrackPoint};
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::situation::observation::Observed;
use crate::situation::state::{Config, Effect, ResumeFlight, Situation};
use crate::store;

use super::Clock;

const BROADCAST_CAPACITY: usize = 1024;
const FLUSH_POINTS_AT: usize = 20;

enum Command {
    Observe(Observed),
    Subscribe(oneshot::Sender<(SituationSnapshot, broadcast::Receiver<LiveEvent>)>),
    Snapshot(oneshot::Sender<SituationSnapshot>),
    Gateway(String, oneshot::Sender<Option<GatewayState>>),
    Stream(String, oneshot::Sender<Option<StreamState>>),
    SetConfig(Config),
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct SituationHandle {
    tx: mpsc::Sender<Command>,
}

fn gone() -> AppError {
    AppError::Unavailable("Lage wird gerade neu gestartet".into())
}

impl SituationHandle {
    pub async fn observe(&self, o: Observed) {
        if self.tx.send(Command::Observe(o)).await.is_err() {
            tracing::debug!("Lage-Aktor beendet – Observation verworfen");
        }
    }

    /// Ein einfacher Kanal für Bausteine, die `runtime` nicht kennen sollen (MQTT-Sitzung). Leitet in
    /// den einen Befehlskanal weiter: Observations bleiben vor späteren Abfragen in der Reihe.
    pub fn observer(&self) -> mpsc::Sender<Observed> {
        let (tx, mut rx) = mpsc::channel::<Observed>(1024);
        let handle = self.clone();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            while let Some(o) = rx.recv().await {
                handle.observe(o).await;
            }
        });
        tx
    }

    async fn ask<T>(&self, make: impl FnOnce(oneshot::Sender<T>) -> Command) -> Result<T, AppError> {
        let (tx, rx) = oneshot::channel();
        self.tx.send(make(tx)).await.map_err(|_| gone())?;
        rx.await.map_err(|_| gone())
    }

    pub async fn subscribe(&self) -> Result<(SituationSnapshot, broadcast::Receiver<LiveEvent>), AppError> {
        self.ask(Command::Subscribe).await
    }

    pub async fn snapshot(&self) -> Result<SituationSnapshot, AppError> {
        self.ask(Command::Snapshot).await
    }

    pub async fn gateway(&self, sn: &str) -> Result<Option<GatewayState>, AppError> {
        let sn = sn.to_string();
        self.ask(|tx| Command::Gateway(sn, tx)).await
    }

    pub async fn stream(&self, sn: &str) -> Result<Option<StreamState>, AppError> {
        let sn = sn.to_string();
        self.ask(|tx| Command::Stream(sn, tx)).await
    }

    pub async fn set_config(&self, config: Config) {
        let _ = self.tx.send(Command::SetConfig(config)).await;
    }
}

pub async fn start(
    pool: SqlitePool,
    config: Config,
    clock: Clock,
    live_requests: mpsc::UnboundedSender<String>,
    shutdown: watch::Receiver<bool>,
) -> Result<(SituationHandle, JoinHandle<()>), sqlx::Error> {
    todo!()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::contract::FlightEndReason;
    use crate::dji::record::{RecordLine, read_lines};
    use crate::dji::translate::Translator;
    use crate::sim::synth::FIXTURE_PATH;
    use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicI64, Ordering};

    fn fixture() -> Vec<RecordLine> {
        let path = std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(FIXTURE_PATH);
        read_lines(std::io::BufReader::new(std::fs::File::open(path).unwrap())).map(Result::unwrap).collect()
    }

    /// Uhr, die der Replay vorstellt – sonst schlösse `tick` den Flug nach Wanduhr.
    fn replay_clock() -> (Clock, Arc<AtomicI64>) {
        let now = Arc::new(AtomicI64::new(0));
        let n = now.clone();
        (Arc::new(move || n.load(Ordering::SeqCst)), now)
    }

    async fn feed(handle: &SituationHandle, now: &AtomicI64, translator: &mut Translator, lines: &[RecordLine]) {
        for line in lines {
            let at = line.at_ms().unwrap();
            now.store(at, Ordering::SeqCst);
            for o in translator.handle(at, &line.topic, line.payload.as_bytes()).observations {
                handle.observe(o).await;
            }
        }
        handle.snapshot().await.unwrap(); // wartet, bis der Aktor alles verarbeitet hat
    }

    async fn run_until_stopped(stop: watch::Sender<bool>, task: JoinHandle<()>) {
        stop.send(true).unwrap();
        task.await.unwrap();
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn replay_stores_one_landed_flight_with_its_track() {
        let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
        let (clock, now) = replay_clock();
        let (live_tx, mut live_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        let (stop, stop_rx) = watch::channel(false);
        let (handle, task) = start(pool.clone(), Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: true }, clock, live_tx, stop_rx).await.unwrap();
        feed(&handle, &now, &mut Translator::new(), &fixture()).await;
        run_until_stopped(stop, task).await;

        let flights = store::flights::list(&pool, 10).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(flights.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(flights[0].end_reason, Some(FlightEndReason::Landed));
        let segments = store::flights::segments(&pool, flights[0].id).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(segments.len(), 2);
        assert!(segments.iter().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>() > 60);
        assert_eq!(live_rx.try_recv().unwrap(), "RCSIM0001");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn restart_mid_flight_continues_same_flight() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let path = dir.path().join("suchlage.db");
        let lines = fixture();
        let half = lines.len() / 2;

        for (part, slice) in [(0, &lines[..half]), (1, &lines[half..])] {
            let pool = crate::db::connect(&path).await.unwrap();
            crate::db::migrate(&pool).await.unwrap();
            let (clock, now) = replay_clock();
            let (live_tx, _live_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
            let (stop, stop_rx) = watch::channel(false);
            let (handle, task) = start(pool.clone(), Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: false }, clock, live_tx, stop_rx).await.unwrap();
            // Ein neuer Prozess hat auch einen neuen Übersetzer (kein Merge-Stand, keine Topologie).
            feed(&handle, &now, &mut Translator::new(), slice).await;
            run_until_stopped(stop, task).await;
            if part == 0 {
                assert_eq!(store::flights::open(&pool).await.unwrap().len(), 1, "Flug läuft beim Herunterfahren");
            }
            pool.close().await;
        }

        let pool = crate::db::connect(&path).await.unwrap();
        let flights = store::flights::list(&pool, 10).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(flights.len(), 1, "kein zweiter Flug nach dem Neustart");
        assert_eq!(flights[0].end_reason, Some(FlightEndReason::Landed));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn subscribe_delivers_snapshot_then_live_events() {
        let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
        let (clock, now) = replay_clock();
        let (live_tx, _) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        let (_stop, stop_rx) = watch::channel(false);
        let (handle, _task) = start(pool, Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: false }, clock, live_tx, stop_rx).await.unwrap();
        let (snapshot, mut rx) = handle.subscribe().await.unwrap();
        assert!(snapshot.gateways.is_empty());
        feed(&handle, &now, &mut Translator::new(), &fixture()[..1]).await;
        assert!(matches!(rx.recv().await.unwrap(), LiveEvent::Gateway { .. }));
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Aktor implementieren**

```rust
struct Actor {
    situation: Situation,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    events: broadcast::Sender<LiveEvent>,
    points: Vec<(i64, u32, TrackPoint)>,
    live_requests: mpsc::UnboundedSender<String>,
    clock: Clock,
}

pub async fn start(
    pool: SqlitePool,
    config: Config,
    clock: Clock,
    live_requests: mpsc::UnboundedSender<String>,
    mut shutdown: watch::Receiver<bool>,
) -> Result<(SituationHandle, JoinHandle<()>), sqlx::Error> {
    let resume: Vec<ResumeFlight> = store::flights::open(&pool)
        .await?
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(flight, segments)| ResumeFlight { flight, segments })
        .collect();
    if !resume.is_empty() {
        tracing::info!(count = resume.len(), "laufende Flüge werden fortgesetzt");
    }
    let next_id = store::flights::next_id(&pool).await?;
    let (events, _) = broadcast::channel(BROADCAST_CAPACITY);
    let mut actor = Actor { situation: Situation::new(config, next_id, resume), pool, events, points: Vec::new(), live_requests, clock };
    let (tx, mut rx) = mpsc::channel::<Command>(4096);

    let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut tick = tokio::time::interval(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));
        tick.set_missed_tick_behavior(tokio::time::MissedTickBehavior::Delay);
        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                cmd = rx.recv() => match cmd {
                    Some(cmd) => actor.handle(cmd).await,
                    None => break,
                },
                _ = tick.tick() => {
                    let now = (actor.clock)();
                    let fx = actor.situation.tick(now);
                    actor.run(fx).await;
                    actor.flush().await;
                }
                _ = shutdown.changed() => break,
            }
        }
        // Erst den Rest der Warteschlange verarbeiten, dann puffern und schließen.
        rx.close();
        while let Ok(cmd) = rx.try_recv() {
            actor.handle(cmd).await;
        }
        actor.flush().await;
        tracing::info!("Lage-Aktor beendet");
    });
    Ok((SituationHandle { tx }, task))
}

impl Actor {
    async fn handle(&mut self, cmd: Command) {
        match cmd {
            Command::Observe(o) => {
                let fx = self.situation.apply(o);
                self.run(fx).await;
            }
            Command::Subscribe(reply) => {
                let rx = self.events.subscribe();
                let _ = reply.send((self.situation.snapshot((self.clock)()), rx));
            }
            Command::Snapshot(reply) => {
                let _ = reply.send(self.situation.snapshot((self.clock)()));
            }
            Command::Gateway(sn, reply) => {
                let _ = reply.send(self.situation.gateway(&sn).cloned());
            }
            Command::Stream(sn, reply) => {
                let _ = reply.send(self.situation.stream(&sn).cloned());
            }
            Command::SetConfig(c) => self.situation.set_config(c),
        }
    }

    async fn run(&mut self, effects: Vec<Effect>) {
        for e in effects {
            match e {
                Effect::Publish(event) => {
                    let _ = self.events.send(event); // ohne Empfänger ist das kein Fehler
                }
                Effect::InsertFlight(f) => {
                    if let Err(err) = store::flights::insert(&self.pool, &f).await {
                        tracing::error!(error = %err, flight = f.id, "Flug nicht gespeichert");
                    }
                }
                Effect::AppendTrackPoint { flight_id, segment, point } => {
                    self.points.push((flight_id, segment, point));
                    if self.points.len() >= FLUSH_POINTS_AT {
                        self.flush().await;
                    }
                }
                Effect::UpdateFlight(f) => {
                    self.flush().await;
                    if let Err(err) = store::flights::update(&self.pool, &f).await {
                        tracing::error!(error = %err, flight = f.id, "Flugende nicht gespeichert");
                    }
                }
                Effect::RequestLiveStart { gateway_sn } => {
                    let _ = self.live_requests.send(gateway_sn);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn flush(&mut self) {
        if self.points.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let points = std::mem::take(&mut self.points);
        let mut by_flight: std::collections::BTreeMap<i64, Vec<(u32, TrackPoint)>> = Default::default();
        for (id, seg, p) in points {
            by_flight.entry(id).or_default().push((seg, p));
        }
        for (id, pts) in by_flight {
            if let Err(err) = store::flights::append_points(&self.pool, id, &pts).await {
                // Verlust einzelner Punkte ist hinnehmbar; der Flug selbst steht schon in der DB.
                tracing::error!(error = %err, flight = id, count = pts.len(), "Spurpunkte nicht gespeichert");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
use axum::extract::{Path, State};
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use axum::routing::{delete, get};
use axum::{Json, Router};

use crate::app::AppState;
use crate::auth::session::Admin;
use crate::contract::{KioskTokenCreate, KioskTokenCreated, KioskTokenView};
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::extract::JsonBody;
use crate::store;
use crate::time::now_ms;

pub fn routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new()
        .route("/kiosk-tokens", get(list_kiosk).post(create_kiosk))
        .route("/kiosk-tokens/{id}", delete(delete_kiosk))
}

async fn list_kiosk(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>) -> Result<Json<Vec<KioskTokenView>>, AppError> {
    Ok(Json(store::kiosk::list(&state.pool).await?))
}

async fn create_kiosk(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>, JsonBody(body): JsonBody<KioskTokenCreate>) -> Result<Json<KioskTokenCreated>, AppError> {
    let label = body.label.trim();
    if label.is_empty() || label.chars().count() > 80 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Bezeichnung: 1 bis 80 Zeichen".into()));
    }
    let (token, secret) = store::kiosk::create(&state.pool, label, now_ms()).await?;
    let url = format!("{}/kiosk#{secret}", state.boot.public_url.as_str().trim_end_matches('/'));
    Ok(Json(KioskTokenCreated { token, url }))
}

async fn delete_kiosk(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<i64>) -> Result<StatusCode, AppError> {
    if store::kiosk::delete(&state.pool, id).await? { Ok(StatusCode::NO_CONTENT) } else { Err(AppError::NotFound) }
}
```

```rust
//! Sitzungscookie und Rollen-Extractors. Rollen: admin ⊃ operator ⊃ viewer; Großbild ist nur viewer.

use axum::extract::FromRequestParts;
use axum::http::request::Parts;
use axum_extra::extract::CookieJar;
use axum_extra::extract::cookie::{Cookie, SameSite};

use crate::app::AppState;
use crate::config::BootConfig;
use crate::contract::Role;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::store::sessions::{self, Principal};
use crate::store::users::User;
use crate::time::now_ms;

pub const COOKIE: &str = "suchlage_sid";

pub fn secure_cookies(boot: &BootConfig) -> bool {
    boot.public_url.scheme() == "https"
}

pub fn session_cookie(token: String, max_age_ms: i64, secure: bool) -> Cookie<'static> {
    Cookie::build((COOKIE, token))
        .path("/")
        .http_only(true)
        .same_site(SameSite::Lax)
        .secure(secure)
        .max_age(time::Duration::milliseconds(max_age_ms))
        .build()
}

async fn principal(parts: &mut Parts, state: &AppState) -> Result<Principal, AppError> {
    todo!()
}

pub struct Viewer(pub Principal);
pub struct Operator(pub User);
pub struct Admin(pub User);

impl FromRequestParts<AppState> for Viewer {
    type Rejection = AppError;
    async fn from_request_parts(parts: &mut Parts, state: &AppState) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        Ok(Viewer(principal(parts, state).await?))
    }
}

impl FromRequestParts<AppState> for Operator {
    type Rejection = AppError;
    async fn from_request_parts(parts: &mut Parts, state: &AppState) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        match principal(parts, state).await? {
            Principal::User(u) => Ok(Operator(u)),
            Principal::Kiosk { .. } => Err(AppError::Forbidden),
        }
    }
}

impl FromRequestParts<AppState> for Admin {
    type Rejection = AppError;
    async fn from_request_parts(parts: &mut Parts, state: &AppState) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        match principal(parts, state).await? {
            Principal::User(u) if u.role == Role::Admin => Ok(Admin(u)),
            _ => Err(AppError::Forbidden),
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Nur mit Feature `dev`: Anmeldung ohne OIDC als „Entwicklung“ (Admin, weil erstes Konto).

use axum::extract::State;
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use axum_extra::extract::CookieJar;

use crate::app::AppState;
use crate::auth::session::{secure_cookies, session_cookie};
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::store::{sessions, users};
use crate::time::now_ms;

pub async fn login(State(state): State<AppState>, jar: CookieJar) -> Result<(CookieJar, StatusCode), AppError> {
    let now = now_ms();
    let user = users::upsert_oidc(&state.pool, "dev", "dev", "Entwicklung", now).await?;
    let token = sessions::create_for_user(&state.pool, user.id, now).await?;
    Ok((jar.add(session_cookie(token, sessions::USER_TTL_MS, secure_cookies(&state.boot))), StatusCode::NO_CONTENT))
}
```

Im `router` vor `.fallback`: `let api = api.merge(crate::web::auth::routes()).merge(crate::web::admin::routes());` (die `.merge`-Aufrufe vor dem `.fallback` des API-Routers).

`run` ersetzen:

```rust
pub async fn run(boot: BootConfig, shutdown: impl Future<Output = ()> + Send + 'static) -> Result<(), std::io::Error> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&boot.data_dir)?;
    let pool = crate::db::connect(&boot.db_path()).await.map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    crate::db::migrate(&pool).await.map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    let (stop_tx, stop_rx) = watch::channel(false);
    // B12 hängt hier den Livebild-Auftragnehmer an; bis dahin werden Anfragen nur geloggt.
    let (live_tx, mut live_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel::<String>();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        while let Some(gw) = live_rx.recv().await {
            tracing::info!(gateway = %gw, "Livebild angefordert (noch ohne Befehlsweg)");
        }
    });
    let config = Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: true };
    let (situation, situation_task) =
        service::start(pool.clone(), config, crate::runtime::system_clock(), live_tx, stop_rx.clone()).await.map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(boot.bind).await?;
    tracing::info!(bind = %boot.bind, public_url = %boot.public_url, "Suchlage läuft");
    let state = AppState { boot: Arc::new(boot), pool: pool.clone(), situation };
    let result = axum::serve(listener, router(state)).with_graceful_shutdown(shutdown).await;
    let _ = stop_tx.send(true);
    let _ = situation_task.await;
    pool.close().await;
    result
}
```

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;

use tokio::sync::{mpsc, watch};

use crate::app::{AppState, router};
use crate::config::BootConfig;
use crate::contract::Role;
use crate::situation::state::Config;
use crate::store::{sessions, users};

pub struct TestApp {
    pub router: Router,
    pub state: AppState,
    pub live_requests: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<String>,
    _stop: watch::Sender<bool>,
}

pub async fn test_app() -> TestApp {
    let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
    let (stop, stop_rx) = watch::channel(false);
    let (live_tx, live_requests) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
    let (situation, _task) = crate::runtime::service::start(pool.clone(), Config { auto_live_on_takeoff: false }, crate::runtime::system_clock(), live_tx, stop_rx)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let boot = BootConfig::from_lookup(|k| (k == "SUCHLAGE_PUBLIC_URL").then(|| "https://suchlage.test".to_string())).unwrap();
    let state = AppState { boot: Arc::new(boot), pool, situation };
    TestApp { router: router(state.clone()), state, live_requests, _stop: stop }
}

impl TestApp {
    /// Legt ein Konto mit der Rolle an und liefert `suchlage_sid=<token>`.
    pub async fn cookie(&self, role: Role) -> String {
        let pool = &self.state.pool;
        if role == Role::Admin && users::count(pool).await.unwrap() > 0 {
            panic!("Admin zuerst anlegen: das erste Konto wird Admin");
        }
        if role == Role::Operator && users::count(pool).await.unwrap() == 0 {
            users::upsert_oidc(pool, "test", "erster", "Admin", 0).await.unwrap();
        }
        let sub = format!("{role:?}-{}", crate::token::generate());
        let user = users::upsert_oidc(pool, "test", &sub, &format!("{role:?}"), 0).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(user.role, role);
        let token = sessions::create_for_user(pool, user.id, crate::time::now_ms()).await.unwrap();
        format!("{}={token}", crate::auth::session::COOKIE)
    }
}

pub fn with_cookie(mut req: Request<Body>, cookie: &str) -> Request<Body> {
    req.headers_mut().insert("cookie", cookie.parse().unwrap());
    req
}
```

```rust
async fn principal(parts: &mut Parts, state: &AppState) -> Result<Principal, AppError> {
    let jar = CookieJar::from_headers(&parts.headers);
    let token = jar.get(COOKIE).map(|c| c.value().to_string()).ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)?;
    sessions::lookup(&state.pool, &token, now_ms()).await?.ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)
}
```

In `web/auth.rs`:

```rust
async fn me(Viewer(principal): Viewer) -> Json<Me> {
    Json(match principal {
        Principal::User(u) => Me { display_name: u.display_name, role: u.role },
        Principal::Kiosk { label, .. } => Me { display_name: label, role: Role::Kiosk },
    })
}

async fn logout(State(state): State<AppState>, jar: CookieJar) -> Result<(CookieJar, StatusCode), AppError> {
    if let Some(c) = jar.get(COOKIE) {
        store::sessions::delete(&state.pool, c.value()).await?;
    }
    Ok((jar.remove(axum_extra::extract::cookie::Cookie::from(COOKIE)), StatusCode::NO_CONTENT))
}

async fn kiosk_enroll(State(state): State<AppState>, jar: CookieJar, JsonBody(body): JsonBody<KioskEnroll>) -> Result<impl IntoResponse, AppError> {
    let Some(token_id) = store::kiosk::find_by_token(&state.pool, body.token.trim()).await? else {
        return Err(AppError::UnauthorizedWith("Zugangslink ungültig oder widerrufen".into()));
    };
    let token = store::sessions::create_for_kiosk(&state.pool, token_id, now_ms()).await?;
    Ok((jar.add(session_cookie(token, store::sessions::KIOSK_TTL_MS, secure_cookies(&state.boot))), StatusCode::NO_CONTENT))
}
```

```rust
//! Live-Strom per SSE (ADR 0001). Nie komprimieren – Cloudflare puffert sonst.

use std::convert::Infallible;
use std::time::Duration;

use axum::extract::State;
use axum::response::sse::{Event, KeepAlive, Sse};
use axum::routing::get;
use axum::{Json, Router};
use futures::Stream;
use tokio::sync::broadcast::{self, error::RecvError};

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use axum::body::Body;
    use axum::http::StatusCode;
    use http_body_util::BodyExt;
    use tower::ServiceExt;

    use crate::contract::Role;
    use crate::situation::observation::{Observation, Observed};
    use crate::testing::{get, test_app, with_cookie};

    async fn next_data(body: &mut Body) -> serde_json::Value {
        loop {
            let frame = tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), body.frame()).await.unwrap().unwrap().unwrap();
            if let Some(bytes) = frame.data_ref() {
                let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes);
                if let Some(data) = text.lines().find_map(|l| l.strip_prefix("data: ")) {
                    return serde_json::from_str(data).unwrap();
                }
            }
        }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn live_starts_with_snapshot_then_streams_events() {
        let app = test_app().await;
        let cookie = app.cookie(Role::Operator).await;
        let res = app.router.clone().oneshot(with_cookie(get("/api/live"), &cookie)).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(res.status(), StatusCode::OK);
        assert_eq!(res.headers()["content-type"], "text/event-stream");
        assert!(res.headers().get("content-encoding").is_none());
        let mut body = res.into_body();
        assert_eq!(next_data(&mut body).await["type"], "snapshot");

        app.state.situation.observe(Observed { at_ms: 1, observation: Observation::Topology { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), aircraft_sn: None } }).await;
        let event = next_data(&mut body).await;
        assert_eq!(event["type"], "gateway");
        assert_eq!(event["gateway"]["sn"], "RC1");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn live_requires_a_session() {
        let app = test_app().await;
        let res = app.router.clone().oneshot(get("/api/live")).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(res.status(), StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED);
    }
}
```

```rust
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn detail_returns_track_segments_and_404_for_unknown() {
        let app = test_app().await;
        let f = examples::flight_landed(3, 1_000);
        store::flights::insert(&app.state.pool, &f).await.unwrap();
        let p = |t| TrackPoint { t_ms: t, lat: 52.0, lon: 10.0, elevation_m: None };
        store::flights::append_points(&app.state.pool, 3, &[(0, p(1_000)), (1, p(9_000))]).await.unwrap();
        let admin = app.cookie(Role::Admin).await;

        let (status, body) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(get("/api/flights/3"), &admin)).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);
        assert_eq!(body["segments"].as_array().unwrap().len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(body["flight"]["end_reason"], "landed");
        assert_eq!(send(&app.router, with_cookie(get("/api/flights/99"), &admin)).await.0, StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);

        let (status, list) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(get("/api/flights"), &admin)).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);
        assert_eq!(list[0]["id"], 3);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implementieren**

```rust
enum Phase {
    Initial(SituationSnapshot, broadcast::Receiver<LiveEvent>),
    Running(broadcast::Receiver<LiveEvent>),
}

fn sse(event: &LiveEvent) -> Result<Event, Infallible> {
    Ok(Event::default().json_data(event).expect("LiveEvent ist serialisierbar"))
}

async fn live(_: Viewer, State(state): State<AppState>) -> Result<Sse<impl Stream<Item = Result<Event, Infallible>>>, AppError> {
    let (snapshot, rx) = state.situation.subscribe().await?;
    let handle: SituationHandle = state.situation.clone();
    // shortcut: ein Abmelden beendet laufende Ströme erst beim nächsten Neuverbinden; reicht für M1.
    let stream = futures::stream::unfold(Some(Phase::Initial(snapshot, rx)), move |phase| {
        let handle = handle.clone();
        async move {
            match phase? {
                Phase::Initial(snapshot, rx) => Some((sse(&LiveEvent::Snapshot { snapshot }), Some(Phase::Running(rx)))),
                Phase::Running(mut rx) => loop {
                    match rx.recv().await {
                        Ok(event) => return Some((sse(&event), Some(Phase::Running(rx)))),
                        Err(RecvError::Lagged(missed)) => {
                            tracing::debug!(missed, "Live-Strom überholt – neuer Snapshot");
                            let (snapshot, fresh) = handle.subscribe().await.ok()?;
                            return Some((sse(&LiveEvent::Snapshot { snapshot }), Some(Phase::Running(fresh))));
                        }
                        Err(RecvError::Closed) => return None,
                    }
                },
            }
        }
    });
    Ok(Sse::new(stream).keep_alive(KeepAlive::new().interval(Duration::from_secs(15))))
}
```

**Strang:** backend

- Create: `src/settings/mod.rs`, `src/auth/setup.rs`
- Modify: `src/auth/mod.rs`, `src/auth/dev.rs` (Seed), `src/web/admin.rs` (Einstellungs-Routen), `src/web/auth.rs` (`options`, `/setup`), `src/app.rs` (State-Feld `settings`, Einrichtungstoken beim Start, Konfigurationsweitergabe an den Aktor), `src/testing.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `store::settings`, `store::users::count` (B6), `Admin` (B8)
- Produces:
  - `settings::{Settings, OidcSettings, DjiSettings, BrokerSettings, SettingsHandle}`
  - `SettingsHandle::load(pool) -> Result<SettingsHandle, sqlx::Error>`, erzeugt `internal_secret` beim ersten Start
  - `SettingsHandle::current() -> Settings`, `subscribe() -> watch::Receiver<Settings>`
  - `SettingsHandle::{update_oidc, update_dji, update_broker, update_media, update_general}` → `Result<(), AppError>`
  - `SettingsHandle::view(&BootConfig) -> SettingsView`
  - `settings::redirect_url(&BootConfig) -> String` = `<public_url>/api/auth/oidc/callback`
  - `auth::setup::{ensure_token(pool, settings, boot, now) -> Option<String>, consume(pool, token, now) -> Result<(), AppError>, required(pool, settings) -> bool}`
  - `auth::dev::seed(&SettingsHandle)` (nur Feature `dev`): Broker `127.0.0.1:1883` mit `suchlage-admin`/`dev-admin` und `rc_url` `tcp://127.0.0.1:1883`; Media unter `127.0.0.1` mit den Ports 8889, 8888, 9996, 1935; DJI `dev`/`dev`/`dev` mit der Workspace-ID aus M0. Gesetzt wird nur, was fehlt.
  - Routen `GET /api/settings`, `PUT /api/settings/{oidc|dji|broker|media|general}`, `POST /api/setup`
  - `AppState.settings: SettingsHandle`

**Validierung (400 mit deutscher Meldung):**
- Issuer `https://…`, auf Loopback auch `http://`.
- `client_id`, `app_id`, `admin_username` und `host` nicht leer.
- `rc_url` beginnt mit `tcp://`, `rtmp_url` mit `rtmp://`. `webrtc_url`, `hls_url` und `playback_url` beginnen mit `http://` oder `https://`.
- `workspace_id` ist eine UUID (36 Zeichen, Bindestriche an den Stellen 8, 13, 18 und 23).
- `port` liegt zwischen 1 und 65535.
- Ein leeres oder `null`-Geheimnis behält den gespeicherten Wert. Fehlt ein Geheimnis beim ersten Speichern, ist das ein Fehler.

```rust
//! Fachliche Einstellungen leben in der DB (ADR 0002). Geheimnisse verlassen den Server nie –
//! die Sicht (`SettingsView`) meldet nur `has_*`.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tokio::sync::watch;

use crate::config::BootConfig;
use crate::contract::*;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::store;
use crate::time::now_ms;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OidcSettings {
    pub issuer: String,
    pub client_id: String,
    pub client_secret: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DjiSettings {
    pub app_id: String,
    pub app_key: String,
    pub license: String,
    pub workspace_id: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BrokerSettings {
    pub host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub admin_username: String,
    pub admin_password: String,
    pub rc_url: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Settings {
    pub oidc: Option<OidcSettings>,
    pub dji: Option<DjiSettings>,
    pub broker: Option<BrokerSettings>,
    pub media: Option<MediaSettings>,
    pub general: GeneralSettings,
    /// Geheimnis des internen MediaMTX-Lesers (ADR 0003); beim ersten Start erzeugt.
    pub internal_secret: String,
}

pub fn default_general() -> GeneralSettings {
    GeneralSettings { auto_live_on_takeoff: true, record_raw_mqtt: true }
}

pub fn redirect_url(boot: &BootConfig) -> String {
    format!("{}/api/auth/oidc/callback", boot.public_url.as_str().trim_end_matches('/'))
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct SettingsHandle {
    pool: SqlitePool,
    tx: watch::Sender<Settings>,
}

fn invalid(msg: &str) -> AppError {
    AppError::Validation(msg.into())
}

fn non_empty(v: &str, field: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let v = v.trim();
    if v.is_empty() { Err(invalid(&format!("{field} darf nicht leer sein"))) } else { Ok(v.to_string()) }
}

fn with_scheme(v: &str, schemes: &[&str], field: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let v = non_empty(v, field)?;
    if schemes.iter().any(|s| v.starts_with(&format!("{s}://"))) {
        Ok(v.trim_end_matches('/').to_string())
    } else {
        Err(invalid(&format!("{field} muss mit {}:// beginnen", schemes.join(":// oder "))))
    }
}

fn is_uuid(v: &str) -> bool {
    v.len() == 36 && v.char_indices().all(|(i, c)| if [8, 13, 18, 23].contains(&i) { c == '-' } else { c.is_ascii_hexdigit() })
}

pub fn issuer_allowed(issuer: &str) -> bool {
    let Ok(url) = url::Url::parse(issuer) else { return false };
    match url.scheme() {
        "https" => true,
        "http" => matches!(url.host(), Some(url::Host::Domain("localhost"))) || url.host().is_some_and(|h| matches!(h, url::Host::Ipv4(ip) if ip.is_loopback())),
        _ => false,
    }
}

/// Neues Geheimnis oder das gespeicherte; beim ersten Speichern Pflicht.
fn secret(input: Option<String>, stored: Option<&String>, field: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    match input.map(|s| s.trim().to_string()).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) {
        Some(s) => Ok(s),
        None => stored.cloned().ok_or_else(|| invalid(&format!("{field} fehlt"))),
    }
}

impl SettingsHandle {
    pub async fn load(pool: SqlitePool) -> Result<Self, sqlx::Error> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub fn current(&self) -> Settings {
        self.tx.borrow().clone()
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> watch::Receiver<Settings> {
        self.tx.subscribe()
    }

    async fn store<T: Serialize>(&self, key: &str, value: &T, apply: impl FnOnce(&mut Settings)) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        store::settings::put(&self.pool, key, value, now_ms()).await?;
        self.tx.send_modify(apply);
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn update_oidc(&self, input: OidcSettingsInput) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn update_dji(&self, input: DjiSettingsInput) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn update_broker(&self, input: BrokerSettingsInput) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn update_media(&self, input: MediaSettings) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn update_general(&self, input: GeneralSettings) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        self.store("general", &input, |s| s.general = input.clone()).await
    }

    pub fn view(&self, boot: &BootConfig) -> SettingsView {
        let s = self.current();
        SettingsView {
            oidc: s.oidc.map(|o| OidcSettingsView { issuer: o.issuer, client_id: o.client_id, has_client_secret: !o.client_secret.is_empty(), redirect_url: redirect_url(boot) }),
            dji: s.dji.map(|d| DjiSettingsView { app_id: d.app_id, has_app_key: !d.app_key.is_empty(), has_license: !d.license.is_empty(), workspace_id: d.workspace_id }),
            broker: s.broker.map(|b| BrokerSettingsView { host: b.host, port: b.port, admin_username: b.admin_username, has_admin_password: !b.admin_password.is_empty(), rc_url: b.rc_url }),
            media: s.media,
            general: s.general,
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn secrets_are_kept_when_omitted_and_never_shown() {
        let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
        let h = SettingsHandle::load(pool.clone()).await.unwrap();
        let input = |secret: Option<&str>| OidcSettingsInput { issuer: "https://id.example.org".into(), client_id: "suchlage".into(), client_secret: secret.map(Into::into) };
        assert!(matches!(h.update_oidc(input(None)).await, Err(AppError::Validation(_))), "erstes Speichern braucht das Geheimnis");
        h.update_oidc(input(Some("geheim"))).await.unwrap();
        h.update_oidc(input(None)).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(h.current().oidc.unwrap().client_secret, "geheim");

        let boot = BootConfig::from_lookup(|_| None).unwrap();
        let json = serde_json::to_string(&h.view(&boot)).unwrap();
        assert!(!json.contains("geheim"));
        assert!(json.contains("/api/auth/oidc/callback"));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn settings_survive_a_reload_and_internal_secret_is_stable() {
        let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
        let h = SettingsHandle::load(pool.clone()).await.unwrap();
        h.update_general(GeneralSettings { auto_live_on_takeoff: false, record_raw_mqtt: true }).await.unwrap();
        let secret = h.current().internal_secret;
        assert_eq!(secret.len(), 64);
        let again = SettingsHandle::load(pool).await.unwrap();
        assert!(!again.current().general.auto_live_on_takeoff);
        assert_eq!(again.current().internal_secret, secret);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn invalid_values_are_rejected_with_a_reason() {
        let h = SettingsHandle::load(crate::db::test_pool().await).await.unwrap();
        let err = h.update_oidc(OidcSettingsInput { issuer: "http://id.example.org".into(), client_id: "x".into(), client_secret: Some("s".into()) }).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(err.to_string().contains("https"), "{err}");
        let err = h.update_broker(BrokerSettingsInput { host: "mqtt".into(), port: 1883, admin_username: "a".into(), admin_password: Some("p".into()), rc_url: "mqtt://x".into() }).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(err.to_string().contains("tcp://"), "{err}");
        let err = h.update_dji(DjiSettingsInput { app_id: "1".into(), app_key: Some("k".into()), license: Some("l".into()), workspace_id: "keine-uuid".into() }).await.unwrap_err();
        assert!(err.to_string().contains("Workspace"), "{err}");
        assert!(issuer_allowed("http://127.0.0.1:9000"), "Loopback für Tests und Dev");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn changes_are_broadcast() {
        let h = SettingsHandle::load(crate::db::test_pool().await).await.unwrap();
        let mut rx = h.subscribe();
        h.update_general(GeneralSettings { auto_live_on_takeoff: false, record_raw_mqtt: false }).await.unwrap();
        rx.changed().await.unwrap();
        assert!(!rx.borrow().general.record_raw_mqtt);
    }
}
```

```rust
    pub async fn load(pool: SqlitePool) -> Result<Self, sqlx::Error> {
        let internal_secret = match store::settings::get::<String>(&pool, "internal_secret").await? {
            Some(s) => s,
            None => {
                let s = crate::token::generate();
                store::settings::put(&pool, "internal_secret", &s, now_ms()).await?;
                s
            }
        };
        let settings = Settings {
            oidc: store::settings::get(&pool, "oidc").await?,
            dji: store::settings::get(&pool, "dji").await?,
            broker: store::settings::get(&pool, "broker").await?,
            media: store::settings::get(&pool, "media").await?,
            general: store::settings::get(&pool, "general").await?.unwrap_or_else(default_general),
            internal_secret,
        };
        Ok(Self { pool, tx: watch::Sender::new(settings) })
    }

    pub async fn update_oidc(&self, input: OidcSettingsInput) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let issuer = non_empty(&input.issuer, "Issuer")?.trim_end_matches('/').to_string();
        if !issuer_allowed(&issuer) {
            return Err(invalid("Issuer muss mit https:// beginnen (http nur für localhost)"));
        }
        let current = self.current().oidc;
        let value = OidcSettings {
            issuer,
            client_id: non_empty(&input.client_id, "Client-ID")?,
            client_secret: secret(input.client_secret, current.as_ref().map(|c| &c.client_secret), "Client-Secret")?,
        };
        self.store("oidc", &value, |s| s.oidc = Some(value.clone())).await
    }

    pub async fn update_dji(&self, input: DjiSettingsInput) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let workspace_id = input.workspace_id.trim().to_lowercase();
        if !is_uuid(&workspace_id) {
            return Err(invalid("Workspace-ID muss eine UUID sein"));
        }
        let current = self.current().dji;
        let value = DjiSettings {
            app_id: non_empty(&input.app_id, "App-ID")?,
            app_key: secret(input.app_key, current.as_ref().map(|c| &c.app_key), "App-Key")?,
            license: secret(input.license, current.as_ref().map(|c| &c.license), "Lizenz")?,
            workspace_id,
        };
        self.store("dji", &value, |s| s.dji = Some(value.clone())).await
    }

    pub async fn update_broker(&self, input: BrokerSettingsInput) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        if input.port == 0 {
            return Err(invalid("Port muss zwischen 1 und 65535 liegen"));
        }
        let current = self.current().broker;
        let value = BrokerSettings {
            host: non_empty(&input.host, "Broker-Adresse")?,
            port: input.port,
            admin_username: non_empty(&input.admin_username, "Admin-Benutzer")?,
            admin_password: secret(input.admin_password, current.as_ref().map(|c| &c.admin_password), "Admin-Passwort")?,
            rc_url: with_scheme(&input.rc_url, &["tcp"], "Adresse für die Fernsteuerung")?,
        };
        self.store("broker", &value, |s| s.broker = Some(value.clone())).await
    }

    pub async fn update_media(&self, input: MediaSettings) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        let value = MediaSettings {
            webrtc_url: with_scheme(&input.webrtc_url, &["http", "https"], "WebRTC-Adresse")?,
            hls_url: with_scheme(&input.hls_url, &["http", "https"], "HLS-Adresse")?,
            playback_url: with_scheme(&input.playback_url, &["http", "https"], "Playback-Adresse")?,
            rtmp_url: with_scheme(&input.rtmp_url, &["rtmp"], "RTMP-Adresse")?,
        };
        self.store("media", &value, |s| s.media = Some(value.clone())).await
    }
```

```rust
        let link = ensure_token(&pool, &settings, &boot, 0).await.unwrap().unwrap();
        let tok = link.split_once("/einrichtung#").unwrap().1.to_string();
        assert!(matches!(consume(&pool, "falsch", 1).await, Err(AppError::UnauthorizedWith(_))));
        assert!(matches!(consume(&pool, &tok, TTL_MS + 1).await, Err(AppError::UnauthorizedWith(_))), "abgelaufen");
        let link = ensure_token(&pool, &settings, &boot, TTL_MS + 2).await.unwrap().unwrap();
        let tok = link.split_once("/einrichtung#").unwrap().1.to_string();
        consume(&pool, &tok, TTL_MS + 3).await.unwrap();
        assert!(consume(&pool, &tok, TTL_MS + 4).await.is_err(), "nur einmal");
    }
}
```

`auth/setup.rs`:

```rust
pub async fn ensure_token(pool: &SqlitePool, settings: &SettingsHandle, boot: &BootConfig, now_ms: i64) -> Result<Option<String>, AppError> {
    if !required(pool, settings).await? {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let secret = token::generate();
    store::settings::put(pool, "setup_token", &Stored { hash: token::hash(&secret), expires_at_ms: now_ms + TTL_MS }, now_ms).await?;
    Ok(Some(format!("{}/einrichtung#{secret}", boot.public_url.as_str().trim_end_matches('/'))))
}

pub async fn consume(pool: &SqlitePool, presented: &str, now_ms: i64) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let rejected = || AppError::UnauthorizedWith("Einrichtungslink ungültig oder abgelaufen – Server neu starten für einen neuen Link".into());
    let stored: Stored = store::settings::get(pool, "setup_token").await?.ok_or_else(rejected)?;
    if stored.expires_at_ms <= now_ms || stored.hash != token::hash(presented.trim()) {
        return Err(rejected());
    }
    sqlx::query("DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = 'setup_token'").execute(pool).await?;
    Ok(())
}
```

```rust
use axum::routing::put;
use crate::contract::{BrokerSettingsInput, DjiSettingsInput, GeneralSettings, MediaSettings, OidcSettingsInput, SettingsView};

// in routes():
        .route("/settings", get(settings_view))
        .route("/settings/{section}", put(settings_update))

async fn settings_view(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>) -> Json<SettingsView> {
    Json(state.settings.view(&state.boot))
}

async fn settings_update(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>, Path(section): Path<String>, JsonBody(body): JsonBody<serde_json::Value>) -> Result<Json<SettingsView>, AppError> {
    fn parse<T: serde::de::DeserializeOwned>(v: serde_json::Value) -> Result<T, AppError> {
        serde_json::from_value(v).map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("Ungültige Anfrage: {e}")))
    }
    let s = &state.settings;
    match section.as_str() {
        "oidc" => s.update_oidc(parse::<OidcSettingsInput>(body)?).await?,
        "dji" => s.update_dji(parse::<DjiSettingsInput>(body)?).await?,
        "broker" => s.update_broker(parse::<BrokerSettingsInput>(body)?).await?,
        "media" => s.update_media(parse::<MediaSettings>(body)?).await?,
        "general" => s.update_general(parse::<GeneralSettings>(body)?).await?,
        _ => return Err(AppError::NotFound),
    }
    Ok(Json(s.view(&state.boot)))
}
```

```rust
async fn options(State(state): State<AppState>) -> Result<Json<AuthOptions>, AppError> {
    Ok(Json(AuthOptions {
        oidc: state.settings.current().oidc.is_some(),
        dev_login: cfg!(feature = "dev"),
        setup_required: crate::auth::setup::required(&state.pool, &state.settings).await?,
        redirect_url: crate::settings::redirect_url(&state.boot),
    }))
}

async fn setup(State(state): State<AppState>, JsonBody(body): JsonBody<SetupRequest>) -> Result<Json<SetupResult>, AppError> {
    if !crate::auth::setup::required(&state.pool, &state.settings).await? {
        return Err(AppError::Conflict("Suchlage ist bereits eingerichtet".into()));
    }
    let input = OidcSettingsInput { issuer: body.issuer, client_id: body.client_id, client_secret: Some(body.client_secret) };
    // Erst prüfen, dann den Token verbrauchen: ein Tippfehler im Issuer kostet keinen neuen Link.
    crate::settings::validate_oidc(&input)?;
    // B11 ergänzt hier: state.oidc.check_discovery(input.issuer.trim()).await?;
    crate::auth::setup::consume(&state.pool, &body.token, now_ms()).await?;
    state.settings.update_oidc(input).await?;
    Ok(Json(SetupResult { login_url: "/api/auth/oidc/start?next=/".into() }))
}
```

```rust
pub fn validate_oidc(input: &OidcSettingsInput) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if !issuer_allowed(input.issuer.trim()) {
        return Err(invalid("Issuer muss mit https:// beginnen (http nur für localhost)"));
    }
    non_empty(&input.client_id, "Client-ID")?;
    if input.client_secret.as_deref().is_none_or(|s| s.trim().is_empty()) {
        return Err(invalid("Client-Secret fehlt"));
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

### Task B11: OIDC-Anmeldung mit Pocket ID

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/auth/oidc.rs`, `tests/fixtures/oidc/test-rsa.pem` (nur Testschlüssel)
- Modify: `src/auth/mod.rs`, `src/web/auth.rs` (Routen `oidc/start`, `oidc/callback`; Discovery-Prüfung in `/setup`), `src/app.rs` (State-Feld `oidc: Arc<OidcState>`), `src/testing.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `settings::{SettingsHandle, OidcSettings, redirect_url, issuer_allowed}` (B10), `store::{users, sessions}` (B6), `session_cookie` (B8)
- Produces:
  - `auth::oidc::OidcState::new()`
  - `OidcState::check_discovery(&self, issuer: &str) -> Result<(), AppError>`
  - `OidcState::begin(&self, cfg: &OidcSettings, redirect_url: &str, next: String) -> Result<(url::Url, String), AppError>`; der zweite Wert ist das CSRF-State für das Bindungscookie
  - `OidcState::finish(&self, cfg, redirect_url, state: &str, code: &str) -> Result<(Identity, String), AppError>`; der zweite Wert ist `next`
  - `auth::oidc::{Identity { issuer, subject, display_name }, safe_next(&str) -> String, STATE_COOKIE}`
  - `GET /api/auth/oidc/start?next=/pfad` → 303 zum IdP und Cookie `suchlage_oidc`
  - `GET /api/auth/oidc/callback?state&code` → 303 auf `next` mit Sitzung. Bei jedem Fehler folgt 303 auf `/anmelden?fehler=oidc`, ohne Details; die gehen ins Log.
