```rust
//! OIDC (Authorization Code + PKCE, State, Nonce) gegen Pocket ID. Muster aus Lifeline
//! `auth/oidc`, aber mit Einstellungen aus der DB statt Env und ohne OnceLock-Globals.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use futures::future::BoxFuture;
use openidconnect::core::{CoreAuthenticationFlow, CoreClient, CoreProviderMetadata};
use openidconnect::{
    AuthorizationCode, ClientId, ClientSecret, CsrfToken, EndpointMaybeSet, EndpointNotSet, EndpointSet, HttpRequest,
    HttpResponse, IssuerUrl, Nonce, PkceCodeChallenge, PkceCodeVerifier, RedirectUrl, Scope, TokenResponse,
};

use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::settings::{OidcSettings, issuer_allowed};

pub const STATE_COOKIE: &str = "suchlage_oidc";
const PENDING_TTL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(600);
const PENDING_MAX: usize = 200;
const DISCOVERY_TTL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3600);

type Client = CoreClient<EndpointSet, EndpointNotSet, EndpointNotSet, EndpointNotSet, EndpointMaybeSet, EndpointMaybeSet>;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Identity {
    pub issuer: String,
    pub subject: String,
    pub display_name: String,
}

struct Pending {
    nonce: String,
    verifier: String,
    next: String,
    created: Instant,
}

pub struct OidcState {
    pending: Mutex<HashMap<String, Pending>>,
    discovery: tokio::sync::Mutex<Option<(String, CoreProviderMetadata, Instant)>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("{0}")]
pub struct HttpError(String);

/// reqwest als `AsyncHttpClient`: keine Redirects (SSRF), Zeitgrenzen, https außer auf Loopback.
struct Http(reqwest::Client);

impl<'c> openidconnect::AsyncHttpClient<'c> for Http {
    type Error = HttpError;
    type Future = BoxFuture<'c, Result<HttpResponse, HttpError>>;

    fn call(&'c self, request: HttpRequest) -> Self::Future {
        Box::pin(async move {
            let (parts, body) = request.into_parts();
            let res = self.0.request(parts.method, parts.uri.to_string()).headers(parts.headers).body(body).send().await.map_err(|e| HttpError(e.to_string()))?;
            let status = res.status();
            let headers = res.headers().clone();
            let bytes = res.bytes().await.map_err(|e| HttpError(e.to_string()))?;
            let mut builder = openidconnect::http::Response::builder().status(status);
            if let Some(h) = builder.headers_mut() {
                *h = headers;
            }
            builder.body(bytes.to_vec()).map_err(|e| HttpError(e.to_string()))
        })
    }
}

fn http_for(issuer: &str) -> Http {
    let loopback = issuer.starts_with("http://");
    Http(
        reqwest::Client::builder()
            .redirect(reqwest::redirect::Policy::none())
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(15))
            .connect_timeout(Duration::from_secs(8))
            .https_only(!loopback)
            .build()
            .expect("HTTP-Client baubar"),
    )
}

fn unavailable() -> AppError {
    AppError::Unavailable("Anmeldedienst nicht erreichbar".into())
}

/// Nur lokale Pfade als Rücksprungziel, nie `//host` oder absolute URLs.
pub fn safe_next(next: &str) -> String {
    if next.starts_with('/') && !next.starts_with("//") && !next.contains('\\') { next.to_string() } else { "/".into() }
}

impl Default for OidcState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl OidcState {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { pending: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()), discovery: tokio::sync::Mutex::new(None) }
    }

    async fn metadata(&self, issuer: &str) -> Result<CoreProviderMetadata, AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn check_discovery(&self, issuer: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
        self.metadata(issuer.trim_end_matches('/')).await.map(|_| ())
    }

    async fn client(&self, cfg: &OidcSettings, redirect_url: &str) -> Result<Client, AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn begin(&self, cfg: &OidcSettings, redirect_url: &str, next: String) -> Result<(url::Url, String), AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn finish(&self, cfg: &OidcSettings, redirect_url: &str, state: &str, code: &str) -> Result<(Identity, String), AppError> {
        todo!()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
pub mod test_idp {
    //! Minimaler OIDC-Anbieter für Tests: Discovery, JWKS, Token-Endpunkt mit RS256-ID-Token.

    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

    use axum::extract::State;
    use axum::routing::{get, post};
    use axum::{Json, Router};
    use openidconnect::core::{CoreIdToken, CoreIdTokenClaims, CoreJsonWebKeySet, CoreJwsSigningAlgorithm, CoreRsaPrivateSigningKey};
    use openidconnect::{Audience, EmptyAdditionalClaims, EndUserUsername, IssuerUrl, JsonWebKeyId, Nonce, PrivateSigningKey, StandardClaims, SubjectIdentifier};

    #[derive(Clone)]
    pub struct TestIdp {
        pub issuer: String,
        pub nonce: Arc<Mutex<String>>,
        key: Arc<CoreRsaPrivateSigningKey>,
    }

    pub async fn start(client_id: &'static str) -> TestIdp {
        let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
        let issuer = format!("http://{}", listener.local_addr().unwrap());
        let pem = std::fs::read_to_string(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/tests/fixtures/oidc/test-rsa.pem")).unwrap();
        let key = CoreRsaPrivateSigningKey::from_pem(&pem, Some(JsonWebKeyId::new("test".into()))).unwrap();
        let idp = TestIdp { issuer: issuer.clone(), nonce: Arc::new(Mutex::new(String::new())), key: Arc::new(key) };
        let app = Router::new()
            .route("/.well-known/openid-configuration", get(discovery))
            .route("/jwks", get(jwks))
            .route("/token", post(move |s: State<TestIdp>| token(s, client_id)))
            .with_state(idp.clone());
        tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap() });
        idp
    }

    async fn discovery(State(idp): State<TestIdp>) -> Json<serde_json::Value> {
        let i = &idp.issuer;
        Json(serde_json::json!({
            "issuer": i, "authorization_endpoint": format!("{i}/authorize"), "token_endpoint": format!("{i}/token"),
            "jwks_uri": format!("{i}/jwks"), "response_types_supported": ["code"], "subject_types_supported": ["public"],
            "id_token_signing_alg_values_supported": ["RS256"]
        }))
    }

    async fn jwks(State(idp): State<TestIdp>) -> Json<CoreJsonWebKeySet> {
        Json(CoreJsonWebKeySet::new(vec![idp.key.as_verification_key()]))
    }

    async fn token(State(idp): State<TestIdp>, client_id: &'static str) -> Json<serde_json::Value> {
        let now = chrono::Utc::now();
        let claims = CoreIdTokenClaims::new(
            IssuerUrl::new(idp.issuer.clone()).unwrap(),
            vec![Audience::new(client_id.into())],
            now + chrono::Duration::minutes(5),
            now,
            StandardClaims::new(SubjectIdentifier::new("sub-1".into())).set_preferred_username(Some(EndUserUsername::new("ruben".into()))),
            EmptyAdditionalClaims {},
        )
        .set_nonce(Some(Nonce::new(idp.nonce.lock().unwrap().clone())));
        let id_token = CoreIdToken::new(claims, idp.key.as_ref(), CoreJwsSigningAlgorithm::RsaSsaPkcs1V15Sha256, None, None).unwrap();
        Json(serde_json::json!({ "access_token": "at", "token_type": "Bearer", "expires_in": 300, "id_token": id_token.to_string() }))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn cfg(issuer: &str) -> OidcSettings {
        OidcSettings { issuer: issuer.into(), client_id: "suchlage".into(), client_secret: "secret".into() }
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn code_flow_with_pkce_and_nonce_yields_identity() {
        let idp = test_idp::start("suchlage").await;
        let oidc = OidcState::new();
        let redirect = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/auth/oidc/callback";
        let (url, state) = oidc.begin(&cfg(&idp.issuer), redirect, "/flights".into()).await.unwrap();
        let q: HashMap<_, _> = url.query_pairs().into_owned().collect();
        assert_eq!(q["state"], state);
        assert_eq!(q["code_challenge_method"], "S256");
        *idp.nonce.lock().unwrap() = q["nonce"].clone();

        let (identity, next) = oidc.finish(&cfg(&idp.issuer), redirect, &state, "code-1").await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(identity, Identity { issuer: idp.issuer.clone(), subject: "sub-1".into(), display_name: "ruben".into() });
        assert_eq!(next, "/flights");
        assert!(oidc.finish(&cfg(&idp.issuer), redirect, &state, "code-1").await.is_err(), "State nur einmal");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn wrong_nonce_is_rejected() {
        let idp = test_idp::start("suchlage").await;
        let oidc = OidcState::new();
        let redirect = "http://127.0.0.1:8080/api/auth/oidc/callback";
        let (_, state) = oidc.begin(&cfg(&idp.issuer), redirect, "/".into()).await.unwrap();
        *idp.nonce.lock().unwrap() = "fremd".into();
        assert!(oidc.finish(&cfg(&idp.issuer), redirect, &state, "c").await.is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn next_only_allows_local_paths() {
        assert_eq!(safe_next("/flights/3"), "/flights/3");
        assert_eq!(safe_next("//evil.example"), "/");
        assert_eq!(safe_next("https://evil.example"), "/");
        assert_eq!(safe_next("/\\evil"), "/");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: OIDC implementieren**

```rust
    async fn metadata(&self, issuer: &str) -> Result<CoreProviderMetadata, AppError> {
        if !issuer_allowed(issuer) {
            return Err(AppError::Validation("Issuer muss mit https:// beginnen (http nur für localhost)".into()));
        }
        let mut cache = self.discovery.lock().await;
        if let Some((cached_issuer, meta, at)) = cache.as_ref()
            && cached_issuer == issuer
            && at.elapsed() < DISCOVERY_TTL
        {
            return Ok(meta.clone());
        }
        let url = IssuerUrl::new(issuer.to_string()).map_err(|_| AppError::Validation("Issuer ist keine gültige URL".into()))?;
        let meta = CoreProviderMetadata::discover_async(url, &http_for(issuer)).await.map_err(|e| {
            tracing::warn!(error = %e, issuer, "OIDC-Discovery fehlgeschlagen");
            AppError::Unavailable("Anmeldedienst nicht erreichbar oder Issuer falsch".into())
        })?;
        *cache = Some((issuer.to_string(), meta.clone(), Instant::now()));
        Ok(meta)
    }

    async fn client(&self, cfg: &OidcSettings, redirect_url: &str) -> Result<Client, AppError> {
        let meta = self.metadata(&cfg.issuer).await?;
        let redirect = RedirectUrl::new(redirect_url.to_string()).map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;
        Ok(CoreClient::from_provider_metadata(meta, ClientId::new(cfg.client_id.clone()), Some(ClientSecret::new(cfg.client_secret.clone())))
            .set_redirect_uri(redirect))
    }

    pub async fn begin(&self, cfg: &OidcSettings, redirect_url: &str, next: String) -> Result<(url::Url, String), AppError> {
        let client = self.client(cfg, redirect_url).await?;
        let (challenge, verifier) = PkceCodeChallenge::new_random_sha256();
        let (url, csrf, nonce) = client
            .authorize_url(CoreAuthenticationFlow::AuthorizationCode, CsrfToken::new_random, Nonce::new_random)
            .add_scope(Scope::new("profile".into()))
            .set_pkce_challenge(challenge)
            .url();
        let mut pending = self.pending.lock().unwrap();
        pending.retain(|_, p| p.created.elapsed() < PENDING_TTL);
        if pending.len() >= PENDING_MAX {
            return Err(AppError::Unavailable("Zu viele offene Anmeldungen – bitte gleich erneut versuchen".into()));
        }
        pending.insert(csrf.secret().clone(), Pending { nonce: nonce.secret().clone(), verifier: verifier.secret().clone(), next: safe_next(&next), created: Instant::now() });
        let url = url::Url::parse(url.as_str()).map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;
        Ok((url, csrf.secret().clone()))
    }

    pub async fn finish(&self, cfg: &OidcSettings, redirect_url: &str, state: &str, code: &str) -> Result<(Identity, String), AppError> {
        let pending = {
            let mut map = self.pending.lock().unwrap();
            map.remove(state).filter(|p| p.created.elapsed() < PENDING_TTL)
        }
        .ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)?;
        let client = self.client(cfg, redirect_url).await?;
        let token = client
            .exchange_code(AuthorizationCode::new(code.to_string()))
            .map_err(|_| unavailable())?
            .set_pkce_verifier(PkceCodeVerifier::new(pending.verifier))
            .request_async(&http_for(&cfg.issuer))
            .await
            .map_err(|e| {
                tracing::warn!(error = %e, "OIDC-Token-Austausch fehlgeschlagen");
                unavailable()
            })?;
        let id_token = token.id_token().ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)?;
        let claims = id_token.claims(&client.id_token_verifier(), &Nonce::new(pending.nonce)).map_err(|e| {
            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "ID-Token abgelehnt");
            AppError::Unauthorized
        })?;
        let display_name = claims
            .preferred_username()
            .map(|u| u.to_string())
            .or_else(|| claims.name().and_then(|n| n.get(None)).map(|n| n.to_string()))
            .or_else(|| claims.email().map(|e| e.to_string()))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| claims.subject().to_string());
        Ok((Identity { issuer: claims.issuer().to_string(), subject: claims.subject().to_string(), display_name }, pending.next))
    }
```

`web/auth.rs`, Routen `.route("/auth/oidc/start", get(oidc_start)).route("/auth/oidc/callback", get(oidc_callback))`:

```rust
use axum::extract::Query;
use axum::response::Redirect;
use axum_extra::extract::cookie::{Cookie, SameSite};

use crate::auth::oidc::STATE_COOKIE;

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct StartQuery {
    next: Option<String>,
}

async fn oidc_start(State(state): State<AppState>, jar: CookieJar, Query(q): Query<StartQuery>) -> Result<(CookieJar, Redirect), AppError> {
    let cfg = state.settings.current().oidc.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unavailable("Anmeldung ist noch nicht eingerichtet".into()))?;
    let redirect = crate::settings::redirect_url(&state.boot);
    let (url, csrf) = state.oidc.begin(&cfg, &redirect, q.next.unwrap_or_else(|| "/".into())).await?;
    let cookie = Cookie::build((STATE_COOKIE, csrf))
        .path("/api/auth/oidc")
        .http_only(true)
        .same_site(SameSite::Lax)
        .secure(secure_cookies(&state.boot))
        .max_age(time::Duration::minutes(10))
        .build();
    Ok((jar.add(cookie), Redirect::to(url.as_str())))
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct CallbackQuery {
    state: Option<String>,
    code: Option<String>,
}

async fn oidc_callback(State(state): State<AppState>, jar: CookieJar, Query(q): Query<CallbackQuery>) -> (CookieJar, Redirect) {
    let jar_out = jar.clone().remove(Cookie::build(STATE_COOKIE).path("/api/auth/oidc").build());
    match complete_login(&state, &jar, q).await {
        Ok((token, next)) => (jar_out.add(session_cookie(token, store::sessions::USER_TTL_MS, secure_cookies(&state.boot))), Redirect::to(&next)),
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "OIDC-Anmeldung abgebrochen");
            (jar_out, Redirect::to("/anmelden?fehler=oidc"))
        }
    }
}

async fn complete_login(state: &AppState, jar: &CookieJar, q: CallbackQuery) -> Result<(String, String), AppError> {
    let (Some(csrf), Some(code)) = (q.state, q.code) else { return Err(AppError::Unauthorized) };
    // Bindet den Rücksprung an den Browser, der die Anmeldung begonnen hat (Session-Fixation).
    if jar.get(STATE_COOKIE).map(|c| c.value()) != Some(csrf.as_str()) {
        return Err(AppError::Unauthorized);
    }
    let cfg = state.settings.current().oidc.ok_or(AppError::Unauthorized)?;
    let (identity, next) = state.oidc.finish(&cfg, &crate::settings::redirect_url(&state.boot), &csrf, &code).await?;
    let now = now_ms();
    let user = store::users::upsert_oidc(&state.pool, &identity.issuer, &identity.subject, &identity.display_name, now).await?;
    Ok((store::sessions::create_for_user(&state.pool, user.id, now).await?, next))
}
```

```rust
        let res = tower::ServiceExt::oneshot(app.router.clone(), get(&format!("/api/auth/oidc/callback?state={st}&code=c"))).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(res.headers()["location"], "/anmelden?fehler=oidc");
    }
```

### Task B12: MQTT-Sitzung, update_topo, Rohmitschnitt, Gerätezustand

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/mqtt.rs` (Anfrage/Antwort-Kanal zur Sitzung, ohne DJI), `src/dji/link.rs` (reiner Posteingang), `src/dji/session.rs` (rumqttc-Schleife), `src/dji/recorder.rs`
- Modify: `src/dji/mod.rs`, `src/lib.rs` (`pub mod mqtt;`), `src/app.rs` (Sitzung starten, `AppState.mqtt`), `src/testing.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Translator` inkl. `persisted`/`restore` (B4), `SituationHandle::observer` (B7), `store::device_state` (B6), `SettingsHandle::subscribe` (B10), `RecordLine` (B1)
- Produces:
  - `mqtt::{MqttLink, LinkRequest, Reply, Correlation, LinkError, DYNSEC_TOPIC, DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC}`: der Kanal von Handlern und Broker-Verwaltung zur einen MQTT-Sitzung. Kennt weder DJI noch dynsec-Befehle.
  - `MqttLink::request(&self, topic: String, payload: Vec<u8>, correlation: Correlation, timeout: Duration) -> Result<serde_json::Value, LinkError>`
  - `MqttLink::channel() -> (MqttLink, mpsc::Receiver<LinkRequest>)`
  - `Correlation::{Tid(String), DynSec(String)}`
  - `LinkError::{NotConnected, Timeout, Closed}` mit deutschem `Display`
  - Bei `Tid` kommt der `data`-Teil von `services_reply` samt `method` zurück, bei `DynSec` das Antwortobjekt des Kommandos.
  - `dji::link::{Inbox, InboxOutput}`: `Inbox::new()`, `Inbox::restore(&mut self, &[StoredDevice]) -> Vec<Observed>`, `Inbox::register(Correlation, Reply)`, `Inbox::on_publish(at_ms, topic, payload) -> InboxOutput { observations, publish: Vec<Outgoing>, persist: Option<(String, String, Value)> }`, `Inbox::fail_all(LinkError)`
  - `dji::session::run(settings: watch::Receiver<Settings>, observer: mpsc::Sender<Observed>, pool: SqlitePool, records_dir: PathBuf, requests: mpsc::Receiver<LinkRequest>, shutdown: watch::Receiver<bool>)`
  - `dji::recorder::Recorder::open(dir: &Path, at_ms: i64) -> io::Result<Recorder>`, `.write(&RecordLine)`, `.file_name()`
  - `AppState.mqtt: MqttLink`

**Regeln für die Sitzung:**
- Im `select!`-Zweig von `eventloop.poll()` und im Anfragezweig wird nie auf `client.publish(...).await` gewartet, sondern `try_publish`/`try_subscribe` genutzt. Sonst wartet die Schleife bei voller Anfrage-Warteschlange (256) auf sich selbst, weil niemand mehr `poll()` aufruft (bekannte rumqttc-Falle).
- Der `Inbox` entsteht einmal je Prozess, vor der ersten Verbindung, und bleibt über Neuverbindungen bestehen (`fail_all` räumt nur die wartenden Antworten). Er wird dabei einmal aus `device_state` wiederhergestellt; die Observations gehen an den Kern. So kennen Kern und Übersetzer nach einem Neustart `live_capacity`, `live_status` und Startpunkt wieder (Referenz 5.1). Ein erneutes Einspielen bei jeder Neuverbindung würde mit der alten Empfangszeit Frische und Position zurückspringen lassen.
- Nach jeder state-Nachricht schreibt die Sitzung den gemergten Stand des Geräts nach `device_state`. state kommt nur bei Änderung; osd wird nicht gespeichert.

`src/mqtt.rs`:

```rust
//! Anfrage/Antwort-Kanal zur einen MQTT-Sitzung des Servers. Kennt weder DJI noch dynsec-Befehle;
//! die Zuordnung der Antworten macht der Posteingang (`dji::link`).

use std::time::Duration;

use serde_json::Value;
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot};

pub const DYNSEC_TOPIC: &str = "$CONTROL/dynamic-security/v1";
pub const DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC: &str = "$CONTROL/dynamic-security/v1/response";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum Correlation {
    Tid(String),
    DynSec(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum LinkError {
    #[error("Broker nicht verbunden")]
    NotConnected,
    #[error("keine Antwort innerhalb der Frist")]
    Timeout,
    #[error("MQTT-Sitzung beendet")]
    Closed,
}

pub type Reply = oneshot::Sender<Result<Value, LinkError>>;

pub struct LinkRequest {
    pub topic: String,
    pub payload: Vec<u8>,
    pub correlation: Correlation,
    pub reply: Reply,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct MqttLink {
    tx: mpsc::Sender<LinkRequest>,
}

impl MqttLink {
    pub fn channel() -> (MqttLink, mpsc::Receiver<LinkRequest>) {
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(64);
        (MqttLink { tx }, rx)
    }

    pub async fn request(&self, topic: String, payload: Vec<u8>, correlation: Correlation, timeout: Duration) -> Result<Value, LinkError> {
        let (reply, rx) = oneshot::channel();
        self.tx.send(LinkRequest { topic, payload, correlation, reply }).await.map_err(|_| LinkError::Closed)?;
        match tokio::time::timeout(timeout, rx).await {
            Ok(Ok(result)) => result,
            Ok(Err(_)) => Err(LinkError::Closed),
            Err(_) => Err(LinkError::Timeout),
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn request_times_out_without_answer() {
        let (link, mut rx) = MqttLink::channel();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let _keep = rx.recv().await; // angenommen, nie beantwortet
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5)).await;
        });
        let r = link.request("t".into(), vec![], Correlation::Tid("x".into()), Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
        assert_eq!(r, Err(LinkError::Timeout));
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Posteingang der MQTT-Sitzung: übersetzt, beantwortet update_topo, ordnet Antworten
//! (services_reply per `tid`, dynsec per `correlationData`) wartenden Anfragen zu und sagt, welcher
//! Gerätestand zu speichern ist. Rein bis auf die oneshot-Kanäle – testbar ohne Broker.

use std::collections::HashMap;

use serde_json::Value;

use super::topic::{self, DjiTopic};
use super::translate::{Outgoing, Translator};
use crate::mqtt::{Correlation, DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC, LinkError, Reply};
use crate::situation::observation::Observed;
use crate::store::device_state::StoredDevice;

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct InboxOutput {
    pub observations: Vec<Observed>,
    pub publish: Vec<Outgoing>,
    /// (Geräte-SN, Gateway-SN, gemergter Stand) nach einer state-Nachricht.
    pub persist: Option<(String, String, Value)>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Inbox {
    translator: Translator,
    pending: HashMap<Correlation, Reply>,
}

impl Inbox {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Spielt gespeicherte Gerätestände ein (Neustart, Neuverbindung). Liefert die Observations dazu.
    pub fn restore(&mut self, devices: &[StoredDevice]) -> Vec<Observed> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub fn register(&mut self, correlation: Correlation, reply: Reply) {
        self.pending.retain(|_, r| !r.is_closed());
        self.pending.insert(correlation, reply);
    }

    pub fn on_publish(&mut self, at_ms: i64, topic: &str, payload: &[u8]) -> InboxOutput {
        todo!()
    }

    pub fn fail_all(&mut self, error: LinkError) {
        for (_, reply) in self.pending.drain() {
            let _ = reply.send(Err(error.clone()));
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::situation::observation::Observation;
    use serde_json::json;
    use tokio::sync::oneshot;

    #[test]
    fn services_reply_resolves_the_waiting_request() {
        let mut inbox = Inbox::new();
        let (tx, mut rx) = oneshot::channel();
        inbox.register(Correlation::Tid("t1".into()), tx);
        inbox.on_publish(0, "thing/product/RC1/services_reply", json!({"tid": "t1", "bid": "b", "method": "live_start_push", "data": {"result": 0}}).to_string().as_bytes());
        assert_eq!(rx.try_recv().unwrap().unwrap(), json!({"method": "live_start_push", "result": 0}));
    }

    #[test]
    fn dynsec_response_resolves_by_correlation_data() {
        let mut inbox = Inbox::new();
        let (tx, mut rx) = oneshot::channel();
        inbox.register(Correlation::DynSec("c7".into()), tx);
        let body = json!({"responses": [{"command": "createClient", "correlationData": "c7"}]});
        let out = inbox.on_publish(0, DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC, body.to_string().as_bytes());
        assert!(out.observations.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(rx.try_recv().unwrap().unwrap()["command"], "createClient");
    }

    #[test]
    fn dynsec_error_is_passed_on_as_value() {
        let mut inbox = Inbox::new();
        let (tx, mut rx) = oneshot::channel();
        inbox.register(Correlation::DynSec("c8".into()), tx);
        let body = json!({"responses": [{"command": "createRole", "error": "Role already exists", "correlationData": "c8"}]});
        inbox.on_publish(0, DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC, body.to_string().as_bytes());
        assert_eq!(rx.try_recv().unwrap().unwrap()["error"], "Role already exists");
    }

    #[test]
    fn topology_is_answered_and_observed() {
        let mut inbox = Inbox::new();
        let out = inbox.on_publish(0, "sys/product/RC1/status", json!({"tid": "t", "bid": "b", "method": "update_topo", "data": {"sub_devices": []}}).to_string().as_bytes());
        assert_eq!(out.publish[0].topic, "sys/product/RC1/status_reply");
        assert_eq!(out.observations.len(), 1);
        assert!(out.persist.is_none(), "nur state wird gespeichert");
    }

    #[test]
    fn disconnect_fails_everything_waiting() {
        let mut inbox = Inbox::new();
        let (tx, mut rx) = oneshot::channel();
        inbox.register(Correlation::Tid("t".into()), tx);
        inbox.fail_all(LinkError::NotConnected);
        assert_eq!(rx.try_recv().unwrap(), Err(LinkError::NotConnected));
    }

    /// Review Focus 3: nach einem Neustart sind Videoquellen und Startpunkt wieder da.
    #[test]
    fn restored_inbox_knows_video_sources_and_home() {
        let mut first = Inbox::new();
        let cap = json!({"gateway": "RC1", "data": {"live_capacity": {"device_list": [{"sn": "AC1", "camera_list": [{"camera_index": "61-0-0", "video_list": [{"video_index": "normal-0", "video_type": "wide"}]}]}]}}});
        let home = json!({"gateway": "RC1", "data": {"home_latitude": 52.0, "home_longitude": 10.0}});
        let a = first.on_publish(1, "thing/product/RC1/state", cap.to_string().as_bytes()).persist.unwrap();
        let b = first.on_publish(2, "thing/product/AC1/state", home.to_string().as_bytes()).persist.unwrap();
        let stored: Vec<StoredDevice> = [(a, 1), (b, 2)]
            .into_iter()
            .map(|((sn, gateway_sn, data), at)| StoredDevice { sn, gateway_sn, data, updated_at_ms: at })
            .collect();

        let observations = Inbox::new().restore(&stored);
        assert!(observations.iter().any(|o| matches!(&o.observation, Observation::VideoSources { gateway_sn, sources } if gateway_sn == "RC1" && sources.len() == 1)));
        assert!(observations.iter().any(|o| matches!(&o.observation, Observation::AircraftTelemetry { telemetry, periodic: false, .. } if telemetry.home.is_some())));
        assert!(observations.iter().all(|o| o.at_ms <= 2), "mit der alten Empfangszeit: die Frische zeigt das wahre Alter");
    }
}
```

`src/dji/recorder.rs`:

```rust
//! Rohmitschnitt der DJI-Nachrichten im M0-Format (Spec §4.5). Eine Datei je Sitzungsstart,
//! nichts wird überschrieben. Nur `thing/` und `sys/` – nie Broker-Verwaltung.

use std::fs::{File, OpenOptions};
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::path::Path;

use super::record::{RecordLine, format_tst};

pub struct Recorder {
    file: File,
    name: String,
}

impl Recorder {
    pub fn open(dir: &Path, at_ms: i64) -> io::Result<Recorder> {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(dir)?;
        let stamp: String = format_tst(at_ms).chars().filter(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).take(14).collect();
        let name = format!("{}T{}Z.jsonl", &stamp[..8], &stamp[8..]);
        let file = OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(dir.join(&name))?;
        Ok(Recorder { file, name })
    }

    pub fn file_name(&self) -> &str {
        &self.name
    }

    pub fn write(&mut self, line: &RecordLine) -> io::Result<()> {
        if !(line.topic.starts_with("thing/") || line.topic.starts_with("sys/")) {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let mut text = serde_json::to_string(line).expect("RecordLine ist serialisierbar");
        text.push('\n');
        self.file.write_all(text.as_bytes())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::dji::record::read_lines;

    #[test]
    fn writes_m0_format_and_skips_broker_control() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let mut r = Recorder::open(dir.path(), 1_792_149_826_953).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r.file_name(), "20261016T112346Z.jsonl");
        r.write(&RecordLine::new(1, "thing/product/AC1/osd", b"{\"a\":\n1}")).unwrap();
        r.write(&RecordLine::new(2, "$CONTROL/dynamic-security/v1", b"{}")).unwrap();
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.path().join(r.file_name())).unwrap();
        let lines: Vec<_> = read_lines(text.as_bytes()).collect();
        assert_eq!(lines.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(lines[0].as_ref().unwrap().payload, "{\"a\":\n1}");
    }
}
```

```rust
    pub fn restore(&mut self, devices: &[StoredDevice]) -> Vec<Observed> {
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for d in devices {
            let Some(data) = d.data.as_object() else { continue };
            out.extend(self.translator.restore(d.updated_at_ms, &d.sn, &d.gateway_sn, data.clone()).observations);
        }
        out
    }

    pub fn on_publish(&mut self, at_ms: i64, topic: &str, payload: &[u8]) -> InboxOutput {
        let mut out = InboxOutput::default();
        if topic == DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC {
            let Ok(body) = serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(payload) else { return out };
            for response in body["responses"].as_array().into_iter().flatten() {
                if let Some(id) = response["correlationData"].as_str()
                    && let Some(reply) = self.pending.remove(&Correlation::DynSec(id.to_string()))
                {
                    let _ = reply.send(Ok(response.clone()));
                }
            }
            return out;
        }
        let translated = self.translator.handle(at_ms, topic, payload);
        if let Some(cmd) = translated.command_reply
            && let Some(reply) = self.pending.remove(&Correlation::Tid(cmd.tid.clone()))
        {
            let _ = reply.send(Ok(serde_json::json!({ "method": cmd.method, "result": cmd.result })));
        }
        if let DjiTopic::State { sn } = topic::parse(topic)
            && let Some((gateway_sn, data)) = self.translator.persisted(sn)
        {
            out.persist = Some((sn.to_string(), gateway_sn, Value::Object(data)));
        }
        out.observations = translated.observations;
        out.publish.extend(translated.reply);
        out
    }
```

Run: `cargo test dji::link`
Expected: 6 passed.

- [ ] **Step 3: rumqttc-Schleife schreiben**

`src/dji/session.rs`. Diese Schleife ist eine dünne Hülle um `Inbox`. Gegen einen echten Broker prüft sie `scripts/dev-smoke.sh` (I1):

```rust
//! MQTT-Sitzung des Servers (rumqttc, ohne TLS). Verbindet mit den Broker-Einstellungen,
//! verbindet nach Fehlern selbst neu und baut die Verbindung bei geänderten Einstellungen neu auf.
//! Kennt `runtime` nicht: Observations gehen über einen einfachen Kanal hinaus.

use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::Duration;

use rumqttc::{AsyncClient, Event, MqttOptions, Packet, QoS};
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, watch};

use super::link::Inbox;
use super::record::RecordLine;
use super::recorder::Recorder;
use super::topic::SUBSCRIPTIONS;
use crate::contract::BrokerStatus;
use crate::mqtt::{DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC, LinkError, LinkRequest};
use crate::settings::Settings;
use crate::situation::observation::{Observation, Observed};
use crate::store;
use crate::time::now_ms;

async fn report(observer: &mpsc::Sender<Observed>, observation: Observation) {
    let _ = observer.send(Observed { at_ms: now_ms(), observation }).await;
}

async fn set_recording(slot: &mut Option<Recorder>, enabled: bool, dir: &std::path::Path, observer: &mpsc::Sender<Observed>) {
    match (enabled, slot.is_some()) {
        (true, false) => match Recorder::open(dir, now_ms()) {
            Ok(r) => {
                report(observer, Observation::RawRecording(Some(r.file_name().to_string()))).await;
                *slot = Some(r);
            }
            Err(e) => tracing::error!(error = %e, "Rohmitschnitt nicht möglich"),
        },
        (false, true) => {
            *slot = None;
            report(observer, Observation::RawRecording(None)).await;
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

/// Posteingang, aus `device_state` wiederhergestellt; die Observations gehen an den Kern. Nur beim Start.
async fn restored_inbox(pool: &SqlitePool, observer: &mpsc::Sender<Observed>) -> Inbox {
    let mut inbox = Inbox::new();
    match store::device_state::all(pool).await {
        Ok(devices) => {
            for o in inbox.restore(&devices) {
                let _ = observer.send(o).await;
            }
        }
        Err(e) => tracing::warn!(error = %e, "Gerätestand nicht lesbar – startet leer"),
    }
    inbox
}

pub async fn run(
    mut settings: watch::Receiver<Settings>,
    observer: mpsc::Sender<Observed>,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    records_dir: PathBuf,
    mut requests: mpsc::Receiver<LinkRequest>,
    mut shutdown: watch::Receiver<bool>,
) {
    let mut recorder: Option<Recorder> = None;
    // Einmal je Prozess: über Neuverbindungen hinweg bleibt der Merge-Stand des Übersetzers erhalten.
    let mut inbox = restored_inbox(&pool, &observer).await;
    loop {
        let current = settings.borrow_and_update().clone();
        set_recording(&mut recorder, current.general.record_raw_mqtt, &records_dir, &observer).await;
        let Some(broker) = current.broker.clone() else {
            report(&observer, Observation::Broker(BrokerStatus::NotConfigured)).await;
            tokio::select! {
                r = settings.changed() => if r.is_err() { return },
                Some(req) = requests.recv() => { let _ = req.reply.send(Err(LinkError::NotConnected)); }
                _ = shutdown.changed() => return,
            }
            continue;
        };

        report(&observer, Observation::Broker(BrokerStatus::Connecting)).await;
        let mut options = MqttOptions::new("suchlage-server", broker.host.clone(), broker.port);
        options.set_credentials(broker.admin_username.clone(), broker.admin_password.clone());
        options.set_keep_alive(Duration::from_secs(30));
        options.set_max_packet_size(1 << 20, 1 << 20);
        let (client, mut eventloop) = AsyncClient::new(options, 256);
        let mut connected = false;

        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                event = eventloop.poll() => match event {
                    Ok(Event::Incoming(Packet::ConnAck(_))) => {
                        connected = true;
                        // try_*: in diesem Zweig nie auf die eigene Warteschlange warten (rumqttc-Falle).
                        for t in SUBSCRIPTIONS.iter().copied().chain([DYNSEC_RESPONSE_TOPIC]) {
                            if let Err(e) = client.try_subscribe(t, QoS::AtLeastOnce) {
                                tracing::error!(error = %e, topic = t, "Abonnieren fehlgeschlagen");
                            }
                        }
                        report(&observer, Observation::Broker(BrokerStatus::Connected)).await;
                    }
                    Ok(Event::Incoming(Packet::Publish(p))) => {
                        let at = now_ms();
                        if let Some(r) = recorder.as_mut()
                            && let Err(e) = r.write(&RecordLine::new(at, &p.topic, &p.payload))
                        {
                            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "Rohmitschnitt: Schreiben fehlgeschlagen");
                        }
                        let out = inbox.on_publish(at, &p.topic, &p.payload);
                        for o in out.observations {
                            let _ = observer.send(o).await;
                        }
                        for msg in out.publish {
                            if let Err(e) = client.try_publish(msg.topic, QoS::AtLeastOnce, false, msg.payload) {
                                tracing::warn!(error = %e, "Antwort nicht gesendet");
                            }
                        }
                        if let Some((sn, gateway_sn, data)) = out.persist
                            && let Err(e) = store::device_state::put(&pool, &sn, &gateway_sn, &data, at).await
                        {
                            tracing::warn!(error = %e, sn, "Gerätestand nicht gespeichert");
                        }
                    }
                    Ok(_) => {}
                    Err(e) => {
                        if connected {
                            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "MQTT-Verbindung verloren");
                        }
                        connected = false;
                        inbox.fail_all(LinkError::NotConnected);
                        report(&observer, Observation::Broker(BrokerStatus::Disconnected)).await;
                        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await; // nächstes poll() verbindet neu
                    }
                },
                Some(req) = requests.recv() => {
                    if !connected {
                        let _ = req.reply.send(Err(LinkError::NotConnected));
                        continue;
                    }
                    match client.try_publish(req.topic, QoS::AtLeastOnce, false, req.payload) {
                        // Die Antwort kann erst im nächsten poll() kommen, also nach dem Eintragen.
                        Ok(()) => inbox.register(req.correlation, req.reply),
                        Err(e) => {
                            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "Befehl nicht gesendet");
                            let _ = req.reply.send(Err(LinkError::NotConnected));
                        }
                    }
                }
                changed = settings.changed() => {
                    if changed.is_err() { return; }
                    let next = settings.borrow_and_update().clone();
                    set_recording(&mut recorder, next.general.record_raw_mqtt, &records_dir, &observer).await;
                    if next.broker != current.broker {
                        let _ = client.try_disconnect();
                        inbox.fail_all(LinkError::NotConnected);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                _ = shutdown.changed() => {
                    let _ = client.try_disconnect();
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

- `set_recording` setzt `Settings.general.record_raw_mqtt` in Zusammenspiel mit `borrow_and_update` um. Beim Neuaufbau nach geänderten Broker-Daten bleibt der Recorder bestehen.
- Das Schreiben von `device_state` wartet kurz auf SQLite, nicht auf rumqttc; das ist im Poll-Zweig unbedenklich. state-Nachrichten kommen nur bei Änderung.
- Der `Inbox` liegt außerhalb der Verbindungsschleife; nach geänderten Broker-Daten oder einem Abriss bleibt sein Stand erhalten.

### Task B13: Livebild-Befehle und Auto-Start

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/dji/command.rs`, `src/dji/control.rs`, `src/runtime/streams.rs`, `src/web/streams.rs`
- Modify: `src/dji/mod.rs`, `src/runtime/mod.rs`, `src/web/mod.rs`, `src/app.rs` (`AppState.streams`, Auto-Start statt Log-Konsument), `src/testing.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mqtt::MqttLink` (B12), `live_error_text` (B4), `SituationHandle` (B7), `store::remote_controllers` (B6), `SettingsHandle` (B10), `Operator` (B8)
- Produces:
  - `dji::command::{envelope(method, data, at_ms) -> (String, Vec<u8>), live_start_push(video_id, url) -> Value, live_stop_push(video_id) -> Value, live_lens_change(video_id, Lens) -> Value}`; der erste Rückgabewert von `envelope` ist die `tid`. Die Fehlertexte kommen aus `dji::translate::live_error_text` (B4).
  - `dji::control::LiveControl::new(MqttLink)` mit `start(gateway_sn, video_id, rtmp_url)`, `stop(gateway_sn, video_id)`, `change_lens(gateway_sn, video_id, lens)` → `Result<(), String>` (deutscher Satz); Frist je Befehl 10 s
  - `runtime::streams::{StreamOrchestrator, publish_url(&MediaSettings, gateway_sn, stream_key) -> String, run_auto(rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<String>, orchestrator)}`
  - `StreamOrchestrator::new(situation, control, pool, settings)` mit `start(gateway_sn, video_id: Option<String>)`, `stop(gateway_sn)`, `lens(gateway_sn, Lens)` → `Result<StreamState, AppError>`
  - Routen `POST /api/streams/start|stop|lens`, Rolle operator
  - `AppState.streams: StreamOrchestrator`

**Regeln:**
- RTMP-Ziel ist `<media.rtmp_url>/live/<gateway_sn>?user=rc-<gateway_sn>&pass=<stream_key>`.
- Standardquelle ist die erste Nicht-FPV-Kamera, sonst die erste gemeldete.
- Fernsteuerung unbekannt oder ohne Fluggerät: 409 „Fernsteuerung nicht verbunden“.
- Nicht gekoppelte Fernsteuerung: 409 „Fernsteuerung ist nicht gekoppelt“.
- `video_id` nicht in den gemeldeten Quellen: 422. Keine Quellen: 422 „Keine Videoquelle gemeldet – ist das Fluggerät eingeschaltet?“
- MediaMTX nicht eingerichtet: 503.
- DJI-Fehler: 422 mit `live_error_text`; der Stream-Zustand wird `failed`, außer beim Linsenwechsel.

```rust
//! Livestream-Services (Referenz Kap. 5.2). MQTT-`url_type` 1 = RTMP – nicht mit dem
//! JSBridge-Enum (2 = RTMP) verwechseln.

use serde_json::{Value, json};

use crate::contract::Lens;

pub fn envelope(method: &str, data: Value, at_ms: i64) -> (String, Vec<u8>) {
    let tid = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let body = json!({ "tid": tid, "bid": uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string(), "timestamp": at_ms, "method": method, "data": data });
    (tid, body.to_string().into_bytes())
}

pub fn live_start_push(video_id: &str, url: &str) -> Value {
    json!({ "url_type": 1, "url": url, "video_id": video_id, "video_quality": 0 })
}

pub fn live_stop_push(video_id: &str) -> Value {
    json!({ "video_id": video_id })
}

pub fn live_lens_change(video_id: &str, lens: Lens) -> Value {
    let video_type = match lens {
        Lens::Normal => "normal",
        Lens::Wide => "wide",
        Lens::Zoom => "zoom",
        Lens::Thermal => "thermal",
    };
    json!({ "video_id": video_id, "video_type": video_type })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn start_push_uses_mqtt_rtmp_enum_and_adaptive_quality() {
        assert_eq!(
            live_start_push("AC1/61-0-0/normal-0", "rtmp://h:1935/live/RC1"),
            json!({"url_type": 1, "url": "rtmp://h:1935/live/RC1", "video_id": "AC1/61-0-0/normal-0", "video_quality": 0})
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn envelope_carries_tid_bid_method_and_timestamp() {
        let (tid, bytes) = envelope("live_stop_push", live_stop_push("v"), 42);
        let v: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["tid"], tid.as_str());
        assert_eq!((v["method"].as_str(), v["timestamp"].as_i64()), (Some("live_stop_push"), Some(42)));
        assert!(v["bid"].as_str().unwrap().len() >= 32);
    }

    #[test]
    fn lens_uses_dji_spelling() {
        assert_eq!(live_lens_change("v", Lens::Thermal)["video_type"], "thermal");
    }
}
```

`src/dji/control.rs`:

```rust
//! Livebild-Befehle an Pilot 2 mit Domänenargumenten. Außerhalb von `dji/` sieht niemand
//! Methodennamen oder Ergebniscodes.

use std::time::Duration;

use crate::contract::Lens;
use crate::time::now_ms;

use super::command;
use super::topic;
use super::translate::live_error_text;
use crate::mqtt::{Correlation, LinkError, MqttLink};

const TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct LiveControl {
    link: MqttLink,
}

impl LiveControl {
    pub fn new(link: MqttLink) -> Self {
        Self { link }
    }

    async fn call(&self, gateway_sn: &str, method: &str, data: serde_json::Value) -> Result<(), String> {
        let (tid, payload) = command::envelope(method, data, now_ms());
        match self.link.request(topic::services(gateway_sn), payload, Correlation::Tid(tid), TIMEOUT).await {
            Ok(reply) => match reply["result"].as_i64() {
                Some(0) => Ok(()),
                Some(code) => Err(live_error_text(code)),
                None => Err("Pilot 2 antwortet ohne Ergebnis.".into()),
            },
            Err(LinkError::Timeout) => Err("Pilot 2 antwortet nicht (keine Antwort in 10 s).".into()),
            Err(e) => Err(format!("Befehl nicht gesendet: {e}.")),
        }
    }

    pub async fn start(&self, gateway_sn: &str, video_id: &str, rtmp_url: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.call(gateway_sn, "live_start_push", command::live_start_push(video_id, rtmp_url)).await
    }

    pub async fn stop(&self, gateway_sn: &str, video_id: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.call(gateway_sn, "live_stop_push", command::live_stop_push(video_id)).await
    }

    pub async fn change_lens(&self, gateway_sn: &str, video_id: &str, lens: Lens) -> Result<(), String> {
        self.call(gateway_sn, "live_lens_change", command::live_lens_change(video_id, lens)).await
    }
}
```

```rust
use axum::extract::State;
use axum::routing::post;
use axum::{Json, Router};

use crate::app::AppState;
use crate::auth::session::Operator;
use crate::contract::{StreamLensRequest, StreamStartRequest, StreamState, StreamStopRequest};
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::extract::JsonBody;

pub fn routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new().route("/streams/start", post(start)).route("/streams/stop", post(stop)).route("/streams/lens", post(lens))
}

async fn start(_: Operator, State(s): State<AppState>, JsonBody(b): JsonBody<StreamStartRequest>) -> Result<Json<StreamState>, AppError> {
    Ok(Json(s.streams.start(&b.gateway_sn, b.video_id).await?))
}

async fn stop(_: Operator, State(s): State<AppState>, JsonBody(b): JsonBody<StreamStopRequest>) -> Result<Json<StreamState>, AppError> {
    Ok(Json(s.streams.stop(&b.gateway_sn).await?))
}

async fn lens(_: Operator, State(s): State<AppState>, JsonBody(b): JsonBody<StreamLensRequest>) -> Result<Json<StreamState>, AppError> {
    Ok(Json(s.streams.lens(&b.gateway_sn, b.lens).await?))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use axum::http::StatusCode;
    use serde_json::json;

    use crate::contract::{Role, examples};
    use crate::situation::observation::{Observation, Observed};
    use crate::testing::{TestApp, post_json, send, test_app, with_cookie};

    /// Fernsteuerung gekoppelt, Fluggerät mit Quellen gemeldet, MediaMTX eingerichtet.
    async fn ready(app: &TestApp) {
        let (rc, _) = crate::store::remote_controllers::create(&app.state.pool, "RC Plus 1", 0).await.unwrap();
        crate::store::remote_controllers::bind(&app.state.pool, rc.id, "RC1", "pw", "streamkey", 1).await.unwrap();
        app.state.settings.update_media(crate::contract::MediaSettings {
            webrtc_url: "http://media:8889".into(), hls_url: "http://media:8888".into(), playback_url: "http://media:9996".into(), rtmp_url: "rtmp://100.64.0.10:1935".into(),
        }).await.unwrap();
        let s = &app.state.situation;
        s.observe(Observed { at_ms: 1, observation: Observation::Topology { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), aircraft_sn: Some("AC1".into()) } }).await;
        s.observe(Observed { at_ms: 2, observation: Observation::VideoSources { gateway_sn: "RC1".into(), sources: examples::video_sources() } }).await;
    }

    /// Beantwortet den nächsten MQTT-Befehl mit `result` und liefert Topic und Nutzlast zurück.
    fn answer(app: &mut TestApp, result: i64) -> tokio::task::JoinHandle<(String, serde_json::Value)> {
        let mut rx = std::mem::replace(&mut app.mqtt_requests, crate::mqtt::MqttLink::channel().1);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let req = rx.recv().await.unwrap();
            let _ = req.reply.send(Ok(json!({"method": "x", "result": result})));
            (req.topic, serde_json::from_slice(&req.payload).unwrap())
        })
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn start_sends_live_start_push_with_rtmp_target_and_reports_live() {
        let mut app = test_app().await;
        ready(&app).await;
        let op = { app.cookie(Role::Admin).await; app.cookie(Role::Operator).await };
        let answered = answer(&mut app, 0);
        let (status, body) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(post_json("/api/streams/start", json!({"gateway_sn": "RC1", "video_id": null})), &op)).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK, "{body}");
        assert_eq!(body["phase"], "live");
        let (topic, payload) = answered.await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(topic, "thing/product/RC1/services");
        assert_eq!(payload["method"], "live_start_push");
        assert_eq!(payload["data"]["video_id"], format!("{}/61-0-0/normal-0", examples::AIRCRAFT_SN), "Standard: erste Nutzlastkamera");
        assert_eq!(payload["data"]["url"], "rtmp://100.64.0.10:1935/live/RC1?user=rc-RC1&pass=streamkey");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn dji_rejection_is_422_and_stream_fails() {
        let mut app = test_app().await;
        ready(&app).await;
        let op = { app.cookie(Role::Admin).await; app.cookie(Role::Operator).await };
        let _ = answer(&mut app, 513011);
        let (status, body) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(post_json("/api/streams/start", json!({"gateway_sn": "RC1", "video_id": null})), &op)).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY);
        assert!(body["error"].as_str().unwrap().contains("513011"));
        assert_eq!(app.state.situation.stream("RC1").await.unwrap().unwrap().phase, crate::contract::StreamPhase::Failed);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn unknown_gateway_and_foreign_video_id_are_refused() {
        let app = test_app().await;
        ready(&app).await;
        let op = { app.cookie(Role::Admin).await; app.cookie(Role::Operator).await };
        let (status, _) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(post_json("/api/streams/start", json!({"gateway_sn": "RCX", "video_id": null})), &op)).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::CONFLICT);
        let (status, _) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(post_json("/api/streams/start", json!({"gateway_sn": "RC1", "video_id": "AC1/99-0-0/normal-0"})), &op)).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn auto_request_after_takeoff_starts_the_default_stream() {
        let mut app = test_app().await;
        ready(&app).await;
        let answered = answer(&mut app, 0);
        let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        tokio::spawn(crate::runtime::streams::run_auto(rx, app.state.streams.clone()));
        tx.send("RC1".into()).unwrap();
        let (_, payload) = answered.await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(payload["method"], "live_start_push");
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Livebild-Ablauf: Quelle wählen, Ziel bauen, Befehl senden, Zustand im Kern führen.
//! Von der API und vom Auto-Start beim Abheben benutzt.

use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;

use crate::contract::{Lens, MediaSettings, StreamState};
use crate::dji::control::LiveControl;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::runtime::service::SituationHandle;
use crate::settings::SettingsHandle;
use crate::situation::observation::{Observation, Observed, StreamCommand};
use crate::situation::stream::idle;
use crate::store;
use crate::time::now_ms;

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct StreamOrchestrator {
    situation: SituationHandle,
    control: LiveControl,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    settings: SettingsHandle,
}

pub fn publish_url(media: &MediaSettings, gateway_sn: &str, stream_key: &str) -> String {
    format!("{}/live/{gateway_sn}?user=rc-{gateway_sn}&pass={stream_key}", media.rtmp_url.trim_end_matches('/'))
}

pub async fn run_auto(mut rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<String>, orchestrator: StreamOrchestrator) {
    while let Some(gateway_sn) = rx.recv().await {
        match orchestrator.start(&gateway_sn, None).await {
            Ok(_) => tracing::info!(gateway = %gateway_sn, "Livebild beim Abheben gestartet"),
            Err(e) => tracing::warn!(gateway = %gateway_sn, error = %e, "Livebild beim Abheben nicht gestartet"),
        }
    }
}

impl StreamOrchestrator {
    pub fn new(situation: SituationHandle, control: LiveControl, pool: SqlitePool, settings: SettingsHandle) -> Self {
        Self { situation, control, pool, settings }
    }

    async fn observe(&self, observation: Observation) {
        self.situation.observe(Observed { at_ms: now_ms(), observation }).await;
    }

    async fn current(&self, gateway_sn: &str) -> Result<StreamState, AppError> {
        Ok(self.situation.stream(gateway_sn).await?.unwrap_or_else(|| idle(gateway_sn, now_ms())))
    }

    pub async fn start(&self, gateway_sn: &str, video_id: Option<String>) -> Result<StreamState, AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn stop(&self, gateway_sn: &str) -> Result<StreamState, AppError> {
        todo!()
    }

    pub async fn lens(&self, gateway_sn: &str, lens: Lens) -> Result<StreamState, AppError> {
        todo!()
    }
}
```

```rust
    pub async fn start(&self, gateway_sn: &str, video_id: Option<String>) -> Result<StreamState, AppError> {
        let gateway = self
            .situation
            .gateway(gateway_sn)
            .await?
            .filter(|g| g.aircraft_sn.is_some())
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Conflict("Fernsteuerung nicht verbunden".into()))?;
        let source = match video_id {
            Some(id) => gateway.video_sources.iter().find(|s| s.video_id == id).ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unprocessable("Diese Videoquelle meldet das Fluggerät nicht".into()))?,
            None => gateway
                .video_sources
                .iter()
                .find(|s| s.camera_label != "FPV")
                .or(gateway.video_sources.first())
                .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unprocessable("Keine Videoquelle gemeldet – ist das Fluggerät eingeschaltet?".into()))?,
        };
        let media = self.settings.current().media.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unavailable("MediaMTX ist nicht eingerichtet (Einstellungen → Video)".into()))?;
        let rc = store::remote_controllers::find_by_sn(&self.pool, gateway_sn).await?;
        let stream_key = rc.and_then(|r| r.stream_key).ok_or_else(|| AppError::Conflict("Fernsteuerung ist nicht gekoppelt".into()))?;
        let url = publish_url(&media, gateway_sn, &stream_key);

        let command = StreamCommand::Start { video_id: source.video_id.clone() };
        self.observe(Observation::CommandIssued { gateway_sn: gateway_sn.into(), command: command.clone() }).await;
        let result = self.control.start(gateway_sn, &source.video_id, &url).await;
        self.observe(Observation::CommandFinished { gateway_sn: gateway_sn.into(), command, result: result.clone() }).await;
        result.map_err(AppError::Unprocessable)?;
        self.current(gateway_sn).await
    }

    pub async fn stop(&self, gateway_sn: &str) -> Result<StreamState, AppError> {
        let current = self.current(gateway_sn).await?;
        let video_id = current.video_id.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unprocessable("Es läuft kein Livebild".into()))?;
        self.observe(Observation::CommandIssued { gateway_sn: gateway_sn.into(), command: StreamCommand::Stop }).await;
        let result = self.control.stop(gateway_sn, &video_id).await;
        self.observe(Observation::CommandFinished { gateway_sn: gateway_sn.into(), command: StreamCommand::Stop, result: result.clone() }).await;
        result.map_err(AppError::Unprocessable)?;
        self.current(gateway_sn).await
    }

    pub async fn lens(&self, gateway_sn: &str, lens: Lens) -> Result<StreamState, AppError> {
        let current = self.current(gateway_sn).await?;
        let video_id = current.video_id.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unprocessable("Es läuft kein Livebild".into()))?;
        let command = StreamCommand::ChangeLens { lens };
        let result = self.control.change_lens(gateway_sn, &video_id, lens).await;
        self.observe(Observation::CommandFinished { gateway_sn: gateway_sn.into(), command, result: result.clone() }).await;
        result.map_err(AppError::Unprocessable)?;
        self.current(gateway_sn).await
    }
```

### Task B14: Broker-Verwaltung, Fernsteuerungen, Pilot-Seite `/pilot`

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/broker/mod.rs`, `src/broker/dynsec.rs`, `src/web/pilot.rs`, `src/dji/pilot.rs`
- Create (aus M0 übernommen und angepasst): `src/dji/pilot/index.html`, `src/dji/pilot/pilot.js`, `src/dji/pilot/pilot.test.mjs`
- Modify: `src/dji/session.rs` (Serverrolle bei ConnAck), `src/web/admin.rs` (Fernsteuerungs-Routen), `src/auth/dev.rs` (Simulator-Fernsteuerung), `src/app.rs`, `src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `mqtt::{MqttLink, DYNSEC_TOPIC}` (B12), `store::remote_controllers` inkl. `create_with_code` (B6), `SettingsHandle` (B10), `Admin` (B8)
- Produces:
  - `broker::dynsec::{rc_username(sn) -> String, DEVICES_ROLE, BLOCK_ROLE, ensure_rc_commands(sn, password) -> Vec<Value>, revoke_rc_commands(sn) -> Vec<Value>, server_role_commands(admin_username) -> Vec<Value>, payload(commands) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<u8>), is_benign(error: &str) -> bool}`
  - `broker::{ensure_rc(&MqttLink, sn, password) -> Result<(), AppError>, revoke_rc(&MqttLink, sn) -> Result<(), AppError>}`
  - `dji::pilot::{PilotLicense, PilotConnection, INDEX_HTML, PILOT_JS}`
  - Routen: `GET /pilot`, `GET /pilot/pilot.js`, `POST /pilot/api/license` und `POST /pilot/api/connect` (öffentlich, Kopplungscode); `GET|POST /api/remote-controllers`, `DELETE /api/remote-controllers/{id}` (admin)
  - `pilot.js`: `pair(code, bridge, log, post)`, `verify`, `readRcSn`, `connect`, `startManualLive`, `PairingError`
  - Nur Feature `dev`: `auth::dev::{SIMULATOR_CODE = "dev-simulator", seed_simulator(&pool)}`; legt die Fernsteuerung „Simulator“ mit festem Code an, wenn sie fehlt

**Ablauf der Kopplung in Pilot 2** (Doku `30.jsbridge.md`: „Authentication is required before the JSBridge-related interfaces can be called“; jede JSBridge-Antwort ist ein JSON-String `{code, message, data}`):
1. `POST /pilot/api/license {code}` → App-ID, App-Key, Lizenz, Workspace-ID, Bezeichnung. Die Geheimnisschranke bleibt der Kopplungscode.
2. Die Seite prüft die Lizenz (`platformVerifyLicense`, `platformIsVerified`).
3. Erst jetzt liest sie die Seriennummer (`platformGetRemoteControllerSN`), tolerant: `data` aus der JSON-Hülle, sonst der Rohwert; danach Prüfung `[A-Za-z0-9]{1,64}`.
4. `POST /pilot/api/connect {code, rc_sn}` → bindet die SN beim ersten Mal, legt den Broker-Zugang an und liefert MQTT- und RTMP-Zugang.
5. Die Seite lädt `thing` und `liveshare`.

**ACL je Fernsteuerung** (ADR 0003). Rollen werden nach Priorität geprüft, der erste Treffer gilt (Annahme für dynsec 2.0.22, `dev:smoke` prüft sie):
- Eigene Rolle `rc-<SN>`, Priorität 3: `publishClientSend` und `subscribePattern` für `sys/product/<SN>/#` und `thing/product/<SN>/#`.
- Gemeinsame Rolle `suchlage-rc-sperre`, Priorität 2: verbietet `publishClientSend thing/product/<X>/#` für jede gekoppelte RC X. Beim Koppeln kommt die eigene SN dazu, beim Widerruf geht sie wieder raus.
- Gemeinsame Rolle `suchlage-rc-geraete`, Priorität 1: erlaubt `publishClientSend thing/product/+/osd` und `+/state` (Fluggeräte-SN vorher unbekannt).
- Serverrolle `suchlage-server` am Admin-Client: `publishClientSend #`, `subscribePattern #`. Die `dynsec init`-Rolle `admin` erlaubt kein Senden auf beliebige Topics.

```rust
//! Befehle für das dynamic-security-Plugin von Mosquitto 2.0.22 (ADR 0003). `%u`/`%c` werden in
//! dieser Version nicht ersetzt (geprüft 16.10.2026) – deshalb eine Rolle je Fernsteuerung.

use serde_json::{Value, json};

pub fn rc_username(sn: &str) -> String {
    format!("rc-{sn}")
}

/// Erlaubt Fluggeräte-Telemetrie mit vorher unbekannter SN (Priorität 1).
pub const DEVICES_ROLE: &str = "suchlage-rc-geraete";
/// Verbietet die Topics aller gekoppelten RCs (Priorität 2), damit `+/osd|state` keine fremde RC trifft.
pub const BLOCK_ROLE: &str = "suchlage-rc-sperre";

fn acl(role: &str, acltype: &str, topic: String, allow: bool) -> Value {
    json!({ "command": "addRoleACL", "rolename": role, "acltype": acltype, "topic": topic, "allow": allow, "priority": 0 })
}

fn client_role(username: &str, role: &str, priority: i32) -> Value {
    json!({ "command": "addClientRole", "username": username, "rolename": role, "priority": priority })
}

pub fn ensure_rc_commands(sn: &str, password: &str) -> Vec<Value> {
    let name = rc_username(sn);
    vec![
        json!({ "command": "createRole", "rolename": name }),
        acl(&name, "publishClientSend", format!("sys/product/{sn}/#"), true),
        acl(&name, "publishClientSend", format!("thing/product/{sn}/#"), true),
        acl(&name, "subscribePattern", format!("sys/product/{sn}/#"), true),
        acl(&name, "subscribePattern", format!("thing/product/{sn}/#"), true),
        json!({ "command": "createRole", "rolename": DEVICES_ROLE }),
        acl(DEVICES_ROLE, "publishClientSend", "thing/product/+/osd".into(), true),
        acl(DEVICES_ROLE, "publishClientSend", "thing/product/+/state".into(), true),
        json!({ "command": "createRole", "rolename": BLOCK_ROLE }),
        acl(BLOCK_ROLE, "publishClientSend", format!("thing/product/{sn}/#"), false),
        json!({ "command": "createClient", "username": name, "password": password }),
        json!({ "command": "setClientPassword", "username": name, "password": password }),
        client_role(&name, &name, 3),
        client_role(&name, BLOCK_ROLE, 2),
        client_role(&name, DEVICES_ROLE, 1),
    ]
}

pub fn revoke_rc_commands(sn: &str) -> Vec<Value> {
    let name = rc_username(sn);
    vec![
        json!({ "command": "deleteClient", "username": name }),
        json!({ "command": "deleteRole", "rolename": name }),
        json!({ "command": "removeRoleACL", "rolename": BLOCK_ROLE, "acltype": "publishClientSend", "topic": format!("thing/product/{sn}/#") }),
    ]
}

pub fn server_role_commands(admin_username: &str) -> Vec<Value> {
    vec![
        json!({ "command": "createRole", "rolename": "suchlage-server" }),
        acl("suchlage-server", "publishClientSend", "#".into(), true),
        acl("suchlage-server", "subscribePattern", "#".into(), true),
        client_role(admin_username, "suchlage-server", 0),
    ]
}

/// Hängt jedem Befehl eine `correlationData` an und baut die Nutzlast.
pub fn payload(mut commands: Vec<Value>) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<u8>) {
    let ids: Vec<String> = commands
        .iter_mut()
        .map(|c| {
            let id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
            c["correlationData"] = json!(id);
            id
        })
        .collect();
    (ids, json!({ "commands": commands }).to_string().into_bytes())
}

/// Fehler, die bei wiederholtem, idempotentem Anlegen erwartbar sind.
pub fn is_benign(error: &str) -> bool {
    let e = error.to_lowercase();
    e.contains("already") || e.contains("not found")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn rc_role_is_bound_to_its_own_serial_number() {
        let cmds = ensure_rc_commands("RCA", "pw");
        let topics: Vec<(&str, &str)> = cmds.iter().filter(|c| c["command"] == "addRoleACL").map(|c| (c["acltype"].as_str().unwrap(), c["topic"].as_str().unwrap())).collect();
        assert!(topics.contains(&("subscribePattern", "thing/product/RCA/#")));
        assert!(!topics.iter().any(|(t, topic)| *t == "subscribePattern" && topic.contains('+')), "kein Abo fremder Geräte");
        assert!(!topics.iter().any(|(_, topic)| topic.contains("%u")), "%u wird in 2.0.22 nicht ersetzt");
    }

    #[test]
    fn own_role_beats_the_block_which_beats_the_wildcard_devices() {
        let cmds = ensure_rc_commands("RCA", "pw");
        let priority = |role: &str| cmds.iter().find(|c| c["command"] == "addClientRole" && c["rolename"] == role).unwrap()["priority"].as_i64().unwrap();
        assert!(priority("rc-RCA") > priority(BLOCK_ROLE) && priority(BLOCK_ROLE) > priority(DEVICES_ROLE));
        let block = cmds.iter().find(|c| c["rolename"] == BLOCK_ROLE && c["command"] == "addRoleACL").unwrap();
        assert_eq!((block["topic"].as_str(), block["allow"].as_bool()), (Some("thing/product/RCA/#"), Some(false)));
        let wildcard: Vec<_> = cmds.iter().filter(|c| c["command"] == "addRoleACL" && c["topic"].as_str().unwrap().contains('+')).collect();
        assert!(wildcard.iter().all(|c| c["rolename"] == DEVICES_ROLE), "Platzhalter nur in der Rolle mit der niedrigsten Priorität");
    }

    #[test]
    fn payload_tags_every_command_for_correlation() {
        let (ids, bytes) = payload(revoke_rc_commands("RCA"));
        let v: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(ids.len(), 3);
        assert_eq!(v["commands"][2]["correlationData"], ids[2].as_str());
        assert_eq!(v["commands"][2]["command"], "removeRoleACL");
    }

    #[test]
    fn repeated_setup_errors_are_benign() {
        assert!(is_benign("Role already exists"));
        assert!(is_benign("Client not found"));
        assert!(!is_benign("Permission denied"));
    }
}
```

`src/broker/mod.rs`:

```rust
//! Broker-Verwaltung über das dynsec-Plugin. Reine Befehls-JSONs in `dynsec`, Versand über `MqttLink`.

pub mod dynsec;

use std::time::Duration;

use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::mqtt::{Correlation, DYNSEC_TOPIC, MqttLink};

/// Jeder Befehl einzeln: der Posteingang (B12) ordnet je `correlationData` genau eine Antwort zu,
/// so zählt jeder Fehler.
async fn run(link: &MqttLink, commands: Vec<serde_json::Value>) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    for command in commands {
        let (ids, payload) = dynsec::payload(vec![command]);
        let response = link
            .request(DYNSEC_TOPIC.into(), payload, Correlation::DynSec(ids[0].clone()), Duration::from_secs(5))
            .await
            .map_err(|e| AppError::Unavailable(format!("Broker nicht erreichbar: {e}")))?;
        if let Some(error) = response["error"].as_str().filter(|e| !dynsec::is_benign(e)) {
            return Err(AppError::Unavailable(format!("Broker lehnt ab: {error}")));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn ensure_rc(link: &MqttLink, sn: &str, password: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    run(link, dynsec::ensure_rc_commands(sn, password)).await
}

pub async fn revoke_rc(link: &MqttLink, sn: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    run(link, dynsec::revoke_rc_commands(sn)).await
}
```

```rust
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn valid_code_binds_the_rc_and_returns_its_own_credentials() {
        let mut app = test_app().await;
        let code = configured(&app).await;
        broker_ok(&mut app);
        let (status, cfg) = send(&app.router, post_json("/pilot/api/connect", json!({"code": code, "rc_sn": "RC1"}))).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK, "{cfg}");
        assert_eq!(cfg["mqttUser"], "rc-RC1");
        assert_eq!(cfg["mqttHost"], "tcp://100.64.0.10:1883");
        assert!(cfg.get("appKey").is_none(), "Lizenz nur im ersten Schritt");
        assert!(cfg["rtmpUrl"].as_str().unwrap().starts_with("rtmp://100.64.0.10:1935/live/RC1?user=rc-RC1&pass="));
        let pass = cfg["mqttPass"].as_str().unwrap().to_string();

        let (_, again) = send(&app.router, post_json("/pilot/api/connect", json!({"code": code, "rc_sn": "RC1"}))).await;
        assert_eq!(again["mqttPass"], pass.as_str(), "stabil über Neustarts von Pilot 2");
        let (status, _) = send(&app.router, post_json("/pilot/api/connect", json!({"code": code, "rc_sn": "RC2"}))).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::CONFLICT, "Code gehört zu RC1");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn invalid_serial_number_is_rejected_before_anything_else() {
        let app = test_app().await;
        let code = configured(&app).await;
        let (status, _) = send(&app.router, post_json("/pilot/api/connect", json!({"code": code, "rc_sn": "../x"}))).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn page_is_served_without_secrets() {
        let app = test_app().await;
        configured(&app).await;
        let (status, html) = send(&app.router, crate::testing::get("/pilot?code=x")).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);
        assert!(!html.to_string().contains("APPKEY-GEHEIM"));
    }
}
```

```rust
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn rc_lifecycle_create_once_visible_code_then_revoke_at_broker() {
        let mut app = test_app().await;
        let admin = app.cookie(Role::Admin).await;
        let (status, created) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(crate::testing::post_json("/api/remote-controllers", serde_json::json!({"label": "RC Plus 1"})), &admin)).await;
        assert_eq!(status, StatusCode::OK);
        let url = created["pairing_url"].as_str().unwrap();
        assert!(url.starts_with("https://suchlage.test/pilot?code="));
        let (_, list) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(get("/api/remote-controllers"), &admin)).await;
        assert!(!list.to_string().contains(url.split_once("code=").unwrap().1), "Code steht nur in der Antwort beim Anlegen");

        let id = created["controller"]["id"].as_i64().unwrap();
        crate::store::remote_controllers::bind(&app.state.pool, id, "RC1", "pw", "k", 1).await.unwrap();
        let mut rx = std::mem::replace(&mut app.mqtt_requests, crate::mqtt::MqttLink::channel().1);
        let revoked = tokio::spawn(async move {
            let mut cmds = vec![];
            while let Ok(Some(req)) = tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_millis(500), rx.recv()).await {
                let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&req.payload).unwrap();
                cmds.push(v["commands"][0]["command"].as_str().unwrap().to_string());
                let _ = req.reply.send(Ok(serde_json::json!({})));
            }
            cmds
        });
        let req = axum::http::Request::delete(format!("/api/remote-controllers/{id}")).body(axum::body::Body::empty()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(send(&app.router, with_cookie(req, &admin)).await.0, StatusCode::NO_CONTENT);
        assert_eq!(revoked.await.unwrap(), vec!["deleteClient", "deleteRole", "removeRoleACL"]);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn revocation_is_refused_when_the_broker_is_unreachable() {
        let app = test_app().await; // niemand beantwortet MQTT-Anfragen → Zeitüberschreitung
        let admin = app.cookie(Role::Admin).await;
        let (rc, _) = crate::store::remote_controllers::create(&app.state.pool, "RC", 0).await.unwrap();
        crate::store::remote_controllers::bind(&app.state.pool, rc.id, "RC1", "pw", "k", 1).await.unwrap();
        let req = axum::http::Request::delete(format!("/api/remote-controllers/{}", rc.id)).body(axum::body::Body::empty()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(send(&app.router, with_cookie(req, &admin)).await.0, StatusCode::SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE);
        assert!(crate::store::remote_controllers::find_by_sn(&app.state.pool, "RC1").await.unwrap().is_some(), "nicht still gelöscht");
    }
```

`web/pilot.rs`:

```rust
async fn by_code(state: &AppState, code: &str) -> Result<RemoteController, AppError> {
    let rejected = || AppError::UnauthorizedWith("Kopplungscode ungültig – in Suchlage unter Einstellungen → Fernsteuerungen einen neuen erzeugen".into());
    if code.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err(rejected());
    }
    rcs::find_by_code(&state.pool, code.trim()).await?.ok_or_else(rejected)
}

async fn license(State(state): State<AppState>, JsonBody(req): JsonBody<LicenseRequest>) -> Result<Json<PilotLicense>, AppError> {
    let rc = by_code(&state, &req.code).await?;
    let dji = state.settings.current().dji.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unavailable("DJI-App-Daten fehlen (Einstellungen → DJI)".into()))?;
    Ok(Json(PilotLicense { app_id: dji.app_id, app_key: dji.app_key, license: dji.license, workspace_id: dji.workspace_id, rc_label: rc.label }))
}

async fn connect(State(state): State<AppState>, JsonBody(req): JsonBody<ConnectRequest>) -> Result<Json<PilotConnection>, AppError> {
    if !is_valid_sn(&req.rc_sn) {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Seriennummer der Fernsteuerung ungültig".into()));
    }
    let rc = by_code(&state, &req.code).await?;
    let settings = state.settings.current();
    let broker = settings.broker.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unavailable("Broker ist nicht eingerichtet".into()))?;
    let media = settings.media.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unavailable("MediaMTX ist nicht eingerichtet".into()))?;

    let (password, stream_key) = match (&rc.sn, &rc.mqtt_password, &rc.stream_key) {
        (Some(sn), Some(p), Some(k)) if *sn == req.rc_sn => (p.clone(), k.clone()),
        (Some(_), _, _) => return Err(AppError::Conflict("Kopplungscode gehört zu einer anderen Fernsteuerung".into())),
        _ => {
            let (p, k) = (crate::token::generate(), crate::token::generate());
            rcs::bind(&state.pool, rc.id, &req.rc_sn, &p, &k, now_ms()).await?;
            let labels = rcs::labels(&state.pool).await?;
            state.situation.observe(Observed { at_ms: now_ms(), observation: Observation::Labels(labels) }).await;
            (p, k)
        }
    };
    // Idempotent: legt nach einem Broker-Neuaufbau fehlende Zugänge wieder an.
    crate::broker::ensure_rc(&state.mqtt, &req.rc_sn, &password).await?;
    Ok(Json(PilotConnection {
        mqtt_host: broker.rc_url,
        mqtt_user: crate::broker::dynsec::rc_username(&req.rc_sn),
        mqtt_pass: password,
        rtmp_url: publish_url(&media, &req.rc_sn, &stream_key),
    }))
}
```

`web/admin.rs`, Routen und Handler:

```rust
        .route("/remote-controllers", get(list_rcs).post(create_rc))
        .route("/remote-controllers/{id}", delete(delete_rc))

async fn list_rcs(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>) -> Result<Json<Vec<RemoteControllerView>>, AppError> {
    Ok(Json(store::remote_controllers::list(&state.pool).await?.iter().map(store::remote_controllers::view).collect()))
}

async fn create_rc(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>, JsonBody(body): JsonBody<RemoteControllerCreate>) -> Result<Json<RemoteControllerCreated>, AppError> {
    let label = body.label.trim();
    if label.is_empty() || label.chars().count() > 80 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Bezeichnung: 1 bis 80 Zeichen".into()));
    }
    let (rc, code) = store::remote_controllers::create(&state.pool, label, now_ms()).await?;
    let pairing_url = format!("{}/pilot?code={code}", state.boot.public_url.as_str().trim_end_matches('/'));
    Ok(Json(RemoteControllerCreated { controller: store::remote_controllers::view(&rc), pairing_url }))
}

async fn delete_rc(_: Admin, State(state): State<AppState>, Path(id): Path<i64>) -> Result<StatusCode, AppError> {
    let rc = store::remote_controllers::list(&state.pool).await?.into_iter().find(|r| r.id == id).ok_or(AppError::NotFound)?;
    // Erst beim Broker widerrufen; scheitert das, bleibt der Eintrag (kein stiller Restzugang).
    if let Some(sn) = &rc.sn {
        crate::broker::revoke_rc(&state.mqtt, sn).await?;
    }
    store::remote_controllers::delete(&state.pool, id).await?;
    let labels = store::remote_controllers::labels(&state.pool).await?;
    state.situation.observe(crate::situation::observation::Observed { at_ms: now_ms(), observation: crate::situation::observation::Observation::Labels(labels) }).await;
    Ok(StatusCode::NO_CONTENT)
}
```
