`src/dji/pilot/pilot.js` baut auf `deploy/pilot/pilot.js` aus M0 auf. `caller` und `startManualLive` bleiben, der Ablauf kommt neu dazu und ist mit `node --test` geprüft:

```js
// Anmeldeseite für DJI Pilot 2 – aus M0 übernommen. Die Zugangsdaten kommen jetzt in zwei Schritten
// vom Server: erst die Lizenz (ohne sie gibt Pilot 2 die Seriennummer nicht heraus), dann der Zugang.

export class PairingError extends Error {}

function caller(bridge, log) {
  return (name, ...args) => {
    const res = JSON.parse(bridge[name](...args));
    log(`${name}: ${res.code === 0 ? "ok" : `FEHLER ${res.code} ${res.message}`}`);
    if (res.code !== 0) throw new Error(name);
    return res.data;
  };
}

/** Seriennummer der Fernsteuerung: `data` aus der JSON-Hülle, sonst der Rohwert. */
export function readRcSn(bridge) {
  const raw = bridge.platformGetRemoteControllerSN();
  let sn = raw;
  let envelope = null;
  try {
    envelope = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    // kein JSON: Rohwert
  }
  if (envelope && typeof envelope === "object" && "code" in envelope) {
    if (envelope.code !== 0) throw new Error("platformGetRemoteControllerSN");
    sn = envelope.data;
  }
  sn = String(sn ?? "").trim();
  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9]{1,64}$/.test(sn)) throw new Error("Seriennummer der Fernsteuerung nicht lesbar");
  return sn;
}

export function verify(lic, bridge, log) {
  const call = caller(bridge, log);
  call("platformVerifyLicense", lic.appId, lic.appKey, lic.license);
  if (!call("platformIsVerified")) throw new Error("platformIsVerified");
}

export function connect(cfg, bridge, log) {
  const call = caller(bridge, log);
  call("platformSetWorkspaceId", cfg.workspaceId);
  call("platformSetInformation", "Suchlage", "DRK I&K", cfg.rcLabel);
  call("platformLoadComponent", "thing", JSON.stringify({
    host: cfg.mqttHost, username: cfg.mqttUser, password: cfg.mqttPass, connectCallback: "onThingConnect",
  }));
  call("platformLoadComponent", "liveshare", JSON.stringify({
    videoPublishType: "video-demand-aux-manual", statusCallback: "onLiveStatus",
  }));
}

/** Ganzer Ablauf. `post(path, body)` liefert `{ ok, body }`. Gibt die zusammengeführte Konfiguration zurück. */
export async function pair(code, bridge, log, post) {
  const lic = await post("/pilot/api/license", { code });
  if (!lic.ok) throw new PairingError(lic.body?.error ?? "Kopplung abgelehnt.");
  verify(lic.body, bridge, log);
  const rcSn = readRcSn(bridge);
  const conn = await post("/pilot/api/connect", { code, rc_sn: rcSn });
  if (!conn.ok) throw new PairingError(conn.body?.error ?? "Kopplung abgelehnt.");
  const cfg = { ...lic.body, ...conn.body };
  connect(cfg, bridge, log);
  return cfg;
}

export function startManualLive(cfg, bridge, log) {
  const call = caller(bridge, log);
  call("liveshareSetConfig", 2, JSON.stringify({ url: cfg.rtmpUrl }));
  call("liveshareStartLive");
}
```

`src/dji/pilot/pilot.test.mjs` ersetzt den M0-Test:

```js
import { test } from "node:test";
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { PairingError, connect, pair, readRcSn, startManualLive, verify } from "./pilot.js";

const lic = { appId: "1", appKey: "k", license: "l", workspaceId: "768d4a2e-1f34-477c-9fcc-872285e895f6", rcLabel: "RC Plus 1" };
const conn = { mqttHost: "tcp://127.0.0.1:1883", mqttUser: "rc-RCSN01", mqttPass: "p", rtmpUrl: "rtmp://127.0.0.1:1935/live/RCSN01?user=rc-RCSN01&pass=x" };

/** JSBridge-Attrappe: jede Antwort ist eine JSON-Hülle wie in Pilot 2. */
function fakeBridge({ failing = {}, data = {}, raw = {} } = {}) {
  const calls = [];
  const handler = { get: (_, name) => (...args) => {
    calls.push([name, ...args]);
    if (name in raw) return raw[name];
    if (failing[name]) return JSON.stringify({ code: -1, message: "nope" });
    return JSON.stringify({ code: 0, message: "", data: name in data ? data[name] : true });
  } };
  return { bridge: new Proxy({}, handler), calls };
}

function fakePost(answers) {
  const sent = [];
  const post = async (path, body) => { sent.push([path, body]); return answers[path]; };
  return { post, sent };
}

test("connect lädt Workspace, Information, thing und liveshare", () => {
  const { bridge, calls } = fakeBridge();
  connect({ ...lic, ...conn }, bridge, () => {});
  assert.deepEqual(calls.map((c) => c[0]), [
    "platformSetWorkspaceId", "platformSetInformation", "platformLoadComponent", "platformLoadComponent",
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(calls[1].slice(1), ["Suchlage", "DRK I&K", "RC Plus 1"]);
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(calls[2][2]), { host: conn.mqttHost, username: conn.mqttUser, password: "p", connectCallback: "onThingConnect" });
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(calls[3][2]).videoPublishType, "video-demand-aux-manual");
});

test("verify bricht bei abgelehnter Lizenz ab", () => {
  const { bridge, calls } = fakeBridge({ failing: { platformVerifyLicense: true } });
  const log = [];
  assert.throws(() => verify(lic, bridge, (m) => log.push(m)), /platformVerifyLicense/);
  assert.equal(calls.length, 1);
  assert.match(log[0], /FEHLER -1 nope/);
});

test("readRcSn liest data aus der Hülle, sonst den Rohwert, und prüft das Format", () => {
  assert.equal(readRcSn(fakeBridge({ data: { platformGetRemoteControllerSN: "RCSN01" } }).bridge), "RCSN01");
  assert.equal(readRcSn(fakeBridge({ raw: { platformGetRemoteControllerSN: "RCSN02" } }).bridge), "RCSN02");
  assert.throws(() => readRcSn(fakeBridge({ failing: { platformGetRemoteControllerSN: true } }).bridge), /platformGetRemoteControllerSN/);
  assert.throws(() => readRcSn(fakeBridge({ raw: { platformGetRemoteControllerSN: '{"x":1' } }).bridge), /nicht lesbar/);
});

test("pair prüft die Lizenz, bevor die Seriennummer gelesen wird, und sendet sie als reinen Text", async () => {
  const { bridge, calls } = fakeBridge({ data: { platformGetRemoteControllerSN: "RCSN01" } });
  const { post, sent } = fakePost({ "/pilot/api/license": { ok: true, body: lic }, "/pilot/api/connect": { ok: true, body: conn } });
  const cfg = await pair("abc", bridge, () => {}, post);
  const names = calls.map((c) => c[0]);
  assert.ok(names.indexOf("platformIsVerified") < names.indexOf("platformGetRemoteControllerSN"));
  assert.deepEqual(sent, [["/pilot/api/license", { code: "abc" }], ["/pilot/api/connect", { code: "abc", rc_sn: "RCSN01" }]]);
  assert.equal(cfg.mqttUser, "rc-RCSN01");
  assert.equal(names.at(-1), "platformLoadComponent");
});

test("pair zeigt die Ablehnung des Servers und ruft Pilot 2 dann nicht auf", async () => {
  const { bridge, calls } = fakeBridge();
  const { post } = fakePost({ "/pilot/api/license": { ok: false, body: { error: "Kopplungscode ungültig" } } });
  await assert.rejects(pair("falsch", bridge, () => {}, post), (e) => e instanceof PairingError && e.message === "Kopplungscode ungültig");
  assert.equal(calls.length, 0);
});

test("startManualLive setzt RTMP und startet", () => {
  const { bridge, calls } = fakeBridge();
  startManualLive(conn, bridge, () => {});
  assert.deepEqual(calls[0], ["liveshareSetConfig", 2, JSON.stringify({ url: conn.rtmpUrl })]);
  assert.equal(calls[1][0], "liveshareStartLive");
});
```

`src/dji/pilot/index.html` bleibt Oberfläche; der Ablauf steht in `pair`:

```html
<!doctype html>
<html lang="de">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <title>Suchlage – Pilot</title>
  <style>
    body { font: 17px system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0; background: #08090b; color: #e8ebee; }
    header { background: #0c0e11; padding: 14px 16px; border-bottom: 1px solid #22262b; font-weight: 600; }
    main { padding: 16px; max-width: 720px; }
    button { font: inherit; font-weight: 600; padding: 14px 22px; margin: 0 8px 12px 0; border: 1px solid #626a73; background: #14171b; color: #e8ebee; border-radius: 0; }
    button.primary { background: #4d94d6; border-color: #4d94d6; color: #08090b; }
    #state { font-size: 20px; margin: 8px 0 16px; }
    #log { white-space: pre-wrap; font: 13px ui-monospace, monospace; background: #0f1215; border: 1px solid #22262b; padding: 8px; color: #9aa2ab; }
  </style>
</head>
<body>
  <header>Suchlage – Pilot 2 verbinden</header>
  <main>
    <div id="state">Wird vorbereitet …</div>
    <button id="connect" class="primary">Verbinden</button>
    <button id="live">Livebild manuell starten</button>
    <div id="log"></div>
  </main>
  <script type="module">
    import { PairingError, connect, pair, startManualLive } from "./pilot/pilot.js";
    const out = document.getElementById("log");
    const stateEl = document.getElementById("state");
    const log = (m) => { out.textContent += `${new Date().toLocaleTimeString("de-DE")} ${m}\n`; };
    const show = (m) => { stateEl.textContent = m; };
    const bridge = window.djiBridge;
    const code = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get("code");
    const post = async (path, body) => {
      const res = await fetch(path, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
      return { ok: res.ok, body: await res.json().catch(() => ({})) };
    };
    let cfg = null;
    window.onThingConnect = (s) => { log(`MQTT: ${s}`); show(String(s) === "true" ? "Mit Suchlage verbunden." : "Verbindung zu Suchlage getrennt."); };
    window.onLiveStatus = (s) => log(`Livebild: ${s}`);

    async function run(action) {
      if (!bridge) return show("Diese Seite in DJI Pilot 2 unter Cloud-Dienst öffnen.");
      if (!code) return show("Kopplungscode fehlt. Den Link aus Suchlage (Einstellungen → Fernsteuerungen) verwenden.");
      try {
        const fresh = !cfg;
        cfg = cfg ?? (await pair(code, bridge, log, post)); // pair verbindet beim ersten Mal selbst
        if (action === "connect" && !fresh) connect(cfg, bridge, log);
        if (action === "live") startManualLive(cfg, bridge, log);
        show(`Angemeldet als ${cfg.rcLabel}.`);
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof PairingError) return show(e.message);
        log(`Abbruch bei ${e.message}`);
        show("Anmeldung fehlgeschlagen – Protokoll unten.");
      }
    }
    document.getElementById("connect").onclick = () => run("connect");
    document.getElementById("live").onclick = () => run("live");
    run("connect");
  </script>
</body>
</html>
```

Der Import lautet `./pilot/pilot.js`, weil die Seite unter `/pilot` liegt. Relativ zu `/pilot` ergibt das `/pilot/pilot.js`.

`src/auth/dev.rs`, nur mit Feature `dev`: die Fernsteuerung des Simulators mit festem Code. So braucht `scripts/dev.sh` keine Kopplungslogik, und ein früher Start vor der Broker-Verbindung legt nichts doppelt an.

```rust
/// Fester Kopplungscode der Simulator-Fernsteuerung. Existiert nur mit Feature `dev`; ohne das
/// Feature kennt der Server keinen Code, den er nicht selbst zufällig erzeugt hat.
pub const SIMULATOR_CODE: &str = "dev-simulator";

pub async fn seed_simulator(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool) -> Result<(), crate::error::AppError> {
    use crate::store::remote_controllers as rcs;
    if rcs::find_by_code(pool, SIMULATOR_CODE).await?.is_none() {
        rcs::create_with_code(pool, "Simulator", SIMULATOR_CODE, now_ms()).await?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn simulator_rc_is_seeded_once() {
        let pool = crate::db::test_pool().await;
        super::seed_simulator(&pool).await.unwrap();
        super::seed_simulator(&pool).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(crate::store::remote_controllers::list(&pool).await.unwrap().len(), 1);
        assert!(crate::store::remote_controllers::find_by_code(&pool, super::SIMULATOR_CODE).await.unwrap().is_some());
    }
}
```

`app.rs`, in `run` direkt nach `crate::auth::dev::seed(&settings)` (ebenfalls unter `#[cfg(feature = "dev")]`): `crate::auth::dev::seed_simulator(&pool).await.map_err(|e| std::io::Error::other(e.to_string()))?;`

`endpoints_without_valid_code_return_no_secrets` schickt den Simulator-Code mit. Im Test-Router wird nie geseedet; ohne Feature `dev` gibt es den Code gar nicht. Am Release-Image prüft das I3.

### Task B15: MediaMTX – Auth-Hook, WHEP-, HLS- und Playback-Proxy

**Strang:** backend

**Files:**
- Create: `src/media/mod.rs`, `src/media/hook.rs`, `src/media/proxy.rs`, `src/web/media.rs`
- Modify: `src/web/flights.rs` (Video im Detail, Route `/flights/{id}/video`), `src/app.rs` (`AppState.http`), `src/testing.rs`, `src/lib.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SettingsHandle` (`media`, `internal_secret`), `store::{remote_controllers, flights}`, `Viewer`/`Operator`
- Produces:
  - `media::hook::{AuthRequest, decide(&AuthRequest, internal_secret: &str, stream_key: Option<&str>) -> bool, INTERNAL_USER = "suchlage-internal"}`
  - `media::proxy::{whep(state, gateway_sn, sdp: Bytes) -> Result<Response, AppError>, hls(state, gateway_sn, file, query) -> Result<Response, AppError>, flight_video(state, &FlightSummary, download: bool, range: Option<HeaderValue>) -> Result<Response, AppError>, flight_video_available(state, &FlightSummary) -> bool, safe_file(&str) -> bool}`
  - Das Flugvideo beginnt bei `max(Flugstart, Beginn der ersten Aufnahme)`: Das Auto-Livebild startet erst nach dem Abheben, `/get` ab Flugstart fände je nach MediaMTX-Version nichts. `Range` geht an MediaMTX, `Accept-Ranges` und `Content-Range` zurück; bietet MediaMTX kein Range an, spult man im heruntergeladenen Video.
  - `media::http_client() -> reqwest::Client`: Verbindungsaufbau 3 s, höchstens 3 Redirects
  - Routen:
    - `POST /internal/media/auth` (MediaMTX)
    - `POST /api/video/{gateway_sn}/whep` (Viewer)
    - `GET /api/video/{gateway_sn}/hls/{*file}` (Viewer)
    - `GET /api/flights/{id}/video?download=0|1` (Operator)
  - `FlightDetail.video` ist gesetzt, wenn der Flug beendet ist und MediaMTX für das Zeitfenster eine Aufnahme meldet.
  - `AppState.http: reqwest::Client`

```rust
//! Entscheidung für MediaMTX `authMethod: http` (ADR 0003). Publish nur die eigene RC auf
//! ihrem Pfad, Lesen und Playback nur der interne Leser (der Server-Proxy).

use serde::Deserialize;

use crate::dji::topic::is_valid_sn;

pub const INTERNAL_USER: &str = "suchlage-internal";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize)]
#[serde(default)]
pub struct AuthRequest {
    pub user: String,
    pub password: String,
    pub action: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub protocol: String,
    pub query: String,
}

impl AuthRequest {
    /// RTMP überträgt Zugangsdaten als `?user=…&pass=…` (M0).
    pub fn credentials(&self) -> (String, String) {
        if !self.user.is_empty() {
            return (self.user.clone(), self.password.clone());
        }
        let mut user = String::new();
        let mut pass = String::new();
        for (k, v) in url::form_urlencoded::parse(self.query.trim_start_matches('?').as_bytes()) {
            match k.as_ref() {
                "user" => user = v.into_owned(),
                "pass" => pass = v.into_owned(),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        (user, pass)
    }

    /// SN aus `live/<SN>`, sonst `None`.
    pub fn gateway_sn(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        self.path.strip_prefix("live/").filter(|sn| is_valid_sn(sn))
    }
}

pub fn decide(req: &AuthRequest, internal_secret: &str, stream_key: Option<&str>) -> bool {
    todo!()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn req(action: &str, path: &str, user: &str, password: &str) -> AuthRequest {
        AuthRequest { action: action.into(), path: path.into(), user: user.into(), password: password.into(), ..Default::default() }
    }

    #[test]
    fn rc_may_publish_only_on_its_own_path_with_its_key() {
        assert!(decide(&req("publish", "live/RC1", "rc-RC1", "key"), "s", Some("key")));
        assert!(!decide(&req("publish", "live/RC1", "rc-RC1", "falsch"), "s", Some("key")));
        assert!(!decide(&req("publish", "live/RC1", "rc-RC2", "key"), "s", Some("key")));
        assert!(!decide(&req("publish", "live/RC1", "rc-RC1", ""), "s", Some("")), "leerer Schlüssel gilt nie");
        assert!(!decide(&req("publish", "andere/RC1", "rc-RC1", "key"), "s", Some("key")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn rtmp_query_credentials_are_understood() {
        let r = AuthRequest { action: "publish".into(), path: "live/RC1".into(), query: "user=rc-RC1&pass=key".into(), ..Default::default() };
        assert!(decide(&r, "s", Some("key")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn only_the_internal_reader_may_read_or_play_back() {
        assert!(decide(&req("read", "live/RC1", INTERNAL_USER, "s"), "s", None));
        assert!(decide(&req("playback", "live/RC1", INTERNAL_USER, "s"), "s", None));
        assert!(!decide(&req("read", "live/RC1", "", ""), "s", None));
        assert!(!decide(&req("read", "live/RC1", INTERNAL_USER, "falsch"), "s", None));
        assert!(!decide(&req("api", "", INTERNAL_USER, "s"), "s", None));
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Video über MediaMTX: Auth-Hook und Proxies für WHEP, HLS und Playback (ADR 0003).

pub mod hook;
pub mod proxy;

pub fn http_client() -> reqwest::Client {
    reqwest::Client::builder()
        .connect_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3))
        // MediaMTX leitet die erste HLS-Anfrage auf ?cookieCheck=1 um (M0-Befund); über http fällt es
        // für die Sitzung auf den Query-Parameter zurück, den die Playlist selbst mitführt.
        .redirect(reqwest::redirect::Policy::limited(3))
        .build()
        .expect("HTTP-Client baubar")
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Entscheidung implementieren**

```rust
pub fn decide(req: &AuthRequest, internal_secret: &str, stream_key: Option<&str>) -> bool {
    let (user, password) = req.credentials();
    match req.action.as_str() {
        "publish" => match (req.gateway_sn(), stream_key) {
            (Some(sn), Some(key)) => !key.is_empty() && user == format!("rc-{sn}") && password == key,
            _ => false,
        },
        "read" | "playback" => !internal_secret.is_empty() && user == INTERNAL_USER && password == internal_secret,
        _ => false,
    }
}
```

```rust
use axum::body::Bytes;
use axum::extract::{Path, RawQuery, State};
use axum::http::StatusCode;
use axum::response::Response;
use axum::routing::{get, post};
use axum::{Json, Router};

use crate::app::AppState;
use crate::auth::session::Viewer;
use crate::dji::topic::is_valid_sn;
use crate::error::AppError;
use crate::media::{hook, proxy};
use crate::store;

/// Unter `/api`.
pub fn routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new().route("/video/{gateway_sn}/whep", post(whep)).route("/video/{gateway_sn}/hls/{*file}", get(hls))
}

/// Außerhalb von `/api`; in Traefik nicht nach außen routen (ADR 0003).
pub fn internal_routes() -> Router<AppState> {
    Router::new().route("/internal/media/auth", post(auth_hook))
}

async fn auth_hook(State(state): State<AppState>, Json(req): Json<hook::AuthRequest>) -> StatusCode {
    todo!()
}

async fn whep(_: Viewer, State(state): State<AppState>, Path(sn): Path<String>, body: Bytes) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
    if !is_valid_sn(&sn) {
        return Err(AppError::NotFound);
    }
    proxy::whep(&state, &sn, body).await
}

async fn hls(_: Viewer, State(state): State<AppState>, Path((sn, file)): Path<(String, String)>, RawQuery(query): RawQuery) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
    if !is_valid_sn(&sn) {
        return Err(AppError::NotFound);
    }
    proxy::hls(&state, &sn, &file, query.as_deref().unwrap_or("")).await
}

mod tests {
    use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

    use axum::body::Body;
    use axum::http::{HeaderMap, Request, StatusCode};
    use axum::response::IntoResponse;
    use http_body_util::BodyExt;
    use tower::ServiceExt;

    use crate::contract::Role;
    use crate::testing::{TestApp, get, post_json, send, test_app, with_cookie};

    /// MediaMTX-Double: WHEP, HLS mit Cookie-Check-Umleitung, Playback /list und /get.
    async fn fake_mediamtx(seen_auth: Arc<Mutex<Vec<String>>>) -> String {
        let s1 = seen_auth.clone();
        let s2 = seen_auth;
        let record = |h: &HeaderMap, seen: &Arc<Mutex<Vec<String>>>| {
            seen.lock().unwrap().push(h.get("authorization").map(|v| v.to_str().unwrap().to_string()).unwrap_or_default());
        };
        let app = axum::Router::new()
            .route("/live/RC1/whep", axum::routing::post(move |h: HeaderMap, body: String| {
                let s = s1.clone();
                async move {
                    record(&h, &s);
                    assert!(body.starts_with("v=0"));
                    (StatusCode::CREATED, [("content-type", "application/sdp"), ("location", "/live/RC1/whep/abc")], "v=0 answer").into_response()
                }
            }))
            .route("/live/RC1/index.m3u8", axum::routing::get(move |h: HeaderMap, q: axum::extract::RawQuery| {
                let s = s2.clone();
                async move {
                    record(&h, &s);
                    if q.0.as_deref() != Some("cookieCheck=1") {
                        return (StatusCode::FOUND, [("location", "/live/RC1/index.m3u8?cookieCheck=1"), ("set-cookie", "cookieCheck=1; Secure")]).into_response();
                    }
                    ([("content-type", "application/vnd.apple.mpegurl")], "#EXTM3U\nmain_stream.m3u8?session=x\n").into_response()
                }
            }))
            // Aufnahme beginnt 30 s nach dem Flugstart (Auto-Livebild nach dem Abheben).
            .route("/list", axum::routing::get(|| async { axum::Json(serde_json::json!([{"start": "2026-10-16T11:27:10Z", "duration": 1050.0}])) }))
            .route("/get", axum::routing::get(|h: HeaderMap, q: axum::extract::RawQuery| async move {
                let range = h.get("range").map(|v| v.to_str().unwrap().to_string()).unwrap_or_default();
                ([("content-type", "video/mp4"), ("accept-ranges", "bytes")], format!("MP4 {} range={range}", q.0.unwrap_or_default())).into_response()
            }));
        let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
        let base = format!("http://{}", listener.local_addr().unwrap());
        tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap() });
        base
    }

    async fn with_media(app: &TestApp, base: &str) {
        app.state.settings.update_media(crate::contract::MediaSettings { webrtc_url: base.into(), hls_url: base.into(), playback_url: base.into(), rtmp_url: "rtmp://127.0.0.1:1935".into() }).await.unwrap();
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn hook_allows_the_rc_publish_and_the_internal_reader() {
        let app = test_app().await;
        let (rc, _) = crate::store::remote_controllers::create(&app.state.pool, "RC", 0).await.unwrap();
        crate::store::remote_controllers::bind(&app.state.pool, rc.id, "RC1", "pw", "key", 1).await.unwrap();
        let ok = serde_json::json!({"action": "publish", "path": "live/RC1", "protocol": "rtmp", "query": "user=rc-RC1&pass=key", "user": "", "password": ""});
        assert_eq!(send(&app.router, post_json("/internal/media/auth", ok)).await.0, StatusCode::OK);
        let bad = serde_json::json!({"action": "publish", "path": "live/RC1", "user": "rc-RC1", "password": "nein"});
        assert_eq!(send(&app.router, post_json("/internal/media/auth", bad)).await.0, StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED);
        let secret = app.state.settings.current().internal_secret;
        let read = serde_json::json!({"action": "read", "path": "live/RC1", "user": "suchlage-internal", "password": secret});
        assert_eq!(send(&app.router, post_json("/internal/media/auth", read)).await.0, StatusCode::OK);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn whep_is_proxied_with_internal_credentials_and_kiosk_may_watch() {
        let seen = Arc::new(Mutex::new(vec![]));
        let base = fake_mediamtx(seen.clone()).await;
        let app = test_app().await;
        with_media(&app, &base).await;
        let (_, secret) = crate::store::kiosk::create(&app.state.pool, "FüKw", 0).await.unwrap();
        let tid = crate::store::kiosk::find_by_token(&app.state.pool, &secret).await.unwrap().unwrap();
        let kiosk = format!("suchlage_sid={}", crate::store::sessions::create_for_kiosk(&app.state.pool, tid, crate::time::now_ms()).await.unwrap());

        let req = Request::post("/api/video/RC1/whep").header("content-type", "application/sdp").body(Body::from("v=0 offer")).unwrap();
        let res = app.router.clone().oneshot(with_cookie(req, &kiosk)).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(res.status(), StatusCode::CREATED);
        assert_eq!(res.headers()["content-type"], "application/sdp");
        assert!(res.headers().get("location").is_none(), "interne Adresse bleibt intern");
        assert_eq!(res.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes(), "v=0 answer");
        let header = seen.lock().unwrap()[0].clone();
        assert!(header.starts_with("Basic "), "{header}");

        let unauth = Request::post("/api/video/RC1/whep").body(Body::from("v=0")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(app.router.clone().oneshot(unauth).await.unwrap().status(), StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn hls_follows_the_cookie_check_and_strips_cookies() {
        let base = fake_mediamtx(Arc::new(Mutex::new(vec![]))).await;
        let app = test_app().await;
        with_media(&app, &base).await;
        app.cookie(Role::Admin).await;
        let c = app.cookie(Role::Operator).await;
        let res = app.router.clone().oneshot(with_cookie(get("/api/video/RC1/hls/index.m3u8"), &c)).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(res.status(), StatusCode::OK);
        assert!(res.headers().get("set-cookie").is_none());
        let body = res.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes();
        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&body).contains("main_stream.m3u8?session=x"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn hls_file_names_cannot_escape_the_stream_directory() {
        assert!(crate::media::proxy::safe_file("main_stream.m3u8"));
        assert!(crate::media::proxy::safe_file("seg_12.mp4"));
        assert!(!crate::media::proxy::safe_file("../secret"));
        assert!(!crate::media::proxy::safe_file("a//b"));
        assert!(!crate::media::proxy::safe_file("x?y"));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn landed_flight_has_video_from_the_playback_window() {
        let base = fake_mediamtx(Arc::new(Mutex::new(vec![]))).await;
        let app = test_app().await;
        with_media(&app, &base).await;
        let flight = crate::contract::examples::flight_landed(4, 1_792_150_000_000);
        crate::store::flights::insert(&app.state.pool, &flight).await.unwrap();
        let admin = app.cookie(Role::Admin).await;
        let (_, detail) = send(&app.router, with_cookie(get("/api/flights/4"), &admin)).await;
        assert_eq!(detail["video"]["download_url"], "/api/flights/4/video?download=1");
        let res = app.router.clone().oneshot(with_cookie(get("/api/flights/4/video?download=1"), &admin)).await.unwrap();
        assert!(res.headers()["content-disposition"].to_str().unwrap().contains("attachment"));
        let body = String::from_utf8(res.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes().to_vec()).unwrap();
        assert!(body.contains("path=live%2FRCSIM0001") && body.contains("format=mp4"), "{body}");
        assert!(body.contains("start=2026-10-16T11%3A27%3A10Z") && body.contains("duration=1050"), "beginnt mit der Aufnahme, nicht mit dem Flug: {body}");

        let ranged = Request::get("/api/flights/4/video").header("range", "bytes=100-").body(Body::empty()).unwrap();
        let res = app.router.clone().oneshot(with_cookie(ranged, &admin)).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(res.headers()["accept-ranges"], "bytes");
        let body = String::from_utf8(res.into_body().collect().await.unwrap().to_bytes().to_vec()).unwrap();
        assert!(body.contains("range=bytes=100-"), "Range geht an MediaMTX: {body}");
    }
}
```

```rust
use axum::body::{Body, Bytes};
use axum::http::{HeaderValue, StatusCode, header};
use axum::response::Response;

use crate::app::AppState;
use crate::contract::{FlightSummary, MediaSettings};
use crate::error::AppError;

use super::hook::INTERNAL_USER;

fn media(state: &AppState) -> Result<MediaSettings, AppError> {
    state.settings.current().media.ok_or_else(|| AppError::Unavailable("MediaMTX ist nicht eingerichtet".into()))
}

fn unreachable_media(e: reqwest::Error) -> AppError {
    tracing::warn!(error = %e, "MediaMTX nicht erreichbar");
    AppError::Unavailable("Videoserver nicht erreichbar".into())
}

/// Erlaubt nur `[A-Za-z0-9._-]`-Segmente, nie `..`.
pub fn safe_file(file: &str) -> bool {
    !file.is_empty() && file.split('/').all(|seg| !seg.is_empty() && seg != ".." && seg.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"._-".contains(&b)))
}
```

```rust
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct VideoQuery {
    #[serde(default)]
    download: u8,
}

async fn video(
    _: Operator,
    State(state): State<AppState>,
    Path(id): Path<i64>,
    axum::extract::Query(q): axum::extract::Query<VideoQuery>,
    headers: axum::http::HeaderMap,
) -> Result<axum::response::Response, AppError> {
    let flight = store::flights::get(&state.pool, id).await?.ok_or(AppError::NotFound)?;
    let range = headers.get(axum::http::header::RANGE).cloned();
    crate::media::proxy::flight_video(&state, &flight, q.download == 1, range).await
}

pub async fn detail(state: &AppState, id: i64) -> Result<FlightDetail, AppError> {
    let flight = store::flights::get(&state.pool, id).await?.ok_or(AppError::NotFound)?;
    let segments = store::flights::segments(&state.pool, id).await?;
    let has_video = flight.ended_at_ms.is_some() && crate::media::proxy::flight_video_available(state, &flight).await;
    let video = has_video.then(|| crate::contract::FlightVideo {
        play_url: format!("/api/flights/{id}/video"),
        download_url: format!("/api/flights/{id}/video?download=1"),
    });
    Ok(FlightDetail { flight, segments, video })
}
```

```rust
async fn auth_hook(State(state): State<AppState>, Json(req): Json<hook::AuthRequest>) -> StatusCode {
    let key = match (req.action.as_str(), req.gateway_sn()) {
        ("publish", Some(sn)) => store::remote_controllers::find_by_sn(&state.pool, sn).await.ok().flatten().and_then(|rc| rc.stream_key),
        _ => None,
    };
    if hook::decide(&req, &state.settings.current().internal_secret, key.as_deref()) {
        StatusCode::OK
    } else {
        tracing::debug!(action = %req.action, path = %req.path, protocol = %req.protocol, "MediaMTX-Zugriff abgelehnt");
        StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED
    }
}
```

`media/proxy.rs`:

```rust
fn passthrough(res: reqwest::Response, extra: Option<(header::HeaderName, String)>) -> Response {
    let status = StatusCode::from_u16(res.status().as_u16()).unwrap_or(StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY);
    let mut out = Response::builder().status(status);
    for name in [header::CONTENT_TYPE, header::CACHE_CONTROL, header::CONTENT_LENGTH, header::ACCEPT_RANGES, header::CONTENT_RANGE] {
        if let Some(v) = res.headers().get(name.as_str()).and_then(|v| HeaderValue::from_bytes(v.as_bytes()).ok()) {
            out = out.header(name, v);
        }
    }
    if let Some((name, value)) = extra {
        out = out.header(name, value);
    }
    // Set-Cookie und Location von MediaMTX gehen bewusst nicht an den Browser.
    out.body(Body::from_stream(res.bytes_stream())).unwrap()
}

fn internal(state: &AppState, req: reqwest::RequestBuilder) -> reqwest::RequestBuilder {
    req.basic_auth(INTERNAL_USER, Some(state.settings.current().internal_secret))
}

pub async fn whep(state: &AppState, gateway_sn: &str, sdp: Bytes) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
    let m = media(state)?;
    let req = state.http.post(format!("{}/live/{gateway_sn}/whep", m.webrtc_url)).header("content-type", "application/sdp").timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10)).body(sdp);
    let res = internal(state, req).send().await.map_err(unreachable_media)?;
    if res.status() == reqwest::StatusCode::NOT_FOUND {
        return Err(AppError::NotFound);
    }
    // shortcut: ohne Trickle-ICE (alle Kandidaten im Offer), daher kein PATCH/DELETE-Proxy; MediaMTX räumt Sitzungen selbst ab.
    Ok(passthrough(res, None))
}

pub async fn hls(state: &AppState, gateway_sn: &str, file: &str, query: &str) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
    if !safe_file(file) {
        return Err(AppError::NotFound);
    }
    let m = media(state)?;
    let mut url = format!("{}/live/{gateway_sn}/{file}", m.hls_url);
    if !query.is_empty() {
        url.push('?');
        url.push_str(query);
    }
    let res = internal(state, state.http.get(url).timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))).send().await.map_err(unreachable_media)?;
    Ok(passthrough(res, None))
}

fn rfc3339(ms: i64) -> String {
    chrono::DateTime::from_timestamp_millis(ms).unwrap_or_default().to_rfc3339_opts(chrono::SecondsFormat::Secs, true)
}

/// Was MediaMTX im Zeitfenster des Flugs aufgezeichnet hat: Beginn (ms) und Dauer (s). Beginnt bei
/// `max(Flugstart, erste Aufnahme)`, denn das Livebild startet erst nach dem Abheben.
async fn recording_window(state: &AppState, m: &MediaSettings, flight: &FlightSummary) -> Option<(i64, i64)> {
    let end = flight.ended_at_ms?;
    let query = [("path", format!("live/{}", flight.gateway_sn)), ("start", rfc3339(flight.started_at_ms)), ("end", rfc3339(end))];
    let req = state.http.get(format!("{}/list", m.playback_url)).query(&query).timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5));
    let res = internal(state, req).send().await.ok().filter(|r| r.status().is_success())?;
    let list = res.json::<Vec<serde_json::Value>>().await.ok()?;
    let first = list.first()?["start"].as_str().and_then(|s| chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(s).ok())?.timestamp_millis();
    let start = flight.started_at_ms.max(first);
    (end > start).then(|| (start, ((end - start) / 1000).max(1)))
}

pub async fn flight_video_available(state: &AppState, flight: &FlightSummary) -> bool {
    let Ok(m) = media(state) else { return false };
    recording_window(state, &m, flight).await.is_some()
}

pub async fn flight_video(state: &AppState, flight: &FlightSummary, download: bool, range: Option<HeaderValue>) -> Result<Response, AppError> {
    let m = media(state)?;
    if flight.ended_at_ms.is_none() {
        return Err(AppError::Unprocessable("Flug läuft noch – das Video gibt es nach der Landung".into()));
    }
    let (start, duration) = recording_window(state, &m, flight).await.ok_or(AppError::NotFound)?;
    let query = [("path", format!("live/{}", flight.gateway_sn)), ("start", rfc3339(start)), ("duration", duration.to_string()), ("format", "mp4".to_string())];
    let mut req = internal(state, state.http.get(format!("{}/get", m.playback_url)).query(&query));
    if let Some(range) = range {
        req = req.header(header::RANGE, range);
    }
    let res = req.send().await.map_err(unreachable_media)?;
    if !res.status().is_success() {
        return Err(AppError::NotFound);
    }
    let disposition = if download { format!("attachment; filename=\"suchlage-flug-{}.mp4\"", flight.id) } else { "inline".into() };
    Ok(passthrough(res, Some((header::CONTENT_DISPOSITION, disposition))))
}
```

Expected: grün (der Filter trifft `media::` und `web::media::`). `duration=1050` folgt aus `flight_landed` (1 080 000 ms) minus den 30 s bis zur ersten Aufnahme im Double.

### Task B16: Simulator `suchlage-sim`

**Strang:** backend

Der Simulator verhält sich wie Pilot 2 auf einer gekoppelten RC Plus. Er holt seine Zugangsdaten über `/pilot/api/connect` (die Lizenz braucht er nicht), meldet sich am Broker an und spielt einen Flug ab: synthetisch oder einen Mitschnitt. Ist der Server noch nicht bereit (Verbindungsfehler oder 503, etwa weil die Broker-Sitzung noch aufgebaut wird), versucht er es mit wachsendem Abstand bis zu 60 s lang erneut; bei 401 oder 409 bricht er mit der Meldung des Servers ab. Livebild-Befehle beantwortet er und sendet dann per ffmpeg ein Testbild an MediaMTX. So läuft der ganze Weg ohne Hardware: Kopplung, ACL, Telemetrie, Flugerkennung, Livebild und Aufzeichnung.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/sim/fake_pilot.rs`, `src/bin/suchlage-sim.rs`
- Modify: `src/sim/mod.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sim::synth::{Scenario, generate}` (B2), `dji::record::{RecordLine, read_lines}` (B1), Pilot-API (B14)
- Produces:
  - `sim::fake_pilot::{Action, handle_service(payload: &[u8]) -> Option<(Vec<u8>, Action)>, rewrite_gateway(line: &RecordLine, from: &str, to: &str) -> RecordLine, Args, parse_args(impl Iterator<Item = String>) -> Result<Args, String>, ConnectOutcome, classify_connect(status: Option<u16>) -> ConnectOutcome, retry_delays() -> Vec<Duration>}`
  - Aufruf: `suchlage-sim --server <url> --code <kopplungscode> [--rc-sn SIMRC0001] [--aircraft-sn SIMAC0001] [--replay <datei.jsonl>] [--speed 1.0] [--loop] [--no-video]`

```rust
//! Pilot-2-Attrappe für den Simulator: beantwortet Livebild-Services wie Pilot 2 und schreibt
//! Mitschnitte auf die Seriennummer der gekoppelten Fernsteuerung um.

use serde_json::{Value, json};

use crate::dji::record::RecordLine;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum Action {
    StartVideo { url: String, video_id: String },
    StopVideo,
    ChangeLens { video_type: String },
    None,
}

/// Antwort (`services_reply`-Nutzlast) und was der Simulator tun soll.
pub fn handle_service(payload: &[u8]) -> Option<(Vec<u8>, Action)> {
    todo!()
}

pub fn rewrite_gateway(line: &RecordLine, from: &str, to: &str) -> RecordLine {
    RecordLine { topic: line.topic.replace(from, to), payload: line.payload.replace(from, to), ..line.clone() }
}

/// Was der Simulator mit einer Antwort von `/pilot/api/connect` tut. `None`: Server nicht erreichbar.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ConnectOutcome {
    Ready,
    Retry,
    GiveUp,
}

pub fn classify_connect(status: Option<u16>) -> ConnectOutcome {
    match status {
        Some(s) if (200..300).contains(&s) => ConnectOutcome::Ready,
        // Server startet noch oder die Broker-Sitzung steht noch nicht.
        None | Some(502..=504) => ConnectOutcome::Retry,
        _ => ConnectOutcome::GiveUp,
    }
}

/// 0,5 s, 1 s, 2 s, 4 s, dann alle 5 s – zusammen gut 60 s.
pub fn retry_delays() -> Vec<std::time::Duration> {
    let mut d: Vec<u64> = vec![500, 1_000, 2_000, 4_000];
    while d.iter().sum::<u64>() < 60_000 {
        d.push(5_000);
    }
    d.into_iter().map(std::time::Duration::from_millis).collect()
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Args {
    pub server: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub rc_sn: String,
    pub aircraft_sn: String,
    pub replay: Option<String>,
    pub speed: f64,
    pub repeat: bool,
    pub video: bool,
}

pub fn parse_args(mut it: impl Iterator<Item = String>) -> Result<Args, String> {
    todo!()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn call(method: &str, data: Value) -> Vec<u8> {
        json!({"tid": "t1", "bid": "b1", "timestamp": 1, "method": method, "data": data}).to_string().into_bytes()
    }

    #[test]
    fn start_push_is_acknowledged_and_starts_video() {
        let (reply, action) = handle_service(&call("live_start_push", json!({"url_type": 1, "url": "rtmp://h/live/RC1?user=rc-RC1&pass=k", "video_id": "AC1/61-0-0/normal-0", "video_quality": 0}))).unwrap();
        let v: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&reply).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((v["tid"].as_str(), v["bid"].as_str(), v["method"].as_str(), v["data"]["result"].as_i64()), (Some("t1"), Some("b1"), Some("live_start_push"), Some(0)));
        assert_eq!(action, Action::StartVideo { url: "rtmp://h/live/RC1?user=rc-RC1&pass=k".into(), video_id: "AC1/61-0-0/normal-0".into() });
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_rtmp_start_is_refused_like_pilot_would() {
        let (reply, action) = handle_service(&call("live_start_push", json!({"url_type": 0, "url": "x", "video_id": "v"}))).unwrap();
        let v: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&reply).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["data"]["result"], 513013);
        assert_eq!(action, Action::None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn stop_and_lens_change_are_acknowledged() {
        assert_eq!(handle_service(&call("live_stop_push", json!({"video_id": "v"}))).unwrap().1, Action::StopVideo);
        assert_eq!(handle_service(&call("live_lens_change", json!({"video_id": "v", "video_type": "thermal"}))).unwrap().1, Action::ChangeLens { video_type: "thermal".into() });
        assert!(handle_service(b"kaputt").is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn replay_is_rewritten_to_the_paired_serial_number() {
        let line = RecordLine::new(1, "thing/product/RCSIM0001/osd", br#"{"gateway":"RCSIM0001","data":{}}"#);
        let out = rewrite_gateway(&line, "RCSIM0001", "RCREAL01");
        assert_eq!(out.topic, "thing/product/RCREAL01/osd");
        assert!(out.payload.contains("\"gateway\":\"RCREAL01\""));
    }

    #[test]
    fn connect_retries_only_while_the_server_is_not_ready() {
        assert_eq!(classify_connect(None), ConnectOutcome::Retry);
        assert_eq!(classify_connect(Some(503)), ConnectOutcome::Retry);
        assert_eq!(classify_connect(Some(200)), ConnectOutcome::Ready);
        assert_eq!(classify_connect(Some(401)), ConnectOutcome::GiveUp, "falscher Code wird nicht besser");
        assert_eq!(classify_connect(Some(409)), ConnectOutcome::GiveUp);
        let total: u128 = retry_delays().iter().map(|d| d.as_millis()).sum();
        assert!((60_000..70_000).contains(&total), "{total}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn args_need_server_and_code() {
        let a = parse_args(["--server", "http://127.0.0.1:8080", "--code", "abc", "--speed", "4", "--loop"].map(String::from).into_iter()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((a.speed, a.repeat, a.video, a.rc_sn.as_str()), (4.0, true, true, "SIMRC0001"));
        assert!(parse_args(["--server", "x"].map(String::from).into_iter()).unwrap_err().contains("--code"));
    }
}
```

```rust
pub fn handle_service(payload: &[u8]) -> Option<(Vec<u8>, Action)> {
    let env: Value = serde_json::from_slice(payload).ok()?;
    let method = env["method"].as_str()?.to_string();
    let data = &env["data"];
    let (result, action) = match method.as_str() {
        // Wie Pilot 2: über MQTT nur RTMP (url_type 1), sonst „Protokoll nicht unterstützt“.
        "live_start_push" if data["url_type"] == 1 => (0, Action::StartVideo { url: data["url"].as_str()?.into(), video_id: data["video_id"].as_str()?.into() }),
        "live_start_push" => (513013, Action::None),
        "live_stop_push" => (0, Action::StopVideo),
        "live_lens_change" => (0, Action::ChangeLens { video_type: data["video_type"].as_str()?.into() }),
        _ => (513099, Action::None),
    };
    let reply = json!({ "tid": env["tid"], "bid": env["bid"], "timestamp": env["timestamp"], "method": method, "data": { "result": result } });
    Some((reply.to_string().into_bytes(), action))
}

pub fn parse_args(mut it: impl Iterator<Item = String>) -> Result<Args, String> {
    let mut a = Args { server: String::new(), code: String::new(), rc_sn: "SIMRC0001".into(), aircraft_sn: "SIMAC0001".into(), replay: None, speed: 1.0, repeat: false, video: true };
    while let Some(flag) = it.next() {
        let mut value = || it.next().ok_or_else(|| format!("{flag} braucht einen Wert"));
        match flag.as_str() {
            "--server" => a.server = value()?,
            "--code" => a.code = value()?,
            "--rc-sn" => a.rc_sn = value()?,
            "--aircraft-sn" => a.aircraft_sn = value()?,
            "--replay" => a.replay = Some(value()?),
            "--speed" => a.speed = value()?.parse().map_err(|_| "--speed: Zahl erwartet".to_string())?,
            "--loop" => a.repeat = true,
            "--no-video" => a.video = false,
            other => return Err(format!("unbekannte Option {other}")),
        }
    }
    if a.server.is_empty() || a.code.is_empty() {
        return Err("Aufruf: suchlage-sim --server <url> --code <kopplungscode> [--rc-sn SN] [--aircraft-sn SN] [--replay datei.jsonl] [--speed 1] [--loop] [--no-video]".into());
    }
    Ok(a)
}
```

`src/bin/suchlage-sim.rs`:

```rust
//! Simulator: verhält sich wie Pilot 2 auf einer gekoppelten Fernsteuerung.

use std::process::{Child, Command, Stdio};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

use rumqttc::{AsyncClient, Event, MqttOptions, Packet, QoS};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use suchlage::dji::record::{RecordLine, read_lines};
use suchlage::sim::fake_pilot::{Action, Args, ConnectOutcome, classify_connect, handle_service, parse_args, retry_delays, rewrite_gateway};
use suchlage::sim::synth::{Scenario, generate};

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> std::process::ExitCode {
    tracing_subscriber::fmt().with_env_filter("info").init();
    let args = match parse_args(std::env::args().skip(1)) {
        Ok(a) => a,
        Err(e) => {
            eprintln!("{e}");
            return std::process::ExitCode::FAILURE;
        }
    };
    if let Err(e) = run(args).await {
        eprintln!("Simulator beendet: {e}");
        return std::process::ExitCode::FAILURE;
    }
    std::process::ExitCode::SUCCESS
}

async fn connect(args: &Args) -> Result<Value, String> {
    let client = reqwest::Client::new();
    let url = format!("{}/pilot/api/connect", args.server.trim_end_matches('/'));
    let mut delays = retry_delays().into_iter();
    loop {
        let res = client.post(&url).json(&json!({ "code": args.code, "rc_sn": args.rc_sn })).send().await;
        let status = res.as_ref().ok().map(|r| r.status().as_u16());
        match (classify_connect(status), res) {
            (ConnectOutcome::Ready, Ok(r)) => return r.json().await.map_err(|e| e.to_string()),
            (ConnectOutcome::GiveUp, Ok(r)) => {
                let body: Value = r.json().await.unwrap_or_default();
                return Err(format!("Kopplung abgelehnt ({}): {}", status.unwrap_or_default(), body["error"]));
            }
            (_, res) => {
                let Some(delay) = delays.next() else {
                    return Err(format!("Server nicht bereit: {}", res.map(|r| r.status().to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string())));
                };
                tracing::info!(?delay, "Server noch nicht bereit – neuer Versuch");
                tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
            }
        }
    }
}

fn lines(args: &Args) -> Result<Vec<RecordLine>, String> {
    match &args.replay {
        None => Ok(generate(&Scenario::live(&args.rc_sn, &args.aircraft_sn), suchlage::time::now_ms())),
        Some(path) => {
            let file = std::fs::File::open(path).map_err(|e| format!("{path}: {e}"))?;
            let all: Vec<RecordLine> = read_lines(std::io::BufReader::new(file)).filter_map(Result::ok).collect();
            // Gateway-SN des Mitschnitts aus dem ersten sys/product/<SN>/status bestimmen und umschreiben.
            let from = all.iter().find_map(|l| l.topic.strip_prefix("sys/product/").and_then(|r| r.strip_suffix("/status")).map(String::from));
            Ok(match from {
                Some(from) => all.iter().map(|l| rewrite_gateway(l, &from, &args.rc_sn)).collect(),
                None => all,
            })
        }
    }
}

fn start_ffmpeg(url: &str) -> Option<Child> {
    Command::new("ffmpeg")
        .args(["-loglevel", "error", "-re", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "testsrc2=size=1280x720:rate=25", "-an", "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-tune", "zerolatency", "-g", "25", "-f", "flv", url])
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|e| tracing::warn!(error = %e, "ffmpeg nicht startbar – Livebild bleibt aus"))
        .ok()
}

async fn run(args: Args) -> Result<(), String> {
    let cfg = connect(&args).await?;
    let host = cfg["mqttHost"].as_str().unwrap_or_default().trim_start_matches("tcp://").to_string();
    let (h, p) = host.rsplit_once(':').ok_or("mqttHost ohne Port")?;
    let mut options = MqttOptions::new(format!("sim-{}", args.rc_sn), h, p.parse::<u16>().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?);
    options.set_credentials(cfg["mqttUser"].as_str().unwrap_or_default(), cfg["mqttPass"].as_str().unwrap_or_default());
    options.set_keep_alive(Duration::from_secs(30));
    let (client, mut eventloop) = AsyncClient::new(options, 256);
    client.subscribe(format!("thing/product/{}/services", args.rc_sn), QoS::AtLeastOnce).await.map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    tracing::info!(rc = %args.rc_sn, "Simulator gekoppelt");
    // Ab hier nie mehr auf `publish(...).await` warten: innerhalb von select! pollt sonst niemand die
    // Ereignisschleife, und eine volle Warteschlange hält alles an (rumqttc-Falle). `try_publish`.
    let send = |topic: String, qos: QoS, payload: Vec<u8>| {
        if let Err(e) = client.try_publish(topic, qos, false, payload) {
            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "Nachricht verworfen (Warteschlange voll oder getrennt)");
        }
    };

    let services_topic = format!("thing/product/{}/services", args.rc_sn);
    let reply_topic = format!("thing/product/{}/services_reply", args.rc_sn);
    let state_topic = format!("thing/product/{}/state", args.rc_sn);
    let mut ffmpeg: Option<Child> = None;

    loop {
        let script = lines(&args)?;
        let t0 = script.first().and_then(|l| l.at_ms().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        let started = Instant::now();
        let mut next = 0;
        while next < script.len() {
            let due = Duration::from_secs_f64(((script[next].at_ms().unwrap_or(t0) - t0) as f64 / 1000.0) / args.speed);
            let wait = due.saturating_sub(started.elapsed());
            tokio::select! {
                _ = tokio::time::sleep(wait) => {
                    let line = &script[next];
                    send(line.topic.clone(), QoS::AtMostOnce, line.payload.clone().into_bytes());
                    next += 1;
                }
                ev = eventloop.poll() => match ev {
                    Ok(Event::Incoming(Packet::Publish(p))) if p.topic == services_topic => {
                        if let Some((reply, action)) = handle_service(&p.payload) {
                            send(reply_topic.clone(), QoS::AtLeastOnce, reply);
                            match action {
                                Action::StartVideo { url, video_id } => {
                                    if args.video && ffmpeg.is_none() { ffmpeg = start_ffmpeg(&url); }
                                    let status = json!({ "data": { "live_status": [{ "video_id": video_id, "video_type": "wide", "video_quality": 0, "status": 1, "error_status": 0 }] }, "gateway": args.rc_sn });
                                    send(state_topic.clone(), QoS::AtLeastOnce, status.to_string().into_bytes());
                                }
                                Action::StopVideo => {
                                    if let Some(mut c) = ffmpeg.take() { let _ = c.kill(); }
                                    let status = json!({ "data": { "live_status": [] }, "gateway": args.rc_sn });
                                    send(state_topic.clone(), QoS::AtLeastOnce, status.to_string().into_bytes());
                                }
                                Action::ChangeLens { video_type } => tracing::info!(%video_type, "Linse gewechselt (simuliert)"),
                                Action::None => {}
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Ok(_) => {}
                    Err(e) => {
                        tracing::warn!(error = %e, "MQTT getrennt – neuer Versuch");
                        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
                    }
                },
            }
        }
        if let Some(mut c) = ffmpeg.take() { let _ = c.kill(); }
        if !args.repeat {
            return Ok(());
        }
        tracing::info!("Flug zu Ende – nächster Durchlauf in 10 s");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(10)).await;
    }
}
```

Das Livebild läuft, bis der Server `live_stop_push` sendet oder der Durchlauf endet. Beim Stoppen leert der Simulator `live_status`; das prüft den Übergang `live → idle` im Kern.

Expected: Baut. Der zweite Aufruf endet mit der Aufrufhilfe und Exit 1, weil `--code` fehlt.

  - `app/frame.css`: Rahmen und gemeinsame Hilfsklassen (`.sl-page`, `.sl-stack`, `.sl-muted`, `.sl-num`, `.sl-sr`, `.sl-ok`, `.sl-table`) sowie die Schraffur als Variablen `--sl-schraffur-verzoegert`, `--sl-schraffur-verloren`. Bausteine bringen ihr eigenes CSS mit und importieren es selbst.
  - `test/handlers.ts`: Standard-Handler aus den Fixtures, ohne Node-Import (auch für den Vorschau-Modus, F2); `test/server.ts`: MSW-`server` daraus
  - `test/render.tsx`: `renderWithApp(ui, { route? })`; eine andere Rolle setzt der Test per `server.use(…/api/me…)`
  - Routen (vollständig, `router.tsx` ändert sich danach nicht mehr): `/anmelden`, `/kiosk`, `/einrichtung` (öffentlich); unter `SessionLayout`: `/` Lage, `/flights` und `/flights/:id` (admin, operator), `/system` und `/settings` (admin)

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router';
import { ThemeModeProvider } from '../theme/ThemeModeProvider';

export function renderWithApp(ui: ReactElement, { route = '/' }: { route?: string } = {}) {
  const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
      <ThemeModeProvider>
        <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[route]}>{ui}</MemoryRouter>
      </ThemeModeProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';

function Probe() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/anmelden" element={<p>Anmeldeseite</p>} />
      <Route path="/settings" element={<RequireRole roles={['admin']}><p>Geheim</p></RequireRole>} />
      <Route path="/" element={<RequireRole roles={['admin', 'operator', 'kiosk']}><p>Lage</p></RequireRole>} />
    </Routes>
  );
}

test('ohne Sitzung geht es zur Anmeldung', async () => {
  server.use(http.get('/api/me', () => HttpResponse.json({ error: 'Nicht angemeldet' }, { status: 401 })));
  renderWithApp(<Probe />, { route: '/' });
  expect(await screen.findByText('Anmeldeseite')).toBeInTheDocument();
});

test('falsche Rolle sieht einen Hinweis statt des Inhalts', async () => {
  renderWithApp(<Probe />, { route: '/settings' });
  expect(await screen.findByText('Diese Seite ist der Verwaltung vorbehalten.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.queryByText('Geheim')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
});

  expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Zur Lage' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/');
});

test('passende Rolle sieht den Inhalt', async () => {
```

- [ ] **Step 2: API-Client und Abfragen**

`frontend/src/api/client.ts`:

```ts
import type { ApiError as ApiErrorBody } from '../contract/generated/ApiError';

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public readonly status: number,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

async function parse<T>(res: Response): Promise<T> {
  if (res.status === 204) return undefined as T;
  const text = await res.text();
  const body = text ? (JSON.parse(text) as unknown) : undefined;
  if (!res.ok) {
    const message = (body as ApiErrorBody | undefined)?.error ?? `Anfrage fehlgeschlagen (${res.status})`;
    throw new ApiError(res.status, message);
  }
  return body as T;
}

export async function apiGet<T>(path: string): Promise<T> {
  return parse<T>(await fetch(path, { credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { accept: 'application/json' } }));
}

export async function apiSend<T>(method: 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE', path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<T> {
  return parse<T>(
    await fetch(path, {
      method,
      credentials: 'same-origin',
      headers: body === undefined ? {} : { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
    }),
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/api/queries.ts`:

```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { AuthOptions } from '../contract/generated/AuthOptions';
import type { FlightDetail } from '../contract/generated/FlightDetail';
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import type { KioskTokenView } from '../contract/generated/KioskTokenView';
import type { Me } from '../contract/generated/Me';
import type { RemoteControllerView } from '../contract/generated/RemoteControllerView';
import type { SettingsView } from '../contract/generated/SettingsView';
import { ApiError, apiGet } from './client';

export const queryKeys = {
  me: ['me'] as const,
  authOptions: ['auth-options'] as const,
  flights: ['flights'] as const,
  flight: (id: number) => ['flights', id] as const,
  settings: ['settings'] as const,
  remoteControllers: ['remote-controllers'] as const,
  kioskTokens: ['kiosk-tokens'] as const,
};

/** 401 ist kein Fehler, sondern „nicht angemeldet“: `null`. */
export function useMe() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.me,
    queryFn: async () => {
      try {
        return await apiGet<Me>('/api/me');
      } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 401) return null;
        throw e;
      }
    },
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });
}

export const useAuthOptions = () => useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.authOptions, queryFn: () => apiGet<AuthOptions>('/api/auth/options') });
// Kein Polling: der LiveProvider (F2) erneuert die Liste bei `flight_started` und `flight_ended`.
export const useFlights = () => useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.flights, queryFn: () => apiGet<FlightSummary[]>('/api/flights') });
export const useFlight = (id: number) => useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.flight(id), queryFn: () => apiGet<FlightDetail>(`/api/flights/${id}`) });
export const useSettings = () => useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.settings, queryFn: () => apiGet<SettingsView>('/api/settings') });
export const useRemoteControllers = () => useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.remoteControllers, queryFn: () => apiGet<RemoteControllerView[]>('/api/remote-controllers') });
export const useKioskTokens = () => useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.kioskTokens, queryFn: () => apiGet<KioskTokenView[]>('/api/kiosk-tokens') });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Ansichtsmodus, Rollen-Schranke, Rahmen**

`frontend/src/app/viewMode.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { Role } from '../contract/generated/Role';
import { useMe } from '../api/queries';

export type ViewMode = 'workstation' | 'kiosk' | 'mobile';
export const MOBILE_BELOW_PX = 768;

export function viewModeFor(role: Role, width: number): ViewMode {
  if (role === 'kiosk') return 'kiosk';
  return width < MOBILE_BELOW_PX ? 'mobile' : 'workstation';
}

export function useViewMode(): ViewMode {
  const { data: me } = useMe();
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(() => window.innerWidth);
  useEffect(() => {
    const onResize = () => setWidth(window.innerWidth);
    window.addEventListener('resize', onResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', onResize);
  }, []);
  return viewModeFor(me?.role ?? 'operator', width);
}
```

`frontend/src/app/RequireRole.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link, Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router';
import type { Role } from '../contract/generated/Role';
import { useMe } from '../api/queries';

export function RequireRole({ roles, children }: { roles: Role[]; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { data: me, isPending, isError } = useMe();
  const location = useLocation();
  if (isPending) return <p className="sl-center" aria-busy="true">Anmeldung wird geprüft …</p>;
  if (isError) return <p className="sl-center" role="alert">Server nicht erreichbar. Die Seite versucht es gleich erneut.</p>;
  if (!me) return <Navigate to={`/anmelden?next=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search)}`} replace />;
  if (!roles.includes(me.role)) {
    return (
      <div className="sl-center">
        <section className="sl-card" role="alert">
          <p>{me.role === 'kiosk' ? 'Das Großbild zeigt nur die Lage.' : 'Diese Seite ist der Verwaltung vorbehalten.'}</p>
          <Link to="/">Zur Lage</Link>
        </section>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return <>{children}</>;
}
```

`frontend/src/app/frame.css`. Alle Farben kommen aus `--lfh-*`, alle Schriftgrößen aus `--lfh-typo-*`; die Hex-Regel gilt nur für TSX. Hier stehen nur Rahmen und gemeinsame Hilfsklassen; jeder Baustein bringt sein CSS selbst mit (`map/map.css`, `video/video.css` …):

```css
:root {
  /* Die eine Handschrift (UI-Richtung): diagonale Schraffur für unsichere Werte. */
  --sl-schraffur-verzoegert: repeating-linear-gradient(135deg, var(--lfh-achtung-fuellung) 0 6px, transparent 6px 12px);
  --sl-schraffur-verloren: repeating-linear-gradient(135deg, var(--lfh-alarm-fuellung-stark) 0 6px, transparent 6px 12px);
  /* Mobil 18 px: kein Lifeline-Token. Zwei Spalten auf 360 px brauchen Platz für „Rückkehr zum Startpunkt“. */
  --sl-datenwert-mobil-groesse: 18px;
}
.sl-frame { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 60px 1fr; grid-template-rows: 52px 1fr; height: 100dvh; background: var(--lfh-grund); color: var(--lfh-text); }
.sl-frame[data-mode='kiosk'] { grid-template-columns: 1fr; cursor: none; }
.sl-frame[data-mode='mobile'] { grid-template-columns: 1fr; grid-template-rows: 48px 1fr 56px; }
.sl-head { grid-column: 1 / -1; display: flex; align-items: stretch; background: var(--lfh-rahmen-grund); border-bottom: 1px solid var(--lfh-rahmen-linie); color: var(--lfh-rahmen-text); }
.sl-head > * { display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 0 16px; border-right: 1px solid var(--lfh-rahmen-linie); }
.sl-head .sl-mark { width: 60px; padding: 0; justify-content: center; border-right: 1px solid var(--lfh-rahmen-linie); }
.sl-head .sl-mark::before { content: ''; width: 14px; height: 14px; background: var(--lfh-rahmen-marke); }
.sl-head .sl-word { font: var(--lfh-typo-seitentitel-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-seitentitel-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-display); letter-spacing: 0.04em; }
.sl-head .sl-status { flex: 1; gap: 12px; min-width: 0; }
.sl-head .sl-clock { font: 500 14px var(--lfh-schrift-zahl); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.sl-rail { background: var(--lfh-rahmen-grund); border-right: 1px solid var(--lfh-rahmen-linie); display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
.sl-rail a { height: 62px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 0 4px; text-align: center; color: var(--lfh-rahmen-gedaempft); text-decoration: none; font: var(--lfh-typo-rail-etikett-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-rail-etikett-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-text); letter-spacing: var(--lfh-typo-rail-etikett-sperrung); text-transform: uppercase; border-left: 2px solid transparent; }
.sl-rail a[aria-current='page'] { background: var(--lfh-rahmen-aktiv); color: var(--lfh-rahmen-text); border-left-color: var(--lfh-rahmen-marke); }
.sl-rail a:focus-visible, .sl-tabs a:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--lfh-bedien); outline-offset: -2px; }
.sl-main { min-width: 0; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; }
.sl-tabs { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 1px; background: var(--lfh-linie); border-top: 1px solid var(--lfh-linie); }
.sl-tabs a { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; background: var(--lfh-kopf); color: var(--lfh-gedaempft); text-decoration: none; font: 600 13px var(--lfh-schrift-text); }
.sl-tabs a[aria-current='page'] { color: var(--lfh-text); box-shadow: inset 0 2px 0 var(--lfh-rahmen-marke); }
.sl-center { min-height: 100dvh; display: grid; place-items: center; background: var(--lfh-grund); color: var(--lfh-text); padding: 16px; }
.sl-card { width: min(440px, 100%); background: var(--lfh-paneel); border: 1px solid var(--lfh-linie); padding: 24px; display: grid; gap: 16px; }
.sl-card h1 { margin: 0; font: 600 22px var(--lfh-schrift-display); }
.sl-card p { margin: 0; color: var(--lfh-text-2); line-height: 1.5; }
.sl-user { cursor: pointer; background: none; border: 0; color: inherit; font: inherit; height: 100%; }
.sl-user:focus-visible { outline: 2px solid var(--lfh-bedien); outline-offset: -2px; }
/* Gemeinsame Hilfsklassen für alle Seiten */
.sl-page { padding: 16px; display: grid; gap: 16px; align-content: start; }
.sl-stack { display: grid; gap: 16px; }
.sl-muted { color: var(--lfh-gedaempft); margin: 0; }
.sl-ok { margin: 0 0 8px; color: var(--lfh-normal-text); }
.sl-num { font-family: var(--lfh-schrift-zahl); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
.sl-sr { position: absolute; width: 1px; height: 1px; overflow: hidden; clip: rect(0 0 0 0); }
.sl-table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font: var(--lfh-typo-text-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-text-klein-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-text); }
.sl-table th { text-align: left; font: var(--lfh-typo-augenbraue-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-augenbraue-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-text); letter-spacing: var(--lfh-typo-augenbraue-sperrung); text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--lfh-schwach); padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--lfh-linie-stark); background: var(--lfh-kopf); }
.sl-table td { padding: 8px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--lfh-linie); color: var(--lfh-text-2); }
.sl-table tr:hover td { background: var(--lfh-flaeche-3); }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { * { transition: none !important; animation: none !important; } }
```

`frontend/src/shell/Clock.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function Clock() {
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);
  return <time className="sl-clock" dateTime={now.toISOString()}>{now.toLocaleTimeString('de-DE')}</time>;
}
```

`frontend/src/shell/UserMenu.tsx`. Draußen bei Sonne braucht es Hell; die Wahl merkt sich `ThemeModeProvider` (`suchlage.theme`):

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Dropdown, type MenuProps } from 'antd';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { apiSend } from '../api/client';
import { queryKeys } from '../api/queries';
import type { Me } from '../contract/generated/Me';
import { useThemeMode, type ThemeModus } from '../theme/ThemeModeProvider';

const initials = (name: string) => name.split(' ').map((p) => p[0] ?? '').join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();

export function UserMenu({ me }: { me: Me }) {
  const { modus, setModus } = useThemeMode();
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  async function logout() {
    await apiSend('POST', '/api/auth/logout').catch(() => {}); // auch ohne Server abmelden
    client.setQueryData(queryKeys.me, null);
    navigate('/anmelden', { replace: true });
  }

  const items: MenuProps['items'] = [
    { key: 'name', label: me.display_name, disabled: true },
    { type: 'divider' },
    {
      key: 'darstellung',
      type: 'group',
      label: 'Darstellung',
      children: (['system', 'light', 'dark'] as ThemeModus[]).map((m) => ({ key: m, label: { system: 'wie System', light: 'Hell', dark: 'Dunkel' }[m] })),
    },
    { type: 'divider' },
    { key: 'logout', label: 'Abmelden' },
  ];
  const onClick: MenuProps['onClick'] = ({ key }) => (key === 'logout' ? void logout() : setModus(key as ThemeModus));

  return (
    <Dropdown menu={{ items, onClick, selectable: true, selectedKeys: [modus] }} trigger={['click']} placement="bottomRight">
      <button type="button" className="sl-user" aria-label={`Benutzermenü von ${me.display_name}`}>{initials(me.display_name)}</button>
    </Dropdown>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { NavLink } from 'react-router';
import type { Role } from '../contract/generated/Role';

const ENTRIES: { to: string; label: string; roles: Role[] }[] = [
  { to: '/', label: 'Lage', roles: ['admin', 'operator'] },
  { to: '/flights', label: 'Flüge', roles: ['admin', 'operator'] },
  { to: '/system', label: 'System', roles: ['admin'] },
  { to: '/settings', label: 'Einstellungen', roles: ['admin'] },
];

export function Rail({ role }: { role: Role }) {
  return (
    <nav className="sl-rail" aria-label="Bereiche">
      {ENTRIES.filter((e) => e.roles.includes(role)).map((e) => (
        <NavLink key={e.to} to={e.to} end={e.to === '/'}>
          {e.label}
        </NavLink>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useMe } from '../api/queries';
import { useViewMode } from '../app/viewMode';
import { Header } from './Header';
import { MobileTabs } from './MobileTabs';
import { Rail } from './Rail';

export function Frame({ status, children }: { status?: ReactNode; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { data: me } = useMe();
  const mode = useViewMode();
  return (
    <div className="sl-frame" data-mode={mode}>
      <Header status={status} me={mode === 'kiosk' ? undefined : me} />
      {mode === 'workstation' && me && <Rail role={me.role} />}
      <main className="sl-main">{children}</main>
      {mode === 'mobile' && <MobileTabs />}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/LoginPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Alert, Button } from 'antd';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { apiSend } from '../api/client';
import { queryKeys, useAuthOptions } from '../api/queries';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const next = params.get('next') ?? '/';
  const { data: options, isPending, isError, refetch } = useAuthOptions();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const client = useQueryClient();

  async function devLogin() {
    await apiSend('POST', '/api/auth/dev-login');
    await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.me });
    navigate(next, { replace: true });
  }

  return (
    <div className="sl-center">
      <section className="sl-card" aria-labelledby="login-title">
        <h1 id="login-title">Suchlage</h1>
        <p>Livebild, Position und Flugspur der Drohne für die Einsatzleitung.</p>
        {params.get('fehler') === 'oidc' && <Alert type="error" title="Anmeldung abgebrochen. Bitte erneut anmelden." />}
        {isPending && <p aria-busy="true">Anmeldeverfahren werden abgerufen …</p>}
        {isError && (
          <>
            <Alert type="error" title="Server nicht erreichbar. Ohne Server ist keine Anmeldung möglich." />
            <Button onClick={() => void refetch()}>Erneut versuchen</Button>
          </>
        )}
        {options?.setup_required && (
          <p>Suchlage ist noch nicht eingerichtet. Den Einrichtungslink aus dem Server-Protokoll öffnen – er gilt 24 Stunden.</p>
        )}
        {options?.oidc && (
          <Button type="primary" size="large" href={`/api/auth/oidc/start?next=${encodeURIComponent(next)}`}>
            Mit Pocket ID anmelden
          </Button>
        )}
        {options?.dev_login && (
          <Button size="large" onClick={devLogin}>
            Als Entwicklung anmelden
          </Button>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/auth/KioskEnrollPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { ApiError, apiSend } from '../api/client';
import { queryKeys } from '../api/queries';

export default function KioskEnrollPage() {
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const client = useQueryClient();
  useEffect(() => {
    const token = window.location.hash.slice(1);
    if (!token) {
      setError('Der Zugangslink ist unvollständig. Den Link aus Einstellungen → Großbild erneut öffnen.');
      return;
    }
    apiSend('POST', '/api/auth/kiosk', { token })
      .then(async () => {
        history.replaceState(null, '', '/kiosk'); // Token nicht in der Adresszeile stehen lassen
        await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.me });
        navigate('/', { replace: true });
      })
      .catch((e: unknown) => setError(e instanceof ApiError ? e.message : 'Server nicht erreichbar.'));
  }, [client, navigate]);
  return (
    <div className="sl-center">
      <section className="sl-card">
        <h1>Großbild koppeln</h1>
        <p role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'}>{error ?? 'Gerät wird angemeldet …'}</p>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { RouterProvider } from 'react-router';
import { router } from './router';

export default function App() {
  const [client] = useState(() => new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: 1, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } } }));

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
      <RouterProvider router={r} />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
```

```ts
class FakeSource {

});
```

`frontend/src/live/reducer.ts`:

```ts
import type { ActiveFlight } from '../contract/generated/ActiveFlight';
import type { AircraftState } from '../contract/generated/AircraftState';
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import type { FreshnessThresholds } from '../contract/generated/FreshnessThresholds';
import type { GatewayState } from '../contract/generated/GatewayState';
import type { LiveEvent } from '../contract/generated/LiveEvent';
import type { StreamState } from '../contract/generated/StreamState';
import type { SystemStatus } from '../contract/generated/SystemStatus';

export interface LiveState {
  ready: boolean;
  /** Serverzeit minus Browserzeit; Frische wird immer gegen die Serveruhr gerechnet. */
  serverOffsetMs: number;
  thresholds: FreshnessThresholds;
  gateways: Record<string, GatewayState>;
  aircraft: Record<string, AircraftState>;
  flights: Record<number, ActiveFlight>;
  ended: Record<string, FlightSummary>;
  streams: Record<string, StreamState>;
  system: SystemStatus | null;
}

export const initialLiveState: LiveState = {
  ready: false,
  serverOffsetMs: 0,
  thresholds: { delayed_after_ms: 6_000, lost_after_ms: 30_000 },
  gateways: {},
  aircraft: {},
  flights: {},
  ended: {},
  streams: {},
  system: null,
};

const byKey = <T,>(items: T[], key: (t: T) => string | number) => Object.fromEntries(items.map((i) => [key(i), i]));

export function applyEvent(s: LiveState, e: LiveEvent, clientNowMs: number): LiveState {
  switch (e.type) {
    case 'snapshot': {
      const snap = e.snapshot;
      return {
        ready: true,
        serverOffsetMs: snap.server_time_ms - clientNowMs,
        thresholds: snap.freshness,
        gateways: byKey(snap.gateways, (g) => g.sn),
        aircraft: byKey(snap.aircraft, (a) => a.sn),
        flights: byKey(snap.active_flights, (f) => f.flight.id),
        ended: s.ended,
        streams: byKey(snap.streams, (st) => st.gateway_sn),
        system: snap.system,
      };
    }
    case 'gateway':
      return { ...s, gateways: { ...s.gateways, [e.gateway.sn]: e.gateway } };
    case 'aircraft':
      return { ...s, aircraft: { ...s.aircraft, [e.aircraft.sn]: e.aircraft } };
    case 'flight_started':
      return { ...s, flights: { ...s.flights, [e.flight.id]: { flight: e.flight, segments: [] } } };
    case 'flight_ended': {
      const flights = { ...s.flights };
      delete flights[e.flight.id];
      return { ...s, flights, ended: { ...s.ended, [e.flight.aircraft_sn]: e.flight } };
    }
    case 'track_point': {
      const active = s.flights[e.flight_id];
      if (!active) return s;
      const segments = active.segments.map((seg) => seg.slice());
      while (segments.length <= e.segment) segments.push([]);
      const target = segments[e.segment]!;
      const last = target.at(-1);
      if (last && last.t_ms >= e.point.t_ms) return s;
      target.push(e.point);
      return { ...s, flights: { ...s.flights, [e.flight_id]: { ...active, segments } } };
    }
    case 'stream':
      return { ...s, streams: { ...s.streams, [e.stream.gateway_sn]: e.stream } };
    case 'system':
      return { ...s, system: e.system };
  }
}
```

Das Kopieren aller Segmente je Punkt ist bei 1 Hz und wenigen tausend Punkten billig. Wird es spürbar, kopiert man nur das betroffene Segment.

`frontend/src/live/freshness.ts`:

```ts
import type { FreshnessThresholds } from '../contract/generated/FreshnessThresholds';

export type Freshness = 'fresh' | 'delayed' | 'lost';

export function freshnessOf(lastSeenMs: number, serverNowMs: number, t: FreshnessThresholds): Freshness {
  const age = serverNowMs - lastSeenMs;
  if (age > t.lost_after_ms) return 'lost';
  if (age > t.delayed_after_ms) return 'delayed';
  return 'fresh';
}

export function ageLabel(ms: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(1, Math.round(ms / 1000));
  if (s < 60) return `vor ${s} s`;
  const min = Math.round(s / 60);
  if (min < 60) return `vor ${min} min`;
  return `vor ${Math.round(min / 60)} h`;
}
```

`frontend/src/live/connection.ts`:

```ts
import type { LiveEvent } from '../contract/generated/LiveEvent';

export type ConnectionStatus = 'connecting' | 'open' | 'reconnecting';

const RETRY_MS = 3_000;

interface Options {
  url: string;
  onEvent: (e: LiveEvent) => void;
  onStatus: (s: ConnectionStatus) => void;
  create?: (url: string) => EventSource;
}

/** Hält genau eine EventSource offen. Der Browser verbindet selbst neu; gibt er auf (CLOSED), nach 3 s ein neuer Versuch. */
export function connectLive({ url, onEvent, onStatus, create = (u) => new EventSource(u, { withCredentials: true }) }: Options): () => void {
  let source: EventSource | null = null;
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
  let stopped = false;

  const open = () => {
    onStatus('connecting');
    const src = create(url);
    source = src;
    src.onopen = () => onStatus('open');
    src.onmessage = (m) => {
      try {
        onEvent(JSON.parse(m.data) as LiveEvent);
      } catch {
        console.warn('Live-Nachricht nicht lesbar – übersprungen');
      }
    };
    src.onerror = () => {
      onStatus('reconnecting');
      if (src.readyState === 2 && !stopped) {
        src.close();
        timer = setTimeout(open, RETRY_MS);
      }
    };
  };

  open();
  return () => {
    stopped = true;
    clearTimeout(timer);
    source?.close();
  };
}
```
