```ts
import type { LiveEvent } from '../contract/generated/LiveEvent';
import type { ConnectionStatus } from './connection';
import { applyEvent, initialLiveState, type LiveState } from './reducer';

export function createLiveStore(now: () => number = () => Date.now()) {
  let state: LiveState = initialLiveState;
  let status: ConnectionStatus = 'connecting';
  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
  const emit = () => listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  return {
    getState: () => state,
    getStatus: () => status,
    subscribe: (l: () => void) => {
      listeners.add(l);
      return () => listeners.delete(l);
    },
    dispatch: (e: LiveEvent) => {
      state = applyEvent(state, e, now());
      emit();
    },
    setStatus: (s: ConnectionStatus) => {
      status = s;
      emit();
    },
  };
}

export type LiveStore = ReturnType<typeof createLiveStore>;
```

`frontend/src/live/LiveProvider.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { queryKeys } from '../api/queries';
import type { LiveEvent } from '../contract/generated/LiveEvent';
import { connectLive } from './connection';
import { demoFeed } from './demoFeed';
import type { LiveState } from './reducer';
import { createLiveStore, type LiveStore } from './situationStore';

const Ctx = createContext<LiveStore | null>(null);

/** `store` für Tests (vorbefüllt, ohne Verbindung); sonst verbindet der Provider selbst. */
export function LiveProvider({ store, url = '/api/live', children }: { store?: LiveStore; url?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [own] = useState(() => store ?? createLiveStore());
  const client = useQueryClient();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (store) return;
    const onEvent = (e: LiveEvent) => {
      own.dispatch(e);
      // Die Flugliste kennt neue und beendete Flüge, ohne zu pollen.
      if (e.type === 'flight_started' || e.type === 'flight_ended') void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: queryKeys.flights });
    };
    // Vorschau-Modus (`mise run ui`): abgespielter Flug statt SSE. Im Build ist MODE 'production', der Zweig fällt weg.
    const connect = import.meta.env.MODE === 'demo' ? demoFeed : connectLive;
    return connect({ url, onEvent, onStatus: own.setStatus });
  }, [own, store, url, client]);
  return <Ctx.Provider value={own}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

function useStore(): LiveStore {
  const s = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!s) throw new Error('useLive außerhalb von <LiveProvider>');
  return s;
}

/** Selektor muss vorhandene Referenzen zurückgeben (kein neues Objekt je Aufruf). */
export function useLive<T>(selector: (s: LiveState) => T): T {
  const s = useStore();
  return useSyncExternalStore(s.subscribe, () => selector(s.getState()));
}

export function useConnectionStatus() {
  const s = useStore();
  return useSyncExternalStore(s.subscribe, s.getStatus);
}

/** Serverzeit, sekündlich fortgeschrieben – Grundlage aller Frische-Anzeigen. */
export function useServerNow(intervalMs = 1000): number {
  const offset = useLive((s) => s.serverOffsetMs);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), intervalMs);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [intervalMs]);
  return now + offset;
}
```

```ts
import type { LiveEvent } from '../contract/generated/LiveEvent';
import { demoFeed, DEMO_GAP } from './demoFeed';

test('Vorschau beginnt mit einem Snapshot auf der aktuellen Uhr und schweigt in der Lücke', () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers({ now: 1_800_000_000_000 });
  const events: LiveEvent[] = [];
  const stop = demoFeed({ url: '/api/live', onEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onStatus: () => {} });
  expect(events[0]!.type).toBe('snapshot');
  const snap = events[0] as Extract<LiveEvent, { type: 'snapshot' }>;
  expect(snap.snapshot.server_time_ms).toBe(1_800_000_000_000);
  expect(snap.snapshot.aircraft[0]!.last_seen_ms).toBe(1_800_000_000_000);

  vi.advanceTimersByTime(DEMO_GAP.from_s * 1000);
  const before = events.length;
  expect(events.some((e) => e.type === 'track_point')).toBe(true);
  vi.advanceTimersByTime((DEMO_GAP.to_s - DEMO_GAP.from_s) * 1000 - 1);
  expect(events.length).toBe(before);
  vi.advanceTimersByTime(1_001);
  const resumed = events.at(-1)!;
  expect(resumed.type === 'track_point' && resumed.segment).toBe(2);
  stop();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});
```

`frontend/src/live/demoFeed.ts`:

```ts
import { snapshotFlying } from '../contract/fixtures.generated';
import type { AircraftState } from '../contract/generated/AircraftState';
import type { LiveEvent } from '../contract/generated/LiveEvent';
import type { ConnectionStatus } from './connection';

/** Funkstille je Runde, in Sekunden ab Start – zeigt Schraffur, grauen Marker und neues Segment. */
export const DEMO_GAP = { from_s: 20, to_s: 35 };
const ROUND_S = 60;

/** Spielt einen Flug aus den Beispieldaten ab. Gleiche Signatur wie `connectLive`. */
export function demoFeed({ onEvent, onStatus }: { url: string; onEvent: (e: LiveEvent) => void; onStatus: (s: ConnectionStatus) => void }): () => void {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const base = snapshotFlying;
  const shift = t0 - base.server_time_ms;
  const plane = base.aircraft[0]!;
  const flight = base.active_flights[0]!;
  const last = flight.segments.at(-1)!.at(-1)!;
  onEvent({
    type: 'snapshot',
    snapshot: {
      ...base,
      server_time_ms: t0,
      gateways: base.gateways.map((g) => ({ ...g, last_seen_ms: t0 })),
      aircraft: [{ ...plane, last_seen_ms: t0 }],
      active_flights: [{ flight: { ...flight.flight, started_at_ms: flight.flight.started_at_ms + shift }, segments: flight.segments.map((s) => s.map((p) => ({ ...p, t_ms: p.t_ms + shift }))) }],
      streams: base.streams.map((s) => ({ ...s, updated_at_ms: t0 })),
    },
  });
  onStatus('open');
  let segment = flight.segments.length - 1;
  let silent = false;
  const id = window.setInterval(() => {
    const now = Date.now();
    const s = Math.round((now - t0) / 1000) % ROUND_S;
    if (s >= DEMO_GAP.from_s && s < DEMO_GAP.to_s) {
      silent = true;
      return;
    }
    if (silent) {
      silent = false;
      segment += 1;
    }
    const a = (now - t0) / 20_000; // ein Kreis von rund 300 m alle 2 Minuten
    const lat = last.lat + 0.0015 * Math.sin(a);
    const lon = last.lon + 0.0025 * (1 - Math.cos(a));
    const heading = ((((a + Math.PI / 2) * 180) / Math.PI) % 360 + 360) % 360;
    const aircraft: AircraftState = { ...plane, last_seen_ms: now, telemetry: { ...plane.telemetry, position: { lat, lon }, heading_deg: heading } };
    onEvent({ type: 'aircraft', aircraft });
    onEvent({ type: 'track_point', flight_id: flight.flight.id, segment, point: { t_ms: now, lat, lon, elevation_m: 62 } });
  }, 1_000);
  return () => window.clearInterval(id);
}
```

```ts
import { meAdmin, meKiosk, meOperator } from '../contract/fixtures.generated';
import { handlersFor } from '../test/handlers';

/**
 * Vorschau ohne Backend: MSW im Browser. Unbekannte Anfragen (Kartenkacheln, Glyphen) gehen ungehindert
 * raus. `?rolle=kiosk` oder `?rolle=operator` zeigt die Oberfläche in dieser Rolle, sonst Verwaltung.
 */
export async function startDemo() {
  const rolle = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('rolle');
  const me = rolle === 'kiosk' ? meKiosk : rolle === 'operator' ? meOperator : meAdmin;
  const { setupWorker } = await import('msw/browser');
  await setupWorker(...handlersFor(me)).start({ onUnhandledRequest: 'bypass', quiet: true });
}
```

```tsx
}
```

```ts
const VERSATILES = 'https://tiles.versatiles.org';

/** Dunkler bzw. heller Shortbread-Stil aus Lifeline, gespeist von VersaTiles (online). */
export function basemapStyle(theme: KartenTheme): StyleSpecification {
  const f = FARBEN[theme];
  return {
    version: 8,
    glyphs: `${VERSATILES}/assets/glyphs/{fontstack}/{range}.pbf`,
    sources: {
      basemap: {
        type: 'vector',
        tiles: [`${VERSATILES}/tiles/osm/{z}/{x}/{y}`],
        minzoom: 0,
        maxzoom: 14,
        attribution: '© OpenStreetMap-Mitwirkende',
      },
    },
    layers: [{ id: 'hintergrund', type: 'background', paint: { 'background-color': f.erde } }, ...regionLayers(f, 'basemap', '')],
  } as StyleSpecification;
}
```

`frontend/src/map/layers.ts`:

```ts
import type { AircraftState } from '../contract/generated/AircraftState';
import type { FreshnessThresholds } from '../contract/generated/FreshnessThresholds';
import type { GatewayState } from '../contract/generated/GatewayState';
import type { TrackPoint } from '../contract/generated/TrackPoint';
import { ageLabel, freshnessOf } from '../live/freshness';

type Point = { type: 'Point'; coordinates: [number, number] };
type Line = { type: 'LineString'; coordinates: [number, number][] };
export interface Collection<G, P> {
  type: 'FeatureCollection';
  features: { type: 'Feature'; geometry: G; properties: P }[];
}

const collection = <G, P>(features: { geometry: G; properties: P }[]): Collection<G, P> => ({
  type: 'FeatureCollection',
  features: features.map((f) => ({ type: 'Feature', ...f })),
});

export function trackCollection(segmentsList: TrackPoint[][][]): Collection<Line, { segment: number }> {
  return collection(
    segmentsList.flatMap((segments) =>
      segments.filter((s) => s.length > 1).map((s, i) => ({ geometry: { type: 'LineString', coordinates: s.map((p) => [p.lon, p.lat] as [number, number]) }, properties: { segment: i } })),
    ),
  );
}

export function aircraftCollection(aircraft: AircraftState[], serverNow: number, t: FreshnessThresholds) {
  return collection<Point, { sn: string; heading: number; freshness: string; age: string }>(
    aircraft.flatMap((a) => {
      const p = a.telemetry.position;
      if (!p) return [];
      const freshness = freshnessOf(a.last_seen_ms, serverNow, t);
      return [{
        geometry: { type: 'Point', coordinates: [p.lon, p.lat] },
        properties: { sn: a.sn, heading: a.telemetry.heading_deg ?? 0, freshness, age: freshness === 'fresh' ? '' : ageLabel(serverNow - a.last_seen_ms) },
      }];
    }),
  );
}

export function gatewayCollection(gateways: GatewayState[], serverNow: number, t: FreshnessThresholds) {
  return collection<Point, { sn: string; label: string; freshness: string; age: string }>(
    gateways.flatMap((g) => {
      if (!g.position) return [];
      const freshness = freshnessOf(g.last_seen_ms, serverNow, t);
      const age = freshness === 'fresh' ? '' : ageLabel(serverNow - g.last_seen_ms);
      return [{ geometry: { type: 'Point', coordinates: [g.position.lon, g.position.lat] }, properties: { sn: g.sn, label: g.label ?? g.sn, freshness, age } }];
    }),
  );
}

export function homeCollection(aircraft: AircraftState[]) {
  return collection<Point, { sn: string }>(
    aircraft.flatMap((a) => (a.telemetry.home ? [{ geometry: { type: 'Point', coordinates: [a.telemetry.home.lon, a.telemetry.home.lat] }, properties: { sn: a.sn } }] : [])),
  );
}

export function boundsOf(segments: TrackPoint[][]): [[number, number], [number, number]] | null {
  const pts = segments.flat();
  if (pts.length === 0) return null;
  const lons = pts.map((p) => p.lon);
  const lats = pts.map((p) => p.lat);
  return [[Math.min(...lons), Math.min(...lats)], [Math.max(...lons), Math.max(...lats)]];
}
```

`frontend/src/map/glyphs.ts`:

```ts
/** Kurs-Glyphe: spitzes Dreieck, nach oben = Norden, MapLibre dreht per `icon-rotate`. */
export function aircraftGlyph(color: string, outlineOnly: boolean, size = 40): ImageData {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = c.height = size;
  const g = c.getContext('2d')!;
  g.beginPath();
  g.moveTo(size / 2, 3);
  g.lineTo(size - 7, size - 5);
  g.lineTo(size / 2, size - 13);
  g.lineTo(7, size - 5);
  g.closePath();
  g.lineWidth = 3;
  g.strokeStyle = color;
  g.stroke();
  if (!outlineOnly) {
    g.fillStyle = color;
    g.fill();
  }
  return g.getImageData(0, 0, size, size);
}

export function homeGlyph(color: string, size = 24): ImageData {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = c.height = size;
  const g = c.getContext('2d')!;
  g.strokeStyle = color;
  g.lineWidth = 2;
  g.beginPath();
  g.moveTo(4, 12);
  g.lineTo(12, 4);
  g.lineTo(20, 12);
  g.moveTo(6, 10);
  g.lineTo(6, 20);
  g.lineTo(18, 20);
  g.lineTo(18, 10);
  g.stroke();
  return g.getImageData(0, 0, size, size);
}
```

```ts
import { drawSymbol } from '@einsatzzeichen/core';
import { addSymbolImage, type MapLike } from '@einsatzzeichen/maplibre';
import type { SymbolSpec } from '@einsatzzeichen/schema';

/** I.1.20 „Trupp Drohne“ einer Hilfsorganisation (geprüft mit 6.4.0; `drk` ist keine Organisation der Bibliothek). */
export const DRONE_TEAM_SPEC: SymbolSpec = {
  kind: 'formation',
  organization: 'hilfsorganisation',
  strength: 'trupp',
  bodyMarks: ['formation-chevron-top'],
};

export const UNIT_IMAGE = 'unit-drone-team';

export function registerUnitSign(map: MapLike) {
  if (map.hasImage(UNIT_IMAGE)) return;
  addSymbolImage(map, UNIT_IMAGE, drawSymbol(DRONE_TEAM_SPEC, { title: 'Trupp Drohne' }), { size: 36, pixelRatio: window.devicePixelRatio || 1 });
}
```

`bodyMarks?: readonly BodyMarkId[]` steht so in `@einsatzzeichen/schema` 6.4.0 (`dist/taxonomy.d.ts`). Mit genau dieser Spec hat `drawSymbol` in einer Node-Probe am 16.10.2026 ohne Fehler gezeichnet.

```tsx
import * as maplibregl from 'maplibre-gl';
import 'maplibre-gl/dist/maplibre-gl.css';
import './maplibreWorker';
import './map.css';
import { Button } from 'antd';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useRollen } from '../components/instrument';
import type { AircraftState } from '../contract/generated/AircraftState';
import type { FreshnessThresholds } from '../contract/generated/FreshnessThresholds';
import type { GatewayState } from '../contract/generated/GatewayState';
import type { TrackPoint } from '../contract/generated/TrackPoint';
import { useThemeMode } from '../theme/ThemeModeProvider';
import { basemapStyle } from './basemapStyle';
import { aircraftGlyph, homeGlyph } from './glyphs';
import { aircraftCollection, boundsOf, gatewayCollection, homeCollection, trackCollection } from './layers';
import { registerUnitSign, UNIT_IMAGE } from './unitSign';

interface Props {
  gateways: GatewayState[];
  aircraft: AircraftState[];
  tracks: TrackPoint[][][];
  serverNow: number;
  thresholds: FreshnessThresholds;
  /** Karte folgt dem ersten Fluggerät. */
  follow?: boolean;
  onFollowChange?: (follow: boolean) => void;
  /** Flugdetail: einmal auf die Spur zoomen, keine Live-Gerätezeichen. */
  staticFit?: boolean;
  label?: string;
}

const EMPTY = { type: 'FeatureCollection', features: [] } as const;

export function SituationMap({ gateways, aircraft, tracks, serverNow, thresholds, follow = false, onFollowChange, staticFit = false, label = 'Lagekarte' }: Props) {
  const container = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mapRef = useRef<maplibregl.Map | null>(null);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const { rollen, dunkel } = useRollen();
  const { modus } = useThemeMode();

  useEffect(() => {
    const map = new maplibregl.Map({
      container: container.current!,
      style: basemapStyle(dunkel ? 'dark' : 'light'),
      center: [10.01, 52.43],
      zoom: 13,
      maxPitch: 0,
      touchPitch: false,
      dragRotate: false,
      attributionControl: { compact: true },
    });
    mapRef.current = map;
    map.on('load', () => {
      map.addImage('aircraft-fresh', aircraftGlyph(rollen.bedien, false));
      map.addImage('aircraft-delayed', aircraftGlyph(rollen.gedaempft, false));
      map.addImage('aircraft-lost', aircraftGlyph(rollen.schwach, true));
      map.addImage('home', homeGlyph(rollen.gedaempft));
      registerUnitSign(map);
      for (const id of ['track', 'aircraft', 'gateways', 'home']) map.addSource(id, { type: 'geojson', data: EMPTY });
      map.addLayer({ id: 'track', type: 'line', source: 'track', paint: { 'line-color': rollen.bedien, 'line-width': 3, 'line-opacity': 0.9 } });
      map.addLayer({ id: 'home', type: 'symbol', source: 'home', layout: { 'icon-image': 'home', 'icon-allow-overlap': true } });
      // Das taktische Zeichen dreht und färbt sich nicht; sein Alter zeigen Deckkraft und Beschriftung.
      map.addLayer({
        id: 'gateways',
        type: 'symbol',
        source: 'gateways',
        layout: { 'icon-image': UNIT_IMAGE, 'icon-allow-overlap': true, 'text-field': ['case', ['==', ['get', 'age'], ''], ['get', 'label'], ['concat', ['get', 'label'], ' · ', ['get', 'age']]], 'text-font': ['noto_sans_regular'], 'text-size': 11, 'text-offset': [0, 2], 'text-anchor': 'top' },
        paint: { 'icon-opacity': ['match', ['get', 'freshness'], 'lost', 0.35, 'delayed', 0.7, 1], 'text-color': ['match', ['get', 'freshness'], 'fresh', rollen.text, rollen.achtungText], 'text-halo-color': rollen.grund, 'text-halo-width': 1.5 },
      });
      map.addLayer({
        id: 'aircraft',
        type: 'symbol',
        source: 'aircraft',
        layout: {
          'icon-image': ['concat', 'aircraft-', ['get', 'freshness']],
          'icon-rotate': ['get', 'heading'],
          'icon-rotation-alignment': 'map',
          'icon-allow-overlap': true,
          'text-field': ['get', 'age'],
          'text-font': ['noto_sans_regular'],
          'text-size': 12,
          'text-offset': [0, 1.8],
          'text-anchor': 'top',
        },
        paint: { 'text-color': rollen.achtungText, 'text-halo-color': rollen.grund, 'text-halo-width': 1.5 },
      });
      setLoaded(true);
    });
    return () => {
      map.remove();
      mapRef.current = null;
      setLoaded(false);
    };
    // Karte einmal je Theme aufbauen; Daten kommen über setData.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [dunkel, modus]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || !loaded) return;
    (map.getSource('track') as maplibregl.GeoJSONSource).setData(trackCollection(tracks) as GeoJSON.FeatureCollection);
    (map.getSource('aircraft') as maplibregl.GeoJSONSource).setData((staticFit ? EMPTY : aircraftCollection(aircraft, serverNow, thresholds)) as GeoJSON.FeatureCollection);
    (map.getSource('gateways') as maplibregl.GeoJSONSource).setData((staticFit ? EMPTY : gatewayCollection(gateways, serverNow, thresholds)) as GeoJSON.FeatureCollection);
    (map.getSource('home') as maplibregl.GeoJSONSource).setData(homeCollection(aircraft) as GeoJSON.FeatureCollection);
  }, [loaded, tracks, aircraft, gateways, serverNow, thresholds, staticFit]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const map = mapRef.current;
    if (!map || !loaded) return;
    if (staticFit) {
      const b = boundsOf(tracks.flat());
      if (b) map.fitBounds(b, { padding: 48, duration: 0 });
      return;
    }
    const p = aircraft[0]?.telemetry.position;
    if (follow && p) {
      const reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
      map.easeTo({ center: [p.lon, p.lat], duration: reduced ? 0 : 400 });
    }
  }, [loaded, follow, aircraft, staticFit, tracks]);

  const zoom = (d: number) => mapRef.current?.zoomTo((mapRef.current?.getZoom() ?? 13) + d, { duration: 150 });

  return (
    <div className="sl-map" role="region" aria-label={label}>
      <div ref={container} className="sl-map-canvas" />
      {!staticFit && (
        <div className="sl-map-tools">
          <Button aria-pressed={follow} type={follow ? 'primary' : 'default'} onClick={() => onFollowChange?.(!follow)}>Folgen</Button>
          <Button aria-label="Vergrößern" onClick={() => zoom(1)}>+</Button>
          <Button aria-label="Verkleinern" onClick={() => zoom(-1)}>−</Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`useThemeMode()` liefert `{ modus, effektiv, setModus, … }` (`ThemeModeWert` in Lifeline). Die Karte baut bei jedem Wechsel von `modus` oder `dunkel` neu auf.

`frontend/src/map/map.css` (in `SituationMap.tsx` importiert):

```css
.sl-map { position: relative; width: 100%; height: 100%; min-height: 240px; background: var(--lfh-grund); }
.sl-map-canvas { position: absolute; inset: 0; }
.sl-map-tools { position: absolute; top: 12px; right: 12px; display: grid; gap: 1px; background: var(--lfh-linie); }
```

Run: `pnpm -C frontend test src/map && pnpm -C frontend typecheck && pnpm -C frontend lint`
Expected: grün. Die Karte selbst prüft der Playwright-Smoke in I2. jsdom hat kein WebGL. Zu sehen ist sie ab F6 im Vorschau-Modus.

### Task F4: Livebild-Paneel (WHEP, HLS-Rückfall, Linsen)

**Strang:** frontend

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/video/{playerMachine,whep,hls,copy}.ts`, `frontend/src/video/{VideoPanel,LensPicker}.tsx`, `frontend/src/video/video.css`
- Create (Tests): `frontend/src/video/playerMachine.test.ts`, `frontend/src/video/hls.test.ts`, `frontend/src/video/VideoPanel.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StreamState`, `VideoSource`, `Lens` (Vertrag), `apiSend` (F1), `Segmentleiste`, `Paneel` (S2)
- Produces:
  - `video/playerMachine.ts`: `PlayerState`, `PlayerEvent`, `nextState(state, event)`, `ICE_TIMEOUT_MS = 8000`, `RETRY_MS = 3000`
  - `video/whep.ts`: `startWhep(url, video, { onConnected, onFailed }) -> Promise<MediaSession>`, `class WhepNotReady extends Error` (HTTP 404: MediaMTX hat den Pfad noch nicht, Pilot 2 sendet noch nicht)
  - `video/hls.ts`: `startHls(url, video, { onConnected, onFailed }) -> Promise<MediaSession>`; auch der native Weg (Safari/iOS) meldet `error` als `onFailed`

**Rückfall-Regeln:**
- Nur `ice_timeout` und `failed` (ICE gescheitert, WHEP-Fehler außer 404) zählen für den Wechsel auf HLS.
- `not_ready` (WHEP 404 direkt nach dem Streamstart) wartet mit demselben Transport und Versuch; `live_start_push` meldet Erfolg, bevor Pilot 2 per RTMP sendet. Sonst landete der Arbeitsplatz im VPN dauerhaft bei 5–10 s HLS-Verzögerung statt 1–2 s WebRTC (Spec §7: ≤ 3 s).
- Jeder neue `stream_live` beginnt wieder mit WebRTC. Ein Wechsel zurück aus laufendem HLS ist bewusst nicht gebaut: Außerhalb des VPN würde ein Versuch das laufende Bild jedes Mal unterbrechen.
  - `MediaSession { close(): void }`
  - `video/copy.ts`: `LENS_LABEL: Record<Lens, string>`, `streamMessage(stream?: StreamState, canControl: boolean): string`
  - `<VideoPanel gatewaySn stream sources canControl />`, `<LensPicker lenses value onChange disabled? />`

**Zustände:**

| Zustand | Anzeige |
|---|---|
| kein Stream | „Kein Livebild.“, operator: „Livebild starten“; Großbild: „Startet automatisch beim Abheben.“ |
| `starting` | „Pilot 2 startet das Livebild …“ |
| `failed` | DJI-Meldung aus `stream.error`, operator: „Erneut versuchen“ |
| live, verbindet | Video schwarz, „Verbindet …“ |
| live, spielt | Video, Kopfzeile „WebRTC“ oder „verzögert (HLS)“ |
| live, hängt | letztes Bild unter Schraffur, „Bild hängt – neuer Versuch in 3 s“ |

```ts
test('Livebild beginnt mit WebRTC', () => {

// Review Focus 2: außerhalb des VPN kommt kein UDP an – der Player darf nicht hängen bleiben.
test('falls back to HLS when ICE does not connect', () => {
  expect(nextState(live(), { type: 'ice_timeout' })).toEqual({ kind: 'connecting', transport: 'hls', attempt: 1 });
});

  expect(s).toEqual({ kind: 'retrying', transport: 'webrtc', attempt: 2 });
  s = nextState(s, { type: 'retry_due' });
  s = nextState(s, { type: 'failed' });

    s = nextState(s, { type: 'retry_due' });

test('neuer Stream beginnt nach HLS wieder mit WebRTC', () => {

  expect(nextState(nextState(hls, { type: 'stream_gone' }), { type: 'stream_live' })).toEqual({ kind: 'connecting', transport: 'webrtc', attempt: 1 });
```

```ts
import { startHls } from './hls';

/** Video-Attrappe, die natives HLS beherrscht wie Safari/iOS. */
function nativeVideo() {
  const el = document.createElement('video');
  el.canPlayType = () => 'maybe';
  el.play = () => Promise.resolve();
  el.load = () => {}; // jsdom kennt load() nicht
  return el;
}

test('native HLS reports errors: ein Fehler am Video-Element führt zum Neuversuch', async () => {
  const el = nativeVideo();
  const onFailed = vi.fn();
  const session = await startHls('/api/video/RC1/hls/index.m3u8', el, { onConnected: () => {}, onFailed });
  el.dispatchEvent(new Event('error'));
  expect(onFailed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  session.close();
  el.dispatchEvent(new Event('error'));
  expect(onFailed).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
});
```

`frontend/src/video/playerMachine.ts`:

```ts
export type Transport = 'webrtc' | 'hls';

export type PlayerState =
  | { kind: 'idle' }
  | { kind: 'connecting'; transport: Transport; attempt: number }
  | { kind: 'playing'; transport: Transport; attempt: number }
  | { kind: 'retrying'; transport: Transport; attempt: number };

export type PlayerEvent =
  | { type: 'stream_live' }
  | { type: 'stream_gone' }
  | { type: 'connected' }
  | { type: 'ice_timeout' }
  | { type: 'not_ready' }
  | { type: 'failed' }
  | { type: 'stalled' }
  | { type: 'retry_due' };

/** Ohne VPN kommt kein UDP an; nach dieser Frist geht es per HLS weiter (Review Focus 2). */
export const ICE_TIMEOUT_MS = 8_000;
export const RETRY_MS = 3_000;
const WEBRTC_ATTEMPTS = 2;

export function nextState(s: PlayerState, e: PlayerEvent): PlayerState {
  if (e.type === 'stream_gone') return { kind: 'idle' };
  switch (s.kind) {
    case 'idle':
      return e.type === 'stream_live' ? { kind: 'connecting', transport: 'webrtc', attempt: 1 } : s;
    case 'connecting':
      if (e.type === 'connected') return { kind: 'playing', transport: s.transport, attempt: s.attempt };
      if (e.type === 'ice_timeout' && s.transport === 'webrtc') return { kind: 'connecting', transport: 'hls', attempt: 1 };
      // Pfad noch nicht da: gleicher Transport, gleicher Versuch.
      if (e.type === 'not_ready') return { kind: 'retrying', transport: s.transport, attempt: s.attempt };
      if (e.type === 'failed') {
        if (s.transport === 'webrtc' && s.attempt >= WEBRTC_ATTEMPTS) return { kind: 'retrying', transport: 'hls', attempt: 1 };
        return { kind: 'retrying', transport: s.transport, attempt: s.attempt + 1 };
      }
      return s;
    case 'playing':
      return e.type === 'stalled' || e.type === 'failed' ? { kind: 'retrying', transport: s.transport, attempt: 1 } : s;
    case 'retrying':
      return e.type === 'retry_due' ? { kind: 'connecting', transport: s.transport, attempt: s.attempt } : s;
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/video/whep.ts`:

```ts
export interface MediaSession {
  close(): void;
}

/** MediaMTX kennt den Pfad noch nicht (WHEP 404): Pilot 2 hat noch nicht zu senden begonnen. */
export class WhepNotReady extends Error {}

interface Callbacks {
  onConnected: () => void;
  onFailed: () => void;
}

function gathered(pc: RTCPeerConnection, maxMs: number): Promise<void> {
  if (pc.iceGatheringState === 'complete') return Promise.resolve();
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const done = () => {
      pc.removeEventListener('icegatheringstatechange', check);
      resolve();
    };
    const check = () => pc.iceGatheringState === 'complete' && done();
    pc.addEventListener('icegatheringstatechange', check);
    setTimeout(done, maxMs);
  });
}

/** WHEP ohne Trickle-ICE: alle Kandidaten im Offer, die Signalisierung läuft über den Server-Proxy. */
export async function startWhep(url: string, video: HTMLVideoElement, cb: Callbacks): Promise<MediaSession> {
  const pc = new RTCPeerConnection();
  pc.addTransceiver('video', { direction: 'recvonly' });
  pc.addTransceiver('audio', { direction: 'recvonly' });
  pc.ontrack = (e) => {
    video.srcObject = e.streams[0] ?? new MediaStream([e.track]);
    void video.play().catch(() => {});
  };
  pc.onconnectionstatechange = () => {
    if (pc.connectionState === 'connected') cb.onConnected();
    if (pc.connectionState === 'failed') cb.onFailed();
  };
  await pc.setLocalDescription(await pc.createOffer());
  await gathered(pc, 2_000);
  const res = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/sdp' }, body: pc.localDescription!.sdp });
  if (!res.ok) {
    pc.close();
    throw res.status === 404 ? new WhepNotReady('WHEP 404') : new Error(`WHEP ${res.status}`);
  }
  await pc.setRemoteDescription({ type: 'answer', sdp: await res.text() });
  return { close: () => pc.close() };
}
```

`frontend/src/video/hls.ts`:

```ts
import type { MediaSession } from './whep';

export async function startHls(url: string, video: HTMLVideoElement, cb: { onConnected: () => void; onFailed: () => void }): Promise<MediaSession> {
  video.addEventListener('playing', cb.onConnected, { once: true });
  // Safari und iOS spielen HLS selbst; sonst hls.js (erst bei Bedarf geladen).
  if (video.canPlayType('application/vnd.apple.mpegurl')) {
    // Ohne diesen Listener bliebe iOS bei 404 oder abgelaufener Playlist für immer auf „Verbindet …“.
    video.addEventListener('error', cb.onFailed, { once: true });
    video.src = url;
    void video.play().catch(() => {});
    return {
      close: () => {
        video.removeEventListener('playing', cb.onConnected);
        video.removeEventListener('error', cb.onFailed);
        video.removeAttribute('src');
        video.load();
      },
    };
  }
  const { default: Hls } = await import('hls.js');
  if (!Hls.isSupported()) {
    cb.onFailed();
    return { close: () => {} };
  }
  const hls = new Hls({ lowLatencyMode: true, xhrSetup: (xhr) => { xhr.withCredentials = true; } });
  hls.on(Hls.Events.ERROR, (_e, data) => data.fatal && cb.onFailed());
  hls.loadSource(url);
  hls.attachMedia(video);
  void video.play().catch(() => {});
  return {
    close: () => {
      video.removeEventListener('playing', cb.onConnected);
      hls.destroy();
    },
  };
}
```

`frontend/src/video/copy.ts`:

```ts
import type { Lens } from '../contract/generated/Lens';
import type { StreamState } from '../contract/generated/StreamState';

export const LENS_LABEL: Record<Lens, string> = { normal: 'Normal', wide: 'Weit', zoom: 'Zoom', thermal: 'Wärme' };

export function streamMessage(stream: StreamState | undefined, canControl: boolean): string {
  switch (stream?.phase) {
    case 'starting':
      return 'Pilot 2 startet das Livebild …';
    case 'stopping':
      return 'Livebild wird beendet …';
    case 'failed':
      return stream.error ?? 'Livebild ließ sich nicht starten.';
    case 'live':
      return '';
    default:
      return canControl ? 'Kein Livebild.' : 'Startet automatisch beim Abheben.';
  }
}
```

```tsx
import { Segmentleiste } from '../components/instrument';
import type { Lens } from '../contract/generated/Lens';
import { LENS_LABEL } from './copy';

export function LensPicker({ lenses, value, onChange }: { lenses: Lens[]; value: Lens | null; onChange: (l: Lens) => void }) {
  if (lenses.length === 0 || !value) return null;
  return <Segmentleiste beschriftung="Linse wählen" optionen={lenses.map((l) => ({ wert: l, label: LENS_LABEL[l] }))} wert={value} onWechsel={onChange} />;
}
```

`frontend/src/video/VideoPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Button } from 'antd';
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useEffect, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { ApiError, apiSend } from '../api/client';
import { Paneel } from '../components/instrument';
import type { Lens } from '../contract/generated/Lens';
import type { StreamState } from '../contract/generated/StreamState';
import type { VideoSource } from '../contract/generated/VideoSource';
import { streamMessage } from './copy';
import { startHls } from './hls';
import { LensPicker } from './LensPicker';
import { ICE_TIMEOUT_MS, RETRY_MS, nextState } from './playerMachine';
import './video.css';
import { WhepNotReady, startWhep, type MediaSession } from './whep';

interface Props {
  gatewaySn: string;
  stream: StreamState | undefined;
  sources: VideoSource[];
  canControl: boolean;
}

export function VideoPanel({ gatewaySn, stream, sources, canControl }: Props) {
  const video = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null);
  const [player, dispatch] = useReducer(nextState, { kind: 'idle' });
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const isLive = stream?.phase === 'live';

  useEffect(() => dispatch({ type: isLive ? 'stream_live' : 'stream_gone' }), [isLive]);

  // Die Sitzung hängt an Transport und Versuch, nicht am ganzen Zustand: der Wechsel
  // `connecting → playing` darf die laufende Verbindung nicht schließen.
  const sessionKey = player.kind === 'connecting' || player.kind === 'playing' ? `${player.transport}:${player.attempt}` : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sessionKey || !video.current) return;
    const el = video.current;
    const transport = sessionKey.split(':')[0];
    let session: MediaSession | undefined;
    let cancelled = false;
    const cb = { onConnected: () => !cancelled && dispatch({ type: 'connected' }), onFailed: () => !cancelled && dispatch({ type: 'failed' }) };
    const base = `/api/video/${encodeURIComponent(gatewaySn)}`;
    const start = transport === 'webrtc' ? startWhep(`${base}/whep`, el, cb) : startHls(`${base}/hls/index.m3u8`, el, cb);
    start
      .then((s) => (cancelled ? s.close() : (session = s)))
      .catch((e: unknown) => !cancelled && dispatch({ type: e instanceof WhepNotReady ? 'not_ready' : 'failed' }));
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      session?.close();
    };
  }, [sessionKey, gatewaySn]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (player.kind !== 'connecting' || player.transport !== 'webrtc') return;
    const id = window.setTimeout(() => dispatch({ type: 'ice_timeout' }), ICE_TIMEOUT_MS);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(id);
  }, [player]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (player.kind !== 'retrying') return;
    const id = window.setTimeout(() => dispatch({ type: 'retry_due' }), RETRY_MS);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(id);
  }, [player]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = video.current;
    if (!el || player.kind !== 'playing') return;
    let timer: number | undefined;
    const waiting = () => { timer = window.setTimeout(() => dispatch({ type: 'stalled' }), 5_000); };
    const playing = () => window.clearTimeout(timer);
    el.addEventListener('waiting', waiting);
    el.addEventListener('playing', playing);
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      el.removeEventListener('waiting', waiting);
      el.removeEventListener('playing', playing);
    };
  }, [player]);

  const command = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ path, body }: { path: string; body: object }) => apiSend('POST', path, body),
    onMutate: () => setError(null),
    onError: (e) => setError(e instanceof ApiError ? e.message : 'Befehl nicht gesendet.'),
  });
  const start = () => command.mutate({ path: '/api/streams/start', body: { gateway_sn: gatewaySn, video_id: null } });
  const stop = () => command.mutate({ path: '/api/streams/stop', body: { gateway_sn: gatewaySn } });
  const lens = (l: Lens) => command.mutate({ path: '/api/streams/lens', body: { gateway_sn: gatewaySn, lens: l } });

  const source = sources.find((s) => s.video_id === stream?.video_id);
  const transportLabel = player.kind === 'playing' ? (player.transport === 'webrtc' ? 'WebRTC' : 'verzögert (HLS)') : '';
  const message = streamMessage(stream, canControl);
  const overlay = isLive
    ? player.kind === 'connecting' ? 'Verbindet …' : player.kind === 'retrying' ? `Bild hängt – neuer Versuch in ${RETRY_MS / 1000} s` : ''
    : message;

  return (
    <Paneel titel="Livebild" meta={[source?.camera_label, transportLabel].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') || undefined}>
      <div className="sl-video" data-player={player.kind}>
        <video ref={video} muted autoPlay playsInline aria-label="Livebild der Drohne" />
        {overlay && <div className="sl-video-overlay" role="status">{overlay}</div>}
      </div>
      {(error || command.isPending) && <p className="sl-video-note" role={error ? 'alert' : 'status'}>{error ?? 'Befehl unterwegs …'}</p>}
      {canControl && (
        <div className="sl-video-actions">
          {!isLive && stream?.phase !== 'starting' && (
            <Button type="primary" onClick={start} loading={command.isPending}>{stream?.phase === 'failed' ? 'Erneut versuchen' : 'Livebild starten'}</Button>
          )}
          {isLive && <LensPicker lenses={source?.switchable_lenses ?? []} value={stream?.lens ?? source?.lens ?? null} onChange={lens} />}
          {isLive && <Button onClick={stop}>Livebild beenden</Button>}
        </div>
      )}
    </Paneel>
  );
}
```

`meta` erwartet `ReactNode`; `undefined` lässt die Zeile leer.

`frontend/src/video/video.css` (in `VideoPanel.tsx` importiert). Die Schraffur kommt aus `--sl-schraffur-*` (F1), der Leitidee „Jeder Wert trägt sein Alter“:

```css
.sl-video { position: relative; aspect-ratio: 16 / 9; background: var(--lfh-grund); border: 1px solid var(--lfh-linie); }
.sl-video video { width: 100%; height: 100%; object-fit: contain; display: block; }
.sl-video-overlay { position: absolute; inset: 0; display: grid; place-items: center; padding: 16px; text-align: center; color: var(--lfh-text-2); font: 500 var(--lfh-typo-text-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-text); }
.sl-video[data-player='retrying'] .sl-video-overlay { background-image: var(--sl-schraffur-verzoegert); color: var(--lfh-achtung-text); }
.sl-video-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 8px; padding-top: 8px; align-items: center; }
.sl-video-note { margin: 8px 0 0; color: var(--lfh-alarm-text); }
.sl-video-note[role='status'] { color: var(--lfh-gedaempft); }
```

### Task F5: Instrumentenleiste

**Strang:** frontend

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/telemetry/{format,readouts}.ts`, `frontend/src/telemetry/TelemetryStrip.tsx`, `frontend/src/telemetry/strip.css`
- Create (Tests): `frontend/src/telemetry/readouts.test.ts`, `frontend/src/telemetry/TelemetryStrip.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AircraftState`, `GatewayState`, `FlightSummary`, `FreshnessThresholds`; `freshnessOf`, `ageLabel` (F2)
- Produces:
  - `telemetry/format.ts`: `fmt.{meters, speed, signedSpeed, heading, percent, clock, duration, distance}`
  - `telemetry/readouts.ts`: `type Tone = 'neutral' | 'normal' | 'achtung' | 'alarm'`, `interface Readout { key, label, value, unit?, tone, note? }`, `readouts(aircraft?, gateway?, flight?, serverNow): Readout[]`
  - `<TelemetryStrip aircraft? gateway? flight? serverNow thresholds size="compact" | "large" | "mobile" />`, Attribut `data-freshness` am Wurzelelement
  - Ein neuer Wert färbt die Zahl 120 ms in `bedien` (UI-Richtung: die einzige Bewegung). Dafür trägt die Zahl `key={value}`; `prefers-reduced-motion` schaltet es ab.

**Töne** (zweiter Kanal ist immer die Zahl oder das Wort):
- Akku `alarm` bei ≤ Rückflugbedarf, `achtung` bei ≤ Rückflugbedarf + 10.
- RTK `normal` bei Fix, `alarm` bei Fehlschlag.
- Funk (SDR 0–5) `alarm` bei ≤ 1, `achtung` bei 2.
- Modus `achtung` bei Rückkehr oder Landung (9, 10, 11, 12).

```ts
import { snapshotFlying } from '../contract/fixtures.generated';
import { readouts } from './readouts';

const a = snapshotFlying.aircraft[0]!;
const g = snapshotFlying.gateways[0]!;
const f = snapshotFlying.active_flights[0]!.flight;
const byKey = (r: ReturnType<typeof readouts>) => Object.fromEntries(r.map((x) => [x.key, x]));

test('Werte und Einheiten aus der Telemetrie', () => {
  const r = byKey(readouts(a, g, f, f.started_at_ms + 601_000));
  expect(r.elevation).toMatchObject({ label: 'Höhe ü. Start', value: '62', unit: 'm' });
  expect(r.speed).toMatchObject({ value: '8,4', unit: 'm/s' });
  expect(r.heading).toMatchObject({ value: '274', unit: '°' });
  expect(r.battery).toMatchObject({ value: '63', unit: '%', note: '18:20 Restflug', tone: 'neutral' });
  expect(r.gnss).toMatchObject({ value: 'RTK fix', tone: 'normal', note: '21 GPS · 18 RTK' });
  expect(r.link).toMatchObject({ value: '4/5', tone: 'neutral' });
  expect(r.flight).toMatchObject({ value: '10:01', label: 'Flugzeit' });
});

test('Akku nahe am Rückflugbedarf wird gelb, darunter rot', () => {
  const low = (percent: number) => ({ ...a, telemetry: { ...a.telemetry, battery: { ...a.telemetry.battery!, percent } } });
  expect(byKey(readouts(low(31), g, f, 0)).battery!.tone).toBe('achtung');
  expect(byKey(readouts(low(22), g, f, 0)).battery!.tone).toBe('alarm');
});

test('schwache Funkverbindung und Rückkehr sind auffällig', () => {
  const weak = { ...g, link: { sdr_quality: 1, lte_quality: 3 } };
  const rth = { ...a, telemetry: { ...a.telemetry, mode: { code: 9, label: 'Rückkehr zum Startpunkt' } } };
  const r = byKey(readouts(rth, weak, f, 0));
  expect(r.link!.tone).toBe('alarm');
  expect(r.mode).toMatchObject({ value: 'Rückkehr zum Startpunkt', tone: 'achtung' });
});

test('ohne Fluggerät stehen Striche statt Nullen', () => {
  const r = byKey(readouts(undefined, g, undefined, 0));
  expect(r.elevation!.value).toBe('–');
  expect(r.flight!.value).toBe('–');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Formatierung und Auswertung**

`frontend/src/telemetry/format.ts`:

```ts
const n0 = new Intl.NumberFormat('de-DE', { maximumFractionDigits: 0 });
const n1 = new Intl.NumberFormat('de-DE', { minimumFractionDigits: 1, maximumFractionDigits: 1 });
const DASH = '–';
const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

export const fmt = {
  meters: (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? DASH : n0.format(v)),
  speed: (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? DASH : n1.format(v)),
  signedSpeed: (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? DASH : `${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${n1.format(v)}`),
  heading: (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? DASH : String(Math.round(v) % 360).padStart(3, '0')),
  percent: (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? DASH : n0.format(v)),
  clock: (ms: number) => new Date(ms).toLocaleTimeString('de-DE'),
  duration: (s: number) => {
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.floor(s));
    const h = Math.floor(t / 3600);
    return h > 0 ? `${h}:${pad(Math.floor((t % 3600) / 60))}:${pad(t % 60)}` : `${pad(Math.floor(t / 60))}:${pad(t % 60)}`;
  },
  distance: (m: number | null | undefined): [string, string] => (m == null ? [DASH, 'm'] : m >= 1000 ? [n1.format(m / 1000), 'km'] : [n0.format(m), 'm']),
};
```

`frontend/src/telemetry/readouts.ts`:

```ts
import type { AircraftState } from '../contract/generated/AircraftState';
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import type { GatewayState } from '../contract/generated/GatewayState';
import type { RtkFix } from '../contract/generated/RtkFix';
import { fmt } from './format';

export type Tone = 'neutral' | 'normal' | 'achtung' | 'alarm';
export interface Readout {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  value: string;
  unit?: string;
  tone: Tone;
  note?: string;
}

const RTK: Record<RtkFix, [string, Tone]> = {
  fixed: ['RTK fix', 'normal'],
  fixing: ['RTK läuft', 'neutral'],
  not_started: ['ohne RTK', 'neutral'],
  failed: ['RTK gestört', 'alarm'],
};

export function readouts(a: AircraftState | undefined, g: GatewayState | undefined, flight: FlightSummary | undefined, serverNow: number): Readout[] {
  const t = a?.telemetry;
  const battery = t?.battery;
  const rth = battery?.return_home_percent ?? null;
  const pct = battery?.percent ?? null;
  const batteryTone: Tone = pct == null || rth == null ? 'neutral' : pct <= rth ? 'alarm' : pct <= rth + 10 ? 'achtung' : 'neutral';
  const [rtkWord, rtkTone] = t?.gnss?.fix ? RTK[t.gnss.fix] : ['–', 'neutral' as Tone];
  const sdr = g?.link.sdr_quality ?? null;
  const linkTone: Tone = sdr == null ? 'neutral' : sdr <= 1 ? 'alarm' : sdr === 2 ? 'achtung' : 'neutral';
  const mode = t?.mode;
  const [dist, distUnit] = fmt.distance(t?.home_distance_m);
  const sats = t?.gnss ? [t.gnss.gps_satellites != null && `${t.gnss.gps_satellites} GPS`, t.gnss.rtk_satellites != null && `${t.gnss.rtk_satellites} RTK`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ') : undefined;
  const flightValue = !flight ? '–' : flight.ended_at_ms != null ? fmt.duration((flight.ended_at_ms - flight.started_at_ms) / 1000) : fmt.duration((serverNow - flight.started_at_ms) / 1000);

  return [
    { key: 'elevation', label: 'Höhe ü. Start', value: fmt.meters(t?.elevation_takeoff_m), unit: 'm', tone: 'neutral' },
    { key: 'speed', label: 'Geschw.', value: fmt.speed(t?.horizontal_speed_mps), unit: 'm/s', tone: 'neutral' },
    { key: 'climb', label: 'Steigen', value: fmt.signedSpeed(t?.vertical_speed_mps), unit: 'm/s', tone: 'neutral' },
    { key: 'heading', label: 'Kurs', value: fmt.heading(t?.heading_deg), unit: '°', tone: 'neutral' },
    { key: 'battery', label: 'Akku', value: fmt.percent(pct), unit: '%', tone: batteryTone, note: battery?.remaining_flight_s != null ? `${fmt.duration(battery.remaining_flight_s)} Restflug` : undefined },
    { key: 'distance', label: 'Abstand Start', value: dist, unit: distUnit, tone: 'neutral' },
    { key: 'gnss', label: 'Positionierung', value: rtkWord, tone: rtkTone, note: sats || undefined },
    { key: 'link', label: 'Funk', value: sdr == null ? '–' : `${sdr}/5`, tone: linkTone, note: g?.link.lte_quality != null ? `LTE ${g.link.lte_quality}/5` : undefined },
    { key: 'mode', label: 'Modus', value: mode?.label ?? '–', tone: mode && [9, 10, 11, 12].includes(mode.code) ? 'achtung' : 'neutral' },
    { key: 'flight', label: flight?.ended_at_ms != null ? 'Letzter Flug' : 'Flugzeit', value: flightValue, tone: 'neutral' },
  ];
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Leiste**

`frontend/src/telemetry/TelemetryStrip.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { AircraftState } from '../contract/generated/AircraftState';
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import type { FreshnessThresholds } from '../contract/generated/FreshnessThresholds';
import type { GatewayState } from '../contract/generated/GatewayState';
import { ageLabel, freshnessOf } from '../live/freshness';
import { fmt } from './format';
import { readouts } from './readouts';
import './strip.css';

interface Props {
  aircraft?: AircraftState;
  gateway?: GatewayState;
  flight?: FlightSummary;
  serverNow: number;
  thresholds: FreshnessThresholds;
  size: 'compact' | 'large' | 'mobile';
  /** Mobil zeigt nur die wichtigsten Werte. */
  only?: string[];
}

export function TelemetryStrip({ aircraft, gateway, flight, serverNow, thresholds, size, only }: Props) {
  const freshness = aircraft ? freshnessOf(aircraft.last_seen_ms, serverNow, thresholds) : 'lost';
  const age = aircraft ? serverNow - aircraft.last_seen_ms : 0;
  const ageCell =
    !aircraft ? { value: '–', tone: 'neutral' } :
    freshness === 'fresh' ? { value: `${Math.max(1, Math.round(age / 1000))} s`, tone: 'neutral' } :
    freshness === 'delayed' ? { value: ageLabel(age), tone: 'achtung' } :
    { value: `Verbindung verloren ${fmt.clock(aircraft.last_seen_ms)}`, tone: 'alarm' };
  const items = readouts(aircraft, gateway, flight, serverNow).filter((r) => !only || only.includes(r.key));

  return (
    <section className={`sl-strip ${freshness === 'delayed' ? 'sl-hatch-delayed' : freshness === 'lost' && aircraft ? 'sl-hatch-lost' : ''}`} data-freshness={freshness} data-size={size} aria-label="Telemetrie">
      {items.map((r) => (
        <div key={r.key} className="sl-cell" data-tone={r.tone}>
          <span className="sl-label">{r.label}</span>
          <span className="sl-value">
            {/* key: ein neuer Wert ist ein neues Element und spielt die 120-ms-Färbung einmal ab */}
            <span key={r.value} className="sl-fresh">{r.value}</span>
            {r.unit && r.value !== '–' && <span className="sl-unit">{r.unit}</span>}
          </span>
          {r.note && <span className="sl-note">{r.note}</span>}
        </div>
      ))}
      <div className="sl-cell sl-age" data-tone={ageCell.tone} aria-live="polite">
        <span className="sl-label">Alter</span>
        <span className="sl-value">{ageCell.value}</span>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`aria-live="polite"` steht nur an der Alterszelle. Sonst würde jede Sekunde die ganze Leiste vorgelesen.

`frontend/src/telemetry/strip.css` (in `TelemetryStrip.tsx` importiert). Größen kommen aus `--lfh-typo-*`, die Schraffur aus `--sl-schraffur-*` (F1):

```css
.sl-strip { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(128px, 1fr)); gap: 1px; background-color: var(--lfh-linie); border-top: 1px solid var(--lfh-linie); }
.sl-cell { background: var(--lfh-paneel); padding: 8px 12px; min-height: 72px; display: grid; align-content: center; gap: 4px; min-width: 0; }
.sl-strip.sl-hatch-delayed .sl-cell { background-image: var(--sl-schraffur-verzoegert); }
.sl-strip.sl-hatch-lost .sl-cell { background-image: var(--sl-schraffur-verloren); }
.sl-label { font: var(--lfh-typo-augenbraue-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-augenbraue-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-text); letter-spacing: var(--lfh-typo-augenbraue-sperrung); text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--lfh-schwach); }
.sl-value { font: var(--lfh-typo-datenwert-klein-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-datenwert-klein-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-zahl); font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; letter-spacing: var(--lfh-typo-datenwert-klein-sperrung); color: var(--lfh-text); white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }
/* Die einzige Bewegung der Oberfläche: ein neuer Wert leuchtet 120 ms in der Bedienfarbe auf. */
@keyframes sl-neuer-wert { from { color: var(--lfh-bedien); } }
.sl-fresh { animation: sl-neuer-wert 120ms ease-out; }
.sl-unit { font: var(--lfh-typo-meta-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-meta-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-text); letter-spacing: 0; color: var(--lfh-gedaempft); margin-left: 4px; }
.sl-note { font: var(--lfh-typo-meta-gewicht) var(--lfh-typo-meta-groesse) var(--lfh-schrift-zahl); color: var(--lfh-gedaempft); }
.sl-cell[data-tone='normal'] .sl-value { color: var(--lfh-normal-text); }
.sl-cell[data-tone='achtung'] .sl-value { color: var(--lfh-achtung-text); }
.sl-cell[data-tone='alarm'] .sl-value { color: var(--lfh-alarm-text); }
.sl-strip[data-freshness='delayed'] .sl-cell:not(.sl-age) .sl-value { color: var(--lfh-gedaempft); }
.sl-strip[data-freshness='lost'] .sl-cell:not(.sl-age) .sl-value { color: var(--lfh-schwach); }
.sl-strip[data-size='large'] .sl-cell { min-height: 104px; }
.sl-strip[data-size='large'] .sl-value { font-size: var(--lfh-typo-datenwert-gross-groesse); letter-spacing: var(--lfh-typo-datenwert-gross-sperrung); }
.sl-strip[data-size='mobile'] { grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; }
.sl-strip[data-size='mobile'] .sl-cell { min-height: 56px; padding: 6px 10px; }
.sl-strip[data-size='mobile'] .sl-value { font-size: var(--sl-datenwert-mobil-groesse); }
```

**Strang:** frontend

**Files:**

- Modify: `frontend/src/views/LagePage.tsx` (Platzhalter aus F1 ersetzen; der Router bleibt unverändert)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `LiveProvider`, `useLive`, `useServerNow`, `useConnectionStatus` (F2); `SituationMap` (F3); `VideoPanel` (F4); `TelemetryStrip` (F5); `Frame` (F1); `useMe` (F1)
- Produces:
  - `views/selectors.ts`: `focusOf(state: LiveState, gatewaySn?: string): Focus`
    - `Focus { gateway?, aircraft?, flight?: ActiveFlight, ended?: FlightSummary, stream?: StreamState }`
    - Ohne Wahl gilt die zuletzt gesehene Fernsteuerung mit Fluggerät, sonst die zuletzt gesehene überhaupt.
  - `<SyncCell />`: SSE-Status und Broker-Status als Wort mit Ton
  - `<EmptyState title text action? />`
  - `<FlightPanel focus serverNow />`
  - `<WorkstationView />`
  - `<LagePage />`: wählt die Ansicht nach `useViewMode()`; F8 ergänzt Kiosk und Mobil. Der `LiveProvider` kommt aus `SessionLayout` (F2).

**Zustände** (je mit eigener Darstellung, Texte wörtlich):

| Lage | Darstellung |
|---|---|
| keine Fernsteuerung | Karte + EmptyState „Keine Fernsteuerung verbunden“ / „In Pilot 2 unter Cloud-Dienst die Kopplungsadresse öffnen. Neue Fernsteuerungen koppelt die Verwaltung unter Einstellungen → Fernsteuerungen.“ |
| RC ohne Fluggerät | EmptyState „Fluggerät aus“ / „Die Fernsteuerung RC Plus 1 ist verbunden. Sobald die M350 eingeschaltet ist, erscheint sie hier.“ |
| Fluggerät am Boden | Leiste und Karte normal, FlightPanel „Am Boden“ bzw. letzter Flug |
| Flug läuft | FlightPanel „Flug läuft“ mit Zeit, Strecke, max. Höhe |
| Telemetrie verzögert/verloren | Schraffur der Leiste (F5), grauer Marker (F3) |
| SSE getrennt | SyncCell „GETRENNT“ in Alarmton, Inhalte bleiben mit Schraffur stehen |
| Video gestört | VideoPanel zeigt den Fehler, Karte und Leiste laufen weiter (Spec §5) |

- [ ] **Step 2: Auswahl und Bausteine**

`frontend/src/views/selectors.ts`:

```ts
import type { ActiveFlight } from '../contract/generated/ActiveFlight';
import type { AircraftState } from '../contract/generated/AircraftState';
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import type { GatewayState } from '../contract/generated/GatewayState';
import type { StreamState } from '../contract/generated/StreamState';
import type { LiveState } from '../live/reducer';

export interface Focus {
  gateway?: GatewayState;
  aircraft?: AircraftState;
  flight?: ActiveFlight;
  ended?: FlightSummary;
  stream?: StreamState;
}

export function focusOf(s: LiveState, gatewaySn?: string): Focus {
  const gateways = Object.values(s.gateways).sort((a, b) => b.last_seen_ms - a.last_seen_ms);
  const gateway = (gatewaySn && s.gateways[gatewaySn]) || gateways.find((g) => g.aircraft_sn) || gateways[0];
  const aircraft = gateway?.aircraft_sn ? s.aircraft[gateway.aircraft_sn] : undefined;
  const flight = aircraft?.flight_id != null ? s.flights[aircraft.flight_id] : undefined;
  return { gateway, aircraft, flight, ended: aircraft ? s.ended[aircraft.sn] : undefined, stream: gateway ? s.streams[gateway.sn] : undefined };
}
```

`focusOf` baut ein neues Objekt. Deshalb darf es **nicht** direkt in `useLive` stehen, sonst rendert `useSyncExternalStore` endlos. Die Ansicht liest den ganzen Zustand mit `useLive((s) => s)`, der Zustand ist eine stabile Referenz, und rechnet den Fokus mit `useMemo`.

`frontend/src/shell/SyncCell.tsx`:

```tsx
import { StatusChip } from '../components/instrument';
import { useConnectionStatus, useLive } from '../live/LiveProvider';

export function SyncCell() {
  const status = useConnectionStatus();
  const broker = useLive((s) => s.system?.broker ?? null);
  if (status === 'reconnecting') return <StatusChip ton="alarm" wort="GETRENNT" title="Live-Verbindung zum Server unterbrochen – baut neu auf" />;
  if (status === 'connecting') return <StatusChip ton="neutral" wort="verbindet" />;
  if (broker && broker !== 'connected') return <StatusChip ton="achtung" wort={broker === 'not_configured' ? 'Broker nicht eingerichtet' : 'Broker getrennt'} />;
  return <StatusChip ton="normal" wort="SYNC" title="Live-Verbindung steht" />;
}
```

`frontend/src/views/FlightPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { Kennzahl, Kennzahlenband, Paneel } from '../components/instrument';
import { fmt } from '../telemetry/format';
import type { Focus } from './selectors';

export function FlightPanel({ focus, serverNow, showLink = true }: { focus: Focus; serverNow: number; showLink?: boolean }) {
  const running = focus.flight?.flight;
  const last = focus.ended;
  const title = running ? 'Flug läuft' : focus.aircraft?.phase === 'airborne' ? 'In der Luft' : 'Am Boden';
  const shown = running ?? last;
  const duration = shown ? ((shown.ended_at_ms ?? serverNow) - shown.started_at_ms) / 1000 : null;
  const [dist, distUnit] = fmt.distance(shown?.distance_m);
  return (
    <Paneel titel={title} meta={last && !running ? `gelandet ${fmt.clock(last.ended_at_ms!)}` : undefined} aktion={showLink ? <Link to="/flights">Alle Flüge</Link> : undefined}>
      {shown ? (
        <Kennzahlenband>
          <Kennzahl titel={running ? 'Flugzeit' : 'Dauer'} wert={fmt.duration(duration ?? 0)} />
          <Kennzahl titel="Strecke" wert={dist} einheit={distUnit} />
          <Kennzahl titel="max. Höhe" wert={fmt.meters(shown.max_elevation_m)} einheit="m" />
        </Kennzahlenband>
      ) : (
        <p className="sl-muted">Noch kein Flug. Er beginnt automatisch beim Abheben.</p>
      )}
    </Paneel>
  );
}
```

`Kennzahlenband` erwartet als Kinder `Kennzahl`-Elemente; so wird es in Lifeline benutzt.

`frontend/src/views/WorkstationView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Button } from 'antd';
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Segmentleiste } from '../components/instrument';
import { useMe } from '../api/queries';
import { useLive, useServerNow } from '../live/LiveProvider';
import { SituationMap } from '../map/SituationMap';
import { TelemetryStrip } from '../telemetry/TelemetryStrip';
import { VideoPanel } from '../video/VideoPanel';
import { EmptyState } from './EmptyState';
import { FlightPanel } from './FlightPanel';
import { focusOf } from './selectors';

export function WorkstationView() {
  const state = useLive((s) => s);
  const serverNow = useServerNow();
  const { data: me } = useMe();
  const [chosen, setChosen] = useState<string | undefined>();
  const [follow, setFollow] = useState(true);
  const [swapped, setSwapped] = useState(false);
  const focus = useMemo(() => focusOf(state, chosen), [state, chosen]);
  const gateways = Object.values(state.gateways);
  const canControl = me?.role === 'admin' || me?.role === 'operator';

  const empty = !focus.gateway
    ? <EmptyState title="Keine Fernsteuerung verbunden" text="In Pilot 2 unter Cloud-Dienst die Kopplungsadresse öffnen. Neue Fernsteuerungen koppelt die Verwaltung unter Einstellungen → Fernsteuerungen." />
    : !focus.gateway.aircraft_sn
      ? <EmptyState title="Fluggerät aus" text={`Die Fernsteuerung ${focus.gateway.label ?? focus.gateway.sn} ist verbunden. Sobald die M350 eingeschaltet ist, erscheint sie hier.`} />
      : null;

  return (
    <div className="sl-work" data-swap={swapped}>
      <div className="sl-work-map">
        <SituationMap
          gateways={gateways}
          aircraft={focus.aircraft ? [focus.aircraft] : []}
          tracks={focus.flight ? [focus.flight.segments] : []}
          serverNow={serverNow}
          thresholds={state.thresholds}
          follow={follow}
          onFollowChange={setFollow}
        />
        {empty && <div className="sl-work-empty">{empty}</div>}
      </div>
      <aside className="sl-work-side" aria-label="Livebild und Flug">
        {gateways.length > 1 && (
          <Segmentleiste beschriftung="Fernsteuerung wählen" optionen={gateways.map((g) => ({ wert: g.sn, label: g.label ?? g.sn }))} wert={focus.gateway?.sn ?? ''} onWechsel={setChosen} />
        )}
        <div className="sl-work-video">
          {focus.gateway && <VideoPanel gatewaySn={focus.gateway.sn} stream={focus.stream} sources={focus.gateway.video_sources} canControl={canControl} />}
          <Button size="small" onClick={() => setSwapped((s) => !s)} aria-pressed={swapped}>{swapped ? 'Karte groß' : 'Livebild groß'}</Button>
        </div>
        <FlightPanel focus={focus} serverNow={serverNow} />
      </aside>
      <div className="sl-work-strip">
        <TelemetryStrip aircraft={focus.aircraft} gateway={focus.gateway} flight={focus.flight?.flight ?? focus.ended} serverNow={serverNow} thresholds={state.thresholds} size="compact" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useViewMode } from '../app/viewMode';
import { useLive } from '../live/LiveProvider';
import { Frame } from '../shell/Frame';
import { SyncCell } from '../shell/SyncCell';
import './views.css';
import { WorkstationView } from './WorkstationView';

function Context() {
  const label = useLive((s) => {
    const g = Object.values(s.gateways).find((x) => x.aircraft_sn);
    return g ? `${g.label ?? g.sn} · M350` : null;
  });
  return label ? <span className="sl-context">{label}</span> : null;
}

export function LagePage() {
  const mode = useViewMode();
  return (
    <Frame status={<><SyncCell /><Context /></>}>
      {mode === 'workstation' && <WorkstationView />}
    </Frame>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implementieren**

`frontend/src/flights/flightRow.ts`:

```ts
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import { fmt } from '../telemetry/format';

export function flightRow(f: FlightSummary, nowMs: number) {
  const start = new Date(f.started_at_ms);
  const [d, unit] = fmt.distance(f.distance_m);
  const outcome = f.end_reason === 'landed' ? 'gelandet' : f.end_reason === 'connection_lost' ? 'Verbindung verloren' : 'läuft';
  return {
    date: start.toLocaleDateString('de-DE', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' }),
    start: start.toLocaleTimeString('de-DE', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    duration: fmt.duration(((f.ended_at_ms ?? nowMs) - f.started_at_ms) / 1000),
    distance: `${d} ${unit}`,
    maxElevation: f.max_elevation_m == null ? '–' : `${fmt.meters(f.max_elevation_m)} m`,
    outcome,
    outcomeTone: (f.end_reason === 'landed' ? 'normal' : f.end_reason === 'connection_lost' ? 'achtung' : 'bedien') as 'normal' | 'achtung' | 'bedien',
  };
}
```

`frontend/src/flights/FlightTable.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { StatusChip } from '../components/instrument';
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import { flightRow } from './flightRow';
import './flights.css';

export function FlightTable({ flights }: { flights: FlightSummary[] }) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return (
    <table className="sl-table">
      <thead>
        <tr><th>Datum</th><th>Start</th><th>Dauer</th><th>Strecke</th><th>max. Höhe</th><th>Ausgang</th><th><span className="sl-sr">Öffnen</span></th></tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody>
        {flights.map((f) => {
          const r = flightRow(f, now);
          return (
            <tr key={f.id}>
              <td>{r.date}</td>
              <td className="sl-num">{r.start}</td>
              <td className="sl-num">{r.duration}</td>
              <td className="sl-num">{r.distance}</td>
              <td className="sl-num">{r.maxElevation}</td>
              <td><StatusChip ton={r.outcomeTone} wort={r.outcome} /></td>
              <td><Link to={`/flights/${f.id}`}>Flug {f.id} ansehen</Link></td>
            </tr>
          );
        })}
      </tbody>
    </table>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/flights/FlightCards.tsx` (mobil):

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { StatusChip } from '../components/instrument';
import type { FlightSummary } from '../contract/generated/FlightSummary';
import { flightRow } from './flightRow';
import './flights.css';

export function FlightCards({ flights }: { flights: FlightSummary[] }) {
  const now = Date.now();
  return (
    <ul className="sl-cards">
      {flights.map((f) => {
        const r = flightRow(f, now);
        return (
          <li key={f.id} className="sl-card-row">
            <span>{r.date} · <span className="sl-num">{r.start}</span></span>
            <span className="sl-num">{r.duration}</span>
            <StatusChip ton={r.outcomeTone} wort={r.outcome} />
            <Link to={`/flights/${f.id}`}>Flug {f.id} ansehen</Link>
          </li>
        );
      })}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/flights/FlightsPage.tsx` (ersetzt den Platzhalter):

```tsx
import { Paneel, PaneelZustand, type PaneelDatenzustand } from '../components/instrument';
import { useFlights } from '../api/queries';
import { useViewMode } from '../app/viewMode';
import { Frame } from '../shell/Frame';
import { FlightCards } from './FlightCards';
import { FlightTable } from './FlightTable';

export function FlightsPage() {
  const { data, isPending, isError, refetch } = useFlights();
  const mobile = useViewMode() === 'mobile';
  const zustand: PaneelDatenzustand = isPending ? 'laden' : isError ? 'fehler' : !data?.length ? 'leer' : 'daten';
  return (
    <Frame>
      <div className="sl-page">
        <Paneel titel="Flüge" meta={data ? `${data.length} aufgezeichnet` : undefined}>
          <PaneelZustand zustand={zustand} titel="Flüge" leerText="Noch kein Flug aufgezeichnet. Jeder Flug erscheint hier automatisch nach dem Abheben." onNeuladen={() => void refetch()}>
            {data && (mobile ? <FlightCards flights={data} /> : <FlightTable flights={data} />)}
          </PaneelZustand>
        </Paneel>
      </div>
    </Frame>
  );
}
```

Ein Leerzustand ohne Aktion ist hier richtig: Flüge entstehen von selbst, eine Aktion führte ins Leere.

`frontend/src/flights/FlightDetailPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { Datenfeld, Datenraster, Kennzahl, Kennzahlenband, Paneel, PaneelZustand } from '../components/instrument';
import { ApiError } from '../api/client';
import { useFlight } from '../api/queries';
import { SituationMap } from '../map/SituationMap';
import { Frame } from '../shell/Frame';
import { flightRow } from './flightRow';
import './flights.css';

export function FlightDetailPage() {
  const id = Number(useParams().id);
  const { data, isPending, isError, error, refetch } = useFlight(id);
  if (error instanceof ApiError && error.status === 404) {
    return <Frame><div className="sl-page"><Paneel titel={`Flug ${id}`}><p role="alert">Flug {id} gibt es nicht. <Link to="/flights">Zur Flugliste</Link></p></Paneel></div></Frame>;
  }
  if (isPending || isError || !data) {
    return (
      <Frame>
        <div className="sl-page">
          <Paneel titel={`Flug ${id}`}>
            <PaneelZustand zustand={isPending ? 'laden' : 'fehler'} titel={`Flug ${id}`} leerText="" onNeuladen={() => void refetch()}>{null}</PaneelZustand>
          </Paneel>
        </div>
      </Frame>
    );
  }
  const f = data.flight;
  const r = flightRow(f, Date.now());
  return (
    <Frame>
      <div className="sl-detail">
        <div className="sl-detail-map">
          <SituationMap gateways={[]} aircraft={[]} tracks={[data.segments]} serverNow={Date.now()} thresholds={{ delayed_after_ms: 6_000, lost_after_ms: 30_000 }} staticFit label={`Spur von Flug ${f.id}`} />
        </div>
        <div className="sl-detail-side">
          <Paneel titel={`Flug ${f.id}`} meta={`${r.date} · ${r.start}`} aktion={<Link to="/flights">Alle Flüge</Link>}>
            <Kennzahlenband>
              <Kennzahl titel="Dauer" wert={r.duration} />
              <Kennzahl titel="Strecke" wert={r.distance} />
              <Kennzahl titel="max. Höhe" wert={r.maxElevation} />
            </Kennzahlenband>
            <Datenraster spalten={2} beschriftung="Flugdaten">
              <Datenfeld label="Ausgang">{r.outcome}</Datenfeld>
              <Datenfeld label="Start" mono>{r.start}</Datenfeld>
              <Datenfeld label="Fernsteuerung" mono>{f.gateway_sn}</Datenfeld>
              <Datenfeld label="Fluggerät" mono>{f.aircraft_sn}</Datenfeld>
            </Datenraster>
          </Paneel>
          <Paneel titel="Video">
            {data.video ? (
              <>
                <video className="sl-detail-video" controls preload="metadata" src={data.video.play_url} aria-label="Flugvideo" />
                <a href={data.video.download_url} download>Video herunterladen</a>
                <p className="sl-muted">Das Video beginnt mit dem Livebild, meist kurz nach dem Abheben.</p>
              </>
            ) : (
              <p className="sl-muted">{f.ended_at_ms == null ? 'Flug läuft noch – das Video gibt es nach der Landung.' : 'Kein Video aufgezeichnet. Das Livebild lief während dieses Flugs nicht.'}</p>
            )}
          </Paneel>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Frame>
  );
}
```

Beide Seiten ersetzen die Platzhalter aus F1 und bringen ihren `Frame` selbst mit; `router.tsx` bleibt unverändert.

`frontend/src/views/KioskView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLive, useServerNow } from '../live/LiveProvider';
import { SituationMap } from '../map/SituationMap';
import { TelemetryStrip } from '../telemetry/TelemetryStrip';
import { useDichte } from '../theme/ThemeModeProvider';
import { VideoPanel } from '../video/VideoPanel';
import { EmptyState } from './EmptyState';
import { focusOf } from './selectors';

const KIOSK_VALUES = ['elevation', 'speed', 'heading', 'battery', 'distance', 'mode', 'flight'];

function useWakeLock() {
  useEffect(() => {
    let lock: { release(): Promise<void> } | undefined;
    const request = () =>
      (navigator as Navigator & { wakeLock?: { request(t: 'screen'): Promise<{ release(): Promise<void> }> } }).wakeLock
        ?.request('screen')
        .then((l) => (lock = l))
        .catch(() => {});
    void request();
    const onVisible = () => document.visibilityState === 'visible' && void request();
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible);
      void lock?.release();
    };
  }, []);
}

export function KioskView() {
  useWakeLock();
  // Großbild wird aus Entfernung gelesen: größere Abstände und Ziele (UI-Richtung).
  const { setDichte } = useDichte();
  useEffect(() => setDichte('komfortabel'), [setDichte]);
  const state = useLive((s) => s);
  const serverNow = useServerNow();
  const focus = useMemo(() => focusOf(state), [state]);
  return (
    <div className="sl-kiosk">
      <div className="sl-kiosk-map">
        <SituationMap
          gateways={Object.values(state.gateways)}
          aircraft={focus.aircraft ? [focus.aircraft] : []}
          tracks={focus.flight ? [focus.flight.segments] : []}
          serverNow={serverNow}
          thresholds={state.thresholds}
          follow
          showTools={false}
        />
        {!focus.gateway?.aircraft_sn && (
          <div className="sl-work-empty">
            <EmptyState title={focus.gateway ? 'Fluggerät aus' : 'Keine Drohne im Einsatz'} text="Das Bild erscheint, sobald die Drohne verbunden ist." />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="sl-kiosk-video">
        {focus.gateway && <VideoPanel gatewaySn={focus.gateway.sn} stream={focus.stream} sources={focus.gateway.video_sources} canControl={false} />}
      </div>
      <div className="sl-kiosk-strip">
        <TelemetryStrip aircraft={focus.aircraft} gateway={focus.gateway} flight={focus.flight?.flight ?? focus.ended} serverNow={serverNow} thresholds={state.thresholds} size="large" only={KIOSK_VALUES} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/views/MobileView.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useLive, useServerNow } from '../live/LiveProvider';
import { SituationMap } from '../map/SituationMap';
import { TelemetryStrip } from '../telemetry/TelemetryStrip';
import { VideoPanel } from '../video/VideoPanel';
import { EmptyState } from './EmptyState';
import { focusOf } from './selectors';

const MOBILE_VALUES = ['elevation', 'speed', 'battery', 'distance', 'gnss', 'mode', 'flight'];

export function MobileView() {
  const state = useLive((s) => s);
  const serverNow = useServerNow();
  const focus = useMemo(() => focusOf(state), [state]);
  return (
    <div className="sl-mobile">
      {focus.gateway ? (
        <VideoPanel gatewaySn={focus.gateway.sn} stream={focus.stream} sources={focus.gateway.video_sources} canControl={false} />
      ) : (
        <EmptyState title="Keine Drohne verbunden" text="Sobald die Fernsteuerung verbunden ist, erscheinen hier Livebild und Position." />
      )}
      <TelemetryStrip aircraft={focus.aircraft} gateway={focus.gateway} flight={focus.flight?.flight ?? focus.ended} serverNow={serverNow} thresholds={state.thresholds} size="mobile" only={MOBILE_VALUES} />
      <div className="sl-mobile-map">
        <SituationMap gateways={Object.values(state.gateways)} aircraft={focus.aircraft ? [focus.aircraft] : []} tracks={focus.flight ? [focus.flight.segments] : []} serverNow={serverNow} thresholds={state.thresholds} follow showTools={false} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task F9: Einrichtung, Einstellungen, Fernsteuerungen, Großbild-Tokens, System

**Strang:** frontend

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/admin/{SettingsForms,RemoteControllersPanel,KioskTokensPanel,OnceVisibleLink}.tsx`, `frontend/src/admin/zustand.ts`, `frontend/src/admin/admin.css`
- Create (Tests): `frontend/src/admin/SettingsForms.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/admin/RemoteControllersPanel.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/admin/SystemPage.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/admin/SetupPage.test.tsx`
- Modify: `frontend/src/admin/{SetupPage,SettingsPage,SystemPage}.tsx` (Platzhalter aus F1 ersetzen; der Router bleibt unverändert)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useSettings`, `useRemoteControllers`, `useKioskTokens`, `useAuthOptions`, `queryKeys`, `apiSend` (F1); `useLive` (F2, Provider aus `SessionLayout`); `PaneelZustand` (S2); Vertragstypen `*Input`, `SettingsView`, `AuthOptions.redirect_url`, `RemoteControllerCreated`, `KioskTokenCreated`, `SetupRequest`, `SetupResult`
- Produces:
  - `<SetupPage />` (öffentlich, Token aus `location.hash`)
  - `<SettingsPage />`: Abschnitte Anmeldung, DJI, Broker, Video, Allgemein, Fernsteuerungen, Großbild
  - `<SystemPage />`
  - `<OnceVisibleLink url hint />`: Link mit QR-Code und Kopierknopf, Hinweis „wird nur jetzt angezeigt“
  - `secretOrNull(value: string | undefined): string | null`

**Geheimnisse:** Ein gespeichertes Feld zeigt als Platzhalter „gespeichert – leer lassen zum Behalten“. Bleibt es leer, geht `null` raus.

**Zustände:** Jede Tabelle und jedes Formular, das Daten abruft, steht in `PaneelZustand` (laden, Fehler mit „Erneut abrufen“, leer). Die Callback-URL kommt immer vom Server (`redirect_url`), nie aus `window.location`; hinter Proxy oder Vite wäre die falsch.

```tsx
  server.use(http.post('/api/setup', async ({ request }) => { body = await request.json(); return HttpResponse.json({ error: 'Anmeldedienst nicht erreichbar oder Issuer falsch' }, { status: 503 }); }));
  renderWithApp(<SetupPage />, { route: '/einrichtung' });
  expect(await screen.findByText(authOptionsOidc.redirect_url)).toBeInTheDocument();
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Issuer'), 'https://id.example.org');
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Client-ID'), 'suchlage');
  await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Client-Secret'), 'geheim');
  await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Einrichten und anmelden' }));
  expect(await screen.findByText('Anmeldedienst nicht erreichbar oder Issuer falsch')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(body).toEqual({ token: 'einrichtungstoken', issuer: 'https://id.example.org', client_id: 'suchlage', client_secret: 'geheim' });
});
```
